  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。

  • 3个月时,凝胶治疗组中18/99名女性(18%)和观察组中23/109名女性(21%)未检测到hrHPV DNA。凝胶 治疗 组中患者报告的不良事件比观察组更常见(32/99 [32%] vs. [10.1111/aogs.70332]。
  • 研究发现 HPV-16 衣壳的自发曲率与其弯曲刚度之间存在明显的脱钩,弯曲模量比之前的估计大一个数量级,并且受到静电环境的强烈影响。 [10.1063/5.0341044]。
  • 多层次干预策略,包括患者教育、激励措施、提供者培训和患者提醒,提高了农村地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 率。从 2023 年到 2024 年初,HPV 疫苗接种 总发病率上升了 26.7%,起始率上升了 51.8%。站点报告了该计划的高可行性(平均 4.4)、可接受性(平均 4.4)和适当性(平均 4.2)。

  • The median serum IL-23 level was 11.8 pg/mL (interquartile range, IQR: 7.8-21.3) in patients with cutaneous verrucae and 8.8 pg/mL (IQR: 7.2-12.0) in controls, with a statistically significant difference (p = 0.018).
  • At 3 months, hrHPV DNA was not detected in 18/99 women (18%) in the gel treatment group and 23/109 women (21%) in the observation group.
  • The study found a clear decoupling between the spontaneous curvature of the HPV-16 capsid and its bending rigidity, with the bending modulus being an order of magnitude larger than previous estimates and strongly influenced by the electrostatic environment.
  • Multilevel intervention strategies, including patient education, incentives, provider training, and patient reminders, increased HPV vaccination rates in rural settings.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Cytokine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.cyto.2026.157196  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 206, 157196
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

皮肤疣中的 IL-23/IL-17 轴:来自 HPV 感染血清细胞因子分析的证据。

The IL-23/IL-17Axis in cutaneous verrucae: evidence from serum cytokine analysis in HPV infection.
作者： Harun Yildirim, Savas Ozturk, Hakan Ayyildiz, Nese Gocer Gurok, Ebru Aydin Yildirim, Zeynep Simal Cokguler, Hanifi Yıldırım, Savaş Öztürk, Hakan Ayyıldız, Neşe Göçer Gürok
摘要中文翻译背景:皮肤疣(疣)是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起的良性表皮增生。宿主 免疫应答 特别是 T 辅助细胞 17 (Th17) 相关细胞因子,例如白细胞介素 17A (IL-17A) 和白细胞介素 23 (IL-23) 在病毒控制和炎症反应的调节中发挥着关键作用。然而,疣患者中这些细胞因子的全身水平尚未得到充分表征。目的:调查皮肤疣患者血清IL-17A和IL-23水平,与健康对照者进行比较,并评估其与年龄、性别、病变数量、病程和病变部位的关系。方法:在这项病例对照研究中,纳入了 49 名掌跖或肛门生殖器疣患者和 41 名年龄和性别匹配的健康对照者。使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)测量血清IL-17A和IL-23水平。使用曼-惠特尼 U 检验分析连续变量,使用卡方检验分析分类变量。使用 Spearman 相关分析评估变量之间的相关性。结果:皮肤疣患者血清 IL-23 中位水平为 11.8 pg/mL(四分位距,IQR:7.8-21.3),对照组为 8.8 pg/mL(IQR:7.2-12.0),差异有统计学意义(p = 0.018)。患者中位血清 IL-17A 水平为 16.6 pg/mL(IQR:8.7-37.9),对照组为 16.4 pg/mL(IQR:10.9-24.5),组间无显着差异(p = 0.755)。在患者中观察到 IL-17A 和 IL-23 水平之间存在很强的正相关性(rs = 0.880,p < 0.001)。细胞因子水平与年龄、性别、病变数量、疾病持续时间或部位之间没有发现显着关联。结论:IL-23水平升高及其与IL-17A的强正相关表明IL-23/IL-17轴可能在与HPV相关的皮肤疣相关的免疫激活中发挥作用。IL-23 可能代表与皮肤疣相关的免疫激活的潜在指标;然而,其独立诊断实用性似乎有限。未来需要进行更大的 队列研究 和组织水平分析的研究,以进一步阐明 Th17 相关细胞因子在疣状 队列研究 中的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
皮肤疣(疣)是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起的良性表皮增生。宿主 免疫应答 特别是 T 辅助细胞 17 (Th17) 相关细胞因子,例如白细胞介素 17A (IL-17A) 和白细胞介素 23 (IL-23) 在病毒控制和炎症反应的调节中发挥着关键作用。
设计与对象
在这项病例对照研究中,纳入了 49 名掌跖或肛门生殖器疣患者和 41 名年龄和性别匹配的健康对照者。
核心方法
使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)测量血清IL-17A和IL-23水平。使用曼-惠特尼 U 检验分析连续变量,使用卡方检验分析分类变量。使用 Spearman 相关分析评估变量之间的相关性。
主要结果
皮肤疣患者的中位血清 IL-23 水平为 11.8 pg/mL(四分位距,IQR:7.8-21.3),对照组为 8.8 pg/mL(IQR:7.2-12.0),具有统计学显着性差异(p = 0.018)。患者中位血清 IL-17A 水平为 16.6 pg/mL(IQR:8.7-37.9),对照组为 16.4 pg/mL(IQR:10.9-24.5),组间无显着差异(p = 0.755)。在患者中观察到 IL-17A 和 IL-23 水平之间存在很强的正相关性(rs = 0.880,p < 0.001)。细胞因子水平与年龄、性别、病变数量、疾病持续时间或部位之间没有发现显着关联。
解释与创新
IL-23 水平升高及其与 IL-17A 的强正相关表明 IL-23/IL-17Axis 可能在与 HPV 相关的皮肤疣相关的免疫激活中发挥作用。
科研与公卫意义
IL-23 可能代表与皮肤疣相关的免疫激活的潜在指标;然而,其独立诊断实用性似乎有限。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其样本量相对较小且缺乏组织水平分析而受到限制。

The IL-23/IL-17Axis in cutaneous verrucae: evidence from serum cytokine analysis in HPV infection.

Authors: Harun Yildirim, Savas Ozturk, Hakan Ayyildiz, Nese Gocer Gurok, Ebru Aydin Yildirim, Zeynep Simal Cokguler, Hanifi Yıldırım, Savaş Öztürk, Hakan Ayyıldız, Neşe Göçer Gürok
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cutaneous verrucae (warts) are benign epidermal proliferations caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. The host immune response particularly T helper 17 (Th17) related cytokines such as interleukin-17A (IL-17A) and interleukin-23 (IL-23) plays a critical role in the regulation of viral control and inflammatory responses. However, the systemic levels of these cytokines in patients with verrucae have not been sufficiently characterized. OBJECTIVE: To investigate serum IL-17A and IL-23 levels in patients with cutaneous verrucae, to compare them with those of healthy controls, and to evaluate their associations with age, sex, number of lesions, disease duration, and lesion localization. METHODS: In this case-control study, 49 patients with palmoplantar or anogenital verrucae and 41 age- and sex-matched healthy controls were included. Serum IL-17A and IL-23 levels were measured using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Continuous variables were analyzed using the Mann-Whitney U test, and categorical variables using the chi-square test. Correlations between variables were assessed using Spearman correlation analysis. RESULTS: The median serum IL-23 level was 11.8 pg/mL (interquartile range, IQR: 7.8-21.3) in patients with cutaneous verrucae and 8.8 pg/mL (IQR: 7.2-12.0) in controls, with a statistically significant difference (p = 0.018). The median serum IL-17A level was 16.6 pg/mL (IQR: 8.7-37.9) in patients and 16.4 pg/mL (IQR: 10.9-24.5) in controls, with no significant difference between the groups (p = 0.755). A strong positive correlation was observed between IL-17A and IL-23 levels in patients (rs = 0.880, p < 0.001). No significant associations were found between cytokine levels and age, sex, number of lesions, disease duration, or localization. CONCLUSION: Elevated IL-23 levels and its strong positive correlation with IL-17A suggest that the IL-23/IL-17Axis may play a role in immune activation associated with HPV-related cutaneous verrucae. IL-23 may represent a potential indicator of immune activation associated with cutaneous verrucae; however, its standalone diagnostic utility appears limited. Future studies with larger cohorts and tissue-level analyses are needed to further elucidate the role of Th17-related cytokines in the pathogenesis of verrucae.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cutaneous verrucae (warts) are benign epidermal proliferations caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection. The host immune response particularly T helper 17 (Th17) related cytokines such as interleukin-17A (IL-17A) and interleukin-23 (IL-23) plays a critical role in the regulation of viral control and inflammatory responses.
Design and population
In this case-control study, 49 patients with palmoplantar or anogenital verrucae and 41 age- and sex-matched healthy controls were included.
Core methods
Serum IL-17A and IL-23 levels were measured using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Continuous variables were analyzed using the Mann-Whitney U test, and categorical variables using the chi-square test. Correlations between variables were assessed using Spearman correlation analysis.
Main results
The median serum IL-23 level was 11.8 pg/mL (interquartile range, IQR: 7.8-21.3) in patients with cutaneous verrucae and 8.8 pg/mL (IQR: 7.2-12.0) in controls, with a statistically significant difference (p = 0.018). The median serum IL-17A level was 16.6 pg/mL (IQR: 8.7-37.9) in patients and 16.4 pg/mL (IQR: 10.9-24.5) in controls, with no significant difference between the groups (p = 0.755). A strong positive correlation was observed between IL-17A and IL-23 levels in patients (rs = 0.880, p < 0.001). No significant associations were found between cytokine levels and age, sex, number of lesions, disease duration, or localization.
Interpretation and novelty
Elevated IL-23 levels and its strong positive correlation with IL-17A suggest that the IL-23/IL-17Axis may play a role in immune activation associated with HPV-related cutaneous verrucae.
Scientific and public-health significance
IL-23 may represent a potential indicator of immune activation associated with cutaneous verrucae; however, its standalone diagnostic utility appears limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its relatively small sample size and the lack of tissue-level analyses.

鉴定 microRNA 作为 高危型HPV 阳性女性宫颈刮片样本中宫颈上皮内瘤变分类的生物标志物。

Identification of microRNAs as biomarkers for triage of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in cervical scrape samples from high-risk HPV-positive women.
作者： Lidy Vannessa Mejía Guarnizo, Clara Esperanza Trujillo Gama, Liliana López kleine, Carlos Alberto Orozco Castaño, Sebastián Prada Padilla, Josefa Antonia Rodríguez García, María Mercedes Bravo Hernandez
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 宫颈癌 (CC) 是全球女性癌症死亡的第四大原因。高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 检测高度敏感,但缺乏识别有进展风险的女性的特异性。迫切需要可靠的生物标志物来识别最有可能患 宫颈癌 的 HR-HPV 阳性女性。异常的 microRNA (miRNA) 表达有助于 HPV 驱动的致癌作用,是一种有前途的诊断工具。方法 HR-HPV 阳性女性的宫颈刮片样本在组织学上分为低级别(≤CIN1)或高级别(CIN2/3)。提取总RNA并进行小RNA测序以鉴定差异表达的miRNA。生物信息学分析包括聚类、ROC 曲线、逻辑回归和功能富集。选定的候选者在独立样本中通过 RT-qPCR 进行验证。结果 19 个失调的 miRNA 区分了低度和高度病变,其中 6 个与病毒和癌症途径相关。三个(hsa-miR-501-3p、hsa-miR-1271-5p、hsa-miR-9-5p)通过 RT-qPCR 进行验证,并显示出很强的辨别能力。hsa-miR-501-3p 达到了很高的诊断准确性(AUC = 0.88),与其他方法结合时,诊断准确性提高到 0.94。经过验证的特征在检测高级病变方面实现了高灵敏度和特异性。结论 这项在哥伦比亚使用宫颈刮片进行的开创性研究表明,miRNA 是高级宫颈病变的潜在生物标志物,并且在筛查中常规收集的临床相关的微创样本支持其使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 (CC) 是全球女性癌症死亡的第四大原因。高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 检测高度敏感,但缺乏识别有进展风险的女性的特异性。
设计与对象
HR-HPV 阳性女性的宫颈刮片样本在组织学上分为低级别 (≤CIN1) 或高级别 (CIN2/3)。提取总RNA并进行小RNA测序以鉴定差异表达的miRNA。
核心方法
生物信息学分析包括聚类、ROC 曲线、逻辑回归和功能富集。
主要结果
19 个失调的 miRNA 区分了低度和高度病变,其中 6 个与病毒和癌症途径相关。三个(hsa-miR-501-3p、hsa-miR-1271-5p、hsa-miR-9-5p)通过 RT-qPCR 进行验证,并显示出很强的辨别能力。
解释与创新
这项在哥伦比亚使用宫颈刮片进行的开创性研究表明,miRNA 是高级宫颈病变的潜在生物标志物,并且在筛查中常规收集的临床相关的微创样本支持其使用。
科研与公卫意义
经过验证的特征在检测高级病变方面实现了高灵敏度和特异性。这可以为开发更准确的 宫颈癌 筛查诊断工具提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。该研究显示出较高的诊断准确性和较强的辨别能力,表明证据强度适中。

Identification of microRNAs as biomarkers for triage of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia in cervical scrape samples from high-risk HPV-positive women.

Authors: Lidy Vannessa Mejía Guarnizo, Clara Esperanza Trujillo Gama, Liliana López kleine, Carlos Alberto Orozco Castaño, Sebastián Prada Padilla, Josefa Antonia Rodríguez García, María Mercedes Bravo Hernandez
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Cervical cancer (CC) is the fourth leading cause of cancer death in women worldwide. High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) testing is highly sensitive but lacks specificity for identifying women at risk of progression. Reliable biomarkers are urgently needed to identify HR-HPV-positive women most likely to develop cervical cancer. Aberrant microRNA (miRNA) expression contributes to HPV-driven carcinogenesis and represents a promising diagnostic tool. Methods Cervical scrape samples from HR-HPV-positive women were histologically classified as low-grade (≤CIN1) or high-grade (CIN2/3). Total RNA was extracted and small RNA sequencing performed to identify differentially expressed miRNAs. Bioinformatic analyses included clustering, ROC curves, logistic regression, and functional enrichment. Selected candidates were validated byRT-qPCR in independent samples. Results Nineteen dysregulated miRNAs distinguished low- from high-grade lesions, six linked to viral and cancer pathways. Three (hsa-miR-501-3p, hsa-miR-1271-5p, hsa-miR-9-5p) were validated by RT-qPCR and showed strong discriminatory capacity. hsa-miR-501-3p reached high diagnostic accuracy (AUC = 0.88), which improved to 0.94 when combined with the others. The validated signature achieved high sensitivity and specificity for detecting high-grade lesions. Conclusion This pioneering study in Colombia using cervical scrapes demonstrates that miRNAs are potential biomarkers for high-grade cervical lesions and that this clinically relevant, minimally invasive sample routinely collected in screening supports their use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer (CC) is the fourth leading cause of cancer death in women worldwide. High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) testing is highly sensitive but lacks specificity for identifying women at risk of progression.
Design and population
Cervical scrape samples from HR-HPV-positive women were histologically classified as low-grade (≤CIN1) or high-grade (CIN2/3). Total RNA was extracted and small RNA sequencing performed to identify differentially expressed miRNAs.
Core methods
Bioinformatic analyses included clustering, ROC curves, logistic regression, and functional enrichment.
Main results
Nineteen dysregulated miRNAs distinguished low- from high-grade lesions, six linked to viral and cancer pathways. Three (hsa-miR-501-3p, hsa-miR-1271-5p, hsa-miR-9-5p) were validated by RT-qPCR and showed strong discriminatory capacity.
Interpretation and novelty
This pioneering study in Colombia using cervical scrapes demonstrates that miRNAs are potential biomarkers for high-grade cervical lesions and that this clinically relevant, minimally invasive sample routinely collected in screening supports their use.
Scientific and public-health significance
The validated signature achieved high sensitivity and specificity for detecting high-grade lesions. This could inform the development of more accurate diagnostic tools for cervical cancer screening.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The study demonstrates high diagnostic accuracy and strong discriminatory capacity, suggesting moderate evidence strength.

Journal: Acta Médica Portuguesa  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.20344/amp.24709  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 39, 8, 438-447
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

过渡到初级 人乳头瘤病毒 筛查后的侵入性 宫颈癌 检测:葡萄牙基于人群的连锁研究。

Invasive Cervical Cancer Detection After Transition to Primary Human Papillomavirus Screening: Population-Based Linkage Study in Portugal.
作者： Rita Sousa, José Alberto Fonseca-Moutinho, Fábio Sousa Gomes, Fernanda Loureiro, Helena Nascimento, Helena Solheiro, Isabel Saavedra, Madalena Ponte, Mónica Barros, Sofia Pereira
摘要中文翻译简介:侵入性 宫颈癌 (ICC) 是评估 宫颈癌 筛查 (CCS) 计划的关键结果。监测 ICC 趋势并区分有组织的筛查计划内外检测到的癌症对于评估计划影响至关重要,特别是在引入基于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的初级筛查等过渡期间。方法:我们在葡萄牙中部地区(2014 年至 2023 年)进行了一项基于人群的回顾性研究。在组织的 CCS 数据库和医院发病记录之间进行确定性链接。根据之前的筛查历史,侵袭性 宫颈癌 病例被分为筛查检测到或非筛查检测。发病率 率使用人口分母计算。拟合具有对数链接和对数转换群体偏移的泊松回归模型来估计 发病率 率比 (IRR),比较基于细胞学(2014 - 2019)和基于 HPV(2020 - 2023)的时期,并根据时间趋势进行调整。结果:共纳入 654 例 ICC 病例。随着时间的推移,筛查参与率基本保持稳定。与细胞学检查时期相比,HPV 检查检查期间的 ICC 筛查检出率显着较高(IRR = 1.94;95% CI 1.11 - 3.40)。相比之下,有组织的筛查计划之外的 ICC 发病率 在不同时期之间没有显着差异(IRR = 0.94;95% CI 0.65 - 1.35)。结论:向基于 HPV 的初级筛查的过渡与 ICC 检测向有组织筛查计划的转变相关,但没有证据表明在有组织筛查计划之外诊断的女性中 发病率 相应增加。然而,鉴于实施的早期阶段和影响 诊断 访问的潜在外部因素,应谨慎解释这些发现。总体而言,结果支持筛查途径中检测的转变,而不是潜在疾病发生的变化。加强筛查、医院和癌症登记数据之间的整合对于 宫颈癌 筛查政策的稳健评估仍然至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
侵入性 宫颈癌 (ICC) 是评估 宫颈癌 筛查计划的关键终点,区分在组织筛查内与外部检测到的癌症对于评估过渡期间的计划影响至关重要,例如采用基于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的初级筛查。
设计与对象
2014 年至 2023 年在葡萄牙中部地区进行了一项基于人群的回顾性研究,将有组织的 宫颈癌 筛查数据库记录与医院发病率数据联系起来,将 ICC 病例分类为筛查检测到或非筛查检测。
核心方法
通过筛查和医院记录之间的确定性联系对 ICC 病例进行分类,并使用人口分母计算 发病率 率。具有对数链接和对数转换群体偏移的泊松回归模型估计了 发病率 比率 (IRR),比较基于细胞学 (2014-2019) 和基于 HPV (2020-2023) 的筛查期,并根据时间趋势进行调整。
主要结果
总共纳入了 654 例 ICC 病例,随着时间的推移,筛查参与度基本保持稳定。与细胞学检查期相比,基于 HPV 的筛查期与筛查检出 ICC 的比率显着较高相关(IRR = 1.94;95% CI 1.11–3.40),而有组织的筛查计划之外的 ICC 发病率 在不同时期之间没有显着差异(IRR = 0.94;95% CI 0.65–1.35)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明向基于 HPV 的初级筛查的过渡将 ICC 检测转向有组织的筛查计划,而没有增加在该计划之外诊断的女性中的 发病率,这支持筛查途径内的检测转变,而不是潜在疾病发生的变化。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,基于 HPV 的初级筛查可以改善有组织的项目中 ICC 的早期检测,凸显了整合筛查、医院和癌症登记数据以进行稳健的政策评估的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于基于 HPV 的筛查实施处于早期阶段,并且影响诊断的潜在外部因素,证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性和因果推断。

Invasive Cervical Cancer Detection After Transition to Primary Human Papillomavirus Screening: Population-Based Linkage Study in Portugal.

Authors: Rita Sousa, José Alberto Fonseca-Moutinho, Fábio Sousa Gomes, Fernanda Loureiro, Helena Nascimento, Helena Solheiro, Isabel Saavedra, Madalena Ponte, Mónica Barros, Sofia Pereira
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Invasive cervical cancer (ICC) is a key outcome for evaluating cervical cancer screening (CCS) programs. Monitoring ICC trends and distinguishing cancers detected within and outside the organized screening program are essential for assessing program impact, particularly during transitions such as the introduction of human papillomavirus (HPV) based primary screening. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective population-based study in Portugal's Central Region (2014 - 2023). Deterministic linkage was performed between the organized CCS database and hospital morbidity records. Invasive cervical cancer cases were classified as screen-detected or non-screen-detected based on prior screening history. Incidence rates were calculated using population denominators. Poisson regression models with log link and log-transformed population offsets were fitted to estimate incidence rate ratios (IRR) comparing cytology-based (2014 - 2019) and HPV-based (2020 - 2023) periods, adjusting for temporal trends. RESULTS: A total of 654 ICC cases were included. Screening participation remained broadly stable over time. The HPV-based period was associated with a significantly higher rate of screen-detected ICC (IRR = 1.94; 95% CI 1.11 - 3.40) compared with the cytology period. In contrast, ICC incidence outside the organized screening program did not differ significantly between periods (IRR = 0.94; 95% CI 0.65 - 1.35). CONCLUSION: The transition to HPV-based primary screening was associated with a shift in ICC detection towards the organized screening program, without evidence of a corresponding increase in incidence among women diagnosed outside the organized screening program. These findings should, however, be interpreted with caution in light of the early phase of implementation and potential external factors influencing access to diagnosis. Overall, the results support a shift in detection within the screening pathway rather than changes in underlying disease occurrence. Strengthening integration between screening, hospital, and cancer registry data remains essential for robust evaluation of cervical cancer screening policies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Invasive cervical cancer (ICC) is a critical endpoint for evaluating cervical cancer screening programs, and distinguishing cancers detected within versus outside organized screening is essential to assess program impact during transitions such as the adoption of human papillomavirus (HPV)-based primary screening.
Design and population
A retrospective population-based study was conducted in Portugal’s Central Region from 2014 to 2023, linking organized cervical cancer screening database records with hospital morbidity data to classify ICC cases as screen-detected or non-screen-detected.
Core methods
Deterministic linkage between screening and hospital records was performed to classify ICC cases, and incidence rates were calculated using population denominators. Poisson regression models with log link and log-transformed population offsets estimated incidence rate ratios (IRR) comparing cytology-based (2014–2019) and HPV-based (2020–2023) screening periods, adjusting for temporal trends.
Main results
A total of 654 ICC cases were included, with screening participation remaining broadly stable over time. The HPV-based screening period was associated with a significantly higher rate of screen-detected ICC (IRR = 1.94; 95% CI 1.11–3.40) compared with the cytology period, while ICC incidence outside the organized screening program did not differ significantly between periods (IRR = 0.94; 95% CI 0.65–1.35).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence that the transition to HPV-based primary screening shifted ICC detection toward the organized screening program without increasing incidence among women diagnosed outside it, supporting a detection shift within the screening pathway rather than changes in underlying disease occurrence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that HPV-based primary screening may improve early detection of ICC within organized programs, highlighting the importance of integrating screening, hospital, and cancer registry data for robust policy evaluation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the early phase of HPV-based screening implementation and potential external factors influencing diagnostic access, which may limit generalizability and causal inference.

扁桃体切除术在 人乳头瘤病毒 阴性患者原发灶不明的宫颈鳞状细胞癌中的作用:一项前瞻性观察研究。

Role of Tonsillectomy in Cervical Squamous Cell Carcinoma With Unknown Primary in Human Papillomavirus-Negative Patients: A Prospective Observational Study.
作者： Prateek V. Jain, Bibek Khanal, Vikram D. Kekatpure, Kapila Manikantan, Gurucharan Sahu, Sanjoy Chatterjee, Indranil Mallick, Pattatheyil Arun
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 原发灶不明的宫颈鳞状细胞癌 (SCCUP) 的治疗不断发展,重点是扁桃体切除术以识别原发灶。虽然来自 HPV 阳性人群的数据令人信服,但为了识别 HPV 阴性肿瘤的原发部位而对口咽部潜在原发部位进行手术切除仍存在争议。方法 在这项前瞻性观察研究中,21 名 SCCUP 患者接受了双侧扁桃体切除术,试图确定可能的原发部位。结果 队列研究 的中位年龄为 59 岁(39-74 岁)。20 名患者中有 2 名 p16 呈阳性。只有 1 名患者(p16 阳性)发现扁桃体有原发灶。15 名患者接受辅助同步放化疗 (CTRT),两名患者接受辅助放疗 (RT)。中位随访时间为 17.6 个月(0.26-60 个月)。2年总生存率和无病生存率分别为81.5%(52.6%–93.7%)和70.1%(42.1%–86.4%)。没有患者出现粘膜复发。结论 印度 SCCUP 患者人乳头瘤病毒 患病率 水平非常低。双侧扁桃体切除术在 p16 阴性患者中识别原发病变的作用值得怀疑。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了原发灶不明的宫颈鳞状细胞癌 (SCCUP) 治疗的演变,特别是双侧扁桃体切除术在确定 HPV 阴性患者原发部位方面的争议性作用。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察研究招募了 21 名 SCCUP 患者,他们接受了双侧扁桃体切除术以确定潜在的原发部位,中位年龄为 59 岁(范围 39-74 岁)。
核心方法
该研究对 21 名 SCCUP 患者进行了双侧扁桃体切除术,试图识别原发性病变,并采用 治疗 辅助治疗,其中 15 名患者接受同步放化疗,2 名患者接受放疗。
主要结果
20 名患者中只有 1 名(p16 阳性)发现扁桃体原发性病变,而 2 名患者则为 p16 阳性。2年总生存率和无病生存率分别为81.5%(52.6%–93.7%)和70.1%(42.1%–86.4%)。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,在印度,SCCUP 中 HPV 的 患病率 非常低,双侧扁桃体切除术在 p16 阴性患者中识别原发性病变的作用值得怀疑。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,双侧扁桃体切除术对于确定 HPV 阴性 SCCUP 的原发部位的效用有限,这可能为手术决策提供信息并减少对该人群的不必要干预。
局限与证据强度
样本量小(n=21)、中位随访时间短(17.6 个月)以及缺乏粘膜复发数据限制了证据的强度,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Role of Tonsillectomy in Cervical Squamous Cell Carcinoma With Unknown Primary in Human Papillomavirus-Negative Patients: A Prospective Observational Study.

Authors: Prateek V. Jain, Bibek Khanal, Vikram D. Kekatpure, Kapila Manikantan, Gurucharan Sahu, Sanjoy Chatterjee, Indranil Mallick, Pattatheyil Arun
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Introduction Management of cervical squamous cell carcinoma with unknown primary (SCCUP) continues to evolve with emphasis on tonsillectomy for identification of the primary. While the data from an HPV positive population is compelling, surgical ablation of potential primary sites in the oropharynx with the goal of identifying the primary in HPV negative tumors is debated. Methods In this prospective observational study, 21 patients with SCCUP underwent bilateral tonsillectomy in an attempt to identify a possible site of primary. Results Median age of this cohort was 59 years (39–74 years). Two of twenty patients were p16 positive. Only 1 patient (p16 positive) revealed a primary in the tonsil. Fifteen patients received adjuvant concurrent chemoradiation (CTRT), whereas two patients received adjuvant radiotherapy (RT). Median follow up was 17.6 months (0.26–60 months). The 2‐year overall and disease‐free survival rates were 81.5% (52.6%–93.7%) and 70.1% (42.1%–86.4%), respectively. No patient evinced mucosal recurrence. Conclusions Prevalence of human papilloma virus in patients with SCCUP is very low in India. Role of bilateral tonsillectomy to identify primary lesion in p16 negative patients is questionable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the evolving management of cervical squamous cell carcinoma with unknown primary (SCCUP), particularly the debated role of bilateral tonsillectomy in identifying the primary site in HPV-negative patients.
Design and population
This prospective observational study enrolled 21 patients with SCCUP who underwent bilateral tonsillectomy to identify a potential primary site, with a median age of 59 years (range 39–74 years).
Core methods
The study performed bilateral tonsillectomy in 21 patients with SCCUP to attempt identification of a primary lesion, with adjuvant treatments including concurrent chemoradiation in 15 patients and radiotherapy in 2 patients.
Main results
Only 1 of 20 patients (p16 positive) revealed a primary in the tonsil, while 2 patients were p16 positive. The 2-year overall and disease-free survival rates were 81.5% (52.6%–93.7%) and 70.1% (42.1%–86.4%), respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that the prevalence of HPV in SCCUP is very low in India and that the role of bilateral tonsillectomy to identify primary lesions in p16-negative patients is questionable.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest limited utility of bilateral tonsillectomy for identifying primary sites in HPV-negative SCCUP, which may inform surgical decision-making and reduce unnecessary interventions in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (n=21), short median follow-up (17.6 months), and lack of mucosal recurrence data limit the strength of evidence, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

9 价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 在日本 16-26 岁男性中的功效、免疫原性和安全性:一项随机、双盲、安慰剂对照试验。

Efficacy, immunogenicity, and safety of the 9-valent human papillomavirus vaccine in males aged 16-26 years in Japan: a randomised, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial.
作者： Koji Hisamoto, Masaki Yamanaka, Masayasu Nomura, Nobufumi Kanno, Anna R Giuliano, Keiko Wan, Yukako Fukao, Nobuyuki Oshima, Akira Wakana, Yoshiyuki Tanaka
摘要中文翻译背景:九价 人乳头瘤病毒(9vHPV;HPV6、11、16、18、31、33、45、52 和 58)疫苗 在女性中的功效已在女性中显示,并通过免疫桥联推断对男性的功效。我们评估了 9vHPV 疫苗 在 16-26 岁男性中的疗效。方法:这项随机、双盲、安慰剂对照、功效、免疫原性和安全性 3 期试验在日本 22 个临床研究、医院、医疗或 治疗 场所进行,对研究者、申办者人员和参与者进行了屏蔽。无 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关病变史且既往无 HPV 疫苗接种 病史的 16-26 岁健康男性被随机分配 (1:1),使用排列块(块大小为 4)集中,在第 1 天、第 2 个月和第 6 个月接受 3 次 9vHPV 疫苗 或安慰剂(生理盐水)肌肉注射。主要和次要疗效终点是与 HPV6、11、16 和 18 以及 HPV31、33、45、52 和 58 相关的 6 个月肛门生殖器持续感染的组合 发病率。对肛门生殖器拭子和外生殖器组织样本进行 HPV DNA 检测。组织样本进行组织病理学 诊断 判定。主要和次要疗效评估在符合方案人群中进行了优效性测试,裕度为 0%。另一个次要终点是第 7 个月时对每种 疫苗 靶向 HPV 类型的抗体反应;在符合方案的免疫原性群体中进行免疫原性分析。对接受至少一剂 疫苗 或安慰剂的参与者进行安全性评估。本研究已在 ClinicalTrials.gov (NCT04635423) 注册并已完成。调查结果:2020 年 11 月 30 日至 2021 年 12 月 25 日期间,招募了 1059 名参与者,并随机分配接受 9vHPV 疫苗 (n=529) 或安慰剂 (n=530)。在基于2023年12月29日就诊截止日期的初始疗效分析中,疫苗的主要和次要终点疗效分别为89·3%(95% CI 55·4-98·2;p=0·0002)和63·5%(2·7-86·0;p=0·023)和91·6%在基于 2024 年 7 月 16 日就诊截止日期的研究结束疗效分析中,分别为 (67·7-98·6) 和 72·9% (38·7-89·3)。每种 疫苗 靶向 HPV 类型的血清转化率均大于 98%。529 名 9vHPV 疫苗 接受者中的 370 名(70%)和 530 名安慰剂接受者中的 162 名(31%)报告了注射部位不良事件,58 名(11%)疫苗 接受者和 52 名(10%)安慰剂接受者中报告了与 疫苗 相关的全身不良事件;大多数病例为轻度或中度。没有死亡、疫苗 相关的严重不良事件或因不良事件而停药的报告。解释:9vHPV 疫苗 可预防男性中与 疫苗 靶向 HPV 类型相关的肛门生殖器持续感染,并具有可接受的安全性。这些发现支持在男性中使用 9vHPV 疫苗 来预防 HPV 相关的肛门生殖器疾病。资金来源:Merck Sharp & Dohme。翻译:有关摘要的日语翻译,请参阅补充材料部分。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 持续性肛门生殖器感染会给男性带来巨大的疾病负担,但 9 价 HPV (9vHPV) 疫苗 在该人群中的疗效数据有限。该研究解决了关于 9vHPV 疫苗 是否可以预防 16-26 岁男性持续感染的知识差距。
设计与对象
在日本 22 个临床中心进行了一项随机、双盲、安慰剂对照 3 期试验。既往无 HPV 疫苗接种 或 HPV 相关病变的 16-26 岁健康男性以 1:1 的比例集中随机化,在第 1 天、第 2 个月和第 6 个月接受 3 次肌内注射 9vHPV 疫苗 或生理盐水安慰剂。
核心方法
主要和次要疗效终点是与 HPV6、11、16、18 和 HPV31、33、45、52、58 相关的 6 个月肛门生殖器持续感染的组合 发病率。对肛门生殖器拭子和外生殖器组织样本进行 HPV DNA 检测,并对组织样本进行组织病理学判定。优效性测试在每个方案群体中使用 0% 的裕度。在第 7 个月时,在符合方案的免疫原性人群中评估了免疫原性,并在接受至少一剂的所有参与者中评估了安全性。
主要结果
2020年11月30日至2021年12月25日期间,共有1059名参与者入组(529名疫苗,530名安慰剂)。疫苗 的主要终点疗效在初始分析中为 89.3%(95% CI 55.4-98.2;p=0.0002),在研究结束时为 91.6%(67.7-98.6)。对于次要终点,最初的疗效为 63.5%(2.7-86.0;p=0.023),研究结束时的疗效为 72.9%(38.7-89.3)。每种 疫苗 靶向 HPV 类型的血清转化率均超过 98%。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,9vHPV 疫苗 可预防男性中与 疫苗 靶向 HPV 类型相关的肛门生殖器持续感染,并表现出可接受的安全性。这是 9vHPV 疫苗 在 16-26 岁日本男性中的首次疗效试验,提供了针对多种致癌和非致癌 HPV 类型的保护作用的直接证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在男性中使用 9vHPV 疫苗 来减少 HPV 相关的肛门生殖器疾病,为国家免疫政策提供信息,并将 疫苗接种 建议扩大到包括日本的青少年和年轻成年男性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其随访期短,并且排除了既往有 HPV 相关病变或 疫苗接种 的参与者,这可能会影响普遍性。由于单一国家环境和对替代终点而不是临床疾病结果的依赖,证据是中等的。

Efficacy, immunogenicity, and safety of the 9-valent human papillomavirus vaccine in males aged 16-26 years in Japan: a randomised, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial.

Authors: Koji Hisamoto, Masaki Yamanaka, Masayasu Nomura, Nobufumi Kanno, Anna R Giuliano, Keiko Wan, Yukako Fukao, Nobuyuki Oshima, Akira Wakana, Yoshiyuki Tanaka
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Efficacy of the nine-valent human papillomavirus (9vHPV; HPV6, 11, 16, 18, 31, 33, 45, 52, and 58) vaccine was shown in females and inferred for males through immunobridging. We evaluated the efficacy of the 9vHPV vaccine in males aged 16-26 years. METHODS: This randomised, double-blind, placebo-controlled, efficacy, immunogenicity, and safety phase 3 trial was conducted at 22 clinical research, hospital, medical, or treatment sites in Japan, with masking of investigators, sponsor personnel, and participants. Healthy males aged 16-26 years with no history of human papillomavirus (HPV)-related lesions and no previous HPV vaccination were randomly assigned (1:1) centrally, using permutated blocks (block sizes of four), to receive three intramuscular injections of the 9vHPV vaccine or placebo (saline) at day 1, month 2, and month 6. The primary and secondary efficacy endpoints were the combined incidences of 6-month anogenital persistent infection related to HPV6, 11, 16, and 18 and HPV31, 33, 45, 52, and 58. Anogenital swabs and external genital tissue samples were tested for the presence of HPV DNA. Tissue samples were adjudicated for histopathology diagnosis. The primary and secondary efficacy evaluations were tested for superiority with a margin of 0% in the per-protocol population. Another secondary endpoint was antibody responses to each vaccine-targeted HPV type at month 7; immunogenicity analyses were conducted in the per-protocol immunogenicity population. Safety was assessed in participants who received at least one dose of vaccine or placebo. This study is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov (NCT04635423) and is completed. FINDINGS: Between Nov 30, 2020, and Dec 25, 2021, 1059 participants were enrolled and randomly assigned to receive the 9vHPV vaccine (n=529) or placebo (n=530). Vaccine efficacy for the primary and secondary endpoints was 89·3% (95% CI 55·4-98·2; p=0·0002) and 63·5% (2·7-86·0; p=0·023), respectively, in the initial efficacy analysis based on a visit cutoff date of Dec 29, 2023, and 91·6% (67·7-98·6) and 72·9% (38·7-89·3) in the end-of-study efficacy analysis based on a visit cutoff date of July 16, 2024. Seroconversion rates were greater than 98% for each vaccine-targeted HPV type. Injection-site adverse events were reported in 370 (70%) of 529 9vHPV vaccine recipients and 162 (31%) of 530 placebo recipients, and vaccine-related systemic adverse events in 58 (11%) vaccine recipients and 52 (10%) placebo recipients; most cases were mild or moderate. No deaths, vaccine-related serious adverse events, or discontinuations due to adverse events were reported. INTERPRETATION: The 9vHPV vaccine prevents anogenital persistent infection related to vaccine-targeted HPV types in males and has an acceptable safety profile. These findings support the use of the 9vHPV vaccine in males to prevent HPV-related anogenital diseases. FUNDING: Merck Sharp & Dohme. TRANSLATIONS: For the Japanese translation of the abstract see Supplementary Material section.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent anogenital infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) types causes substantial disease burden in males, yet efficacy data for the 9-valent HPV (9vHPV) vaccine in this population were limited. The study addressed the knowledge gap regarding whether the 9vHPV vaccine prevents persistent infection in males aged 16–26 years.
Design and population
A randomised, double-blind, placebo-controlled phase 3 trial was conducted at 22 clinical sites in Japan. Healthy males aged 16–26 years with no prior HPV vaccination or HPV-related lesions were centrally randomised 1:1 to receive three intramuscular injections of 9vHPV vaccine or saline placebo at day 1, month 2, and month 6.
Core methods
The primary and secondary efficacy endpoints were the combined incidences of 6-month anogenital persistent infection related to HPV6, 11, 16, 18 and HPV31, 33, 45, 52, 58. Anogenital swabs and external genital tissue samples were tested for HPV DNA, with tissue samples adjudicated for histopathology. Superiority testing used a 0% margin in the per-protocol population. Immunogenicity was assessed at month 7 in the per-protocol immunogenicity population, and safety was evaluated in all participants receiving at least one dose.
Main results
Between Nov 30, 2020, and Dec 25, 2021, 1059 participants were enrolled (529 vaccine, 530 placebo). Vaccine efficacy for the primary endpoint was 89.3% (95% CI 55.4–98.2; p=0.0002) in the initial analysis and 91.6% (67.7–98.6) at study end. For the secondary endpoint, efficacy was 63.5% (2.7–86.0; p=0.023) initially and 72.9% (38.7–89.3) at study end. Seroconversion rates exceeded 98% for each vaccine-targeted HPV type.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that the 9vHPV vaccine prevents anogenital persistent infection related to vaccine-targeted HPV types in males and demonstrates an acceptable safety profile. This is the first efficacy trial of the 9vHPV vaccine in Japanese males aged 16–26 years, providing direct evidence of protection against multiple oncogenic and non-oncogenic HPV types.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of the 9vHPV vaccine in males to reduce HPV-related anogenital diseases, informing national immunisation policies and expanding vaccination recommendations to include adolescent and young adult males in Japan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its short follow-up period and the exclusion of participants with prior HPV-related lesions or vaccination, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate due to the single-country setting and reliance on surrogate endpoints rather than clinical disease outcomes.

乳腺癌中 HPV16 E5、E6 和 E7 癌基因表达介导的 PI3K/AKT/mTOR 信号通路的转录调节。

Transcriptional modulation of the PI3K/AKT/mTOR signaling pathway mediated by HPV16 E5 , E6 , and E7 oncogene expression in breast cancer.
作者： Beatriz Eda de Oliveira Isídio, Pedro Henrique Bezerra Fontes, Gabriel Rômulo Parente da Silva, Stephanie Loureiro Leão, Bianca de França São Marcos, Isabelle Silva Simões, Elisa Fotin Genn Barros, David Beltrán Lussón, Gabriela Vitória de Araujo, Isabela Duarte de Farias
摘要中文翻译目的 乳腺癌是女性中最常见的恶性肿瘤。自1990年代以来,人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)已在乳腺肿瘤中被检测到。除其致癌潜能外,由病毒介导的免疫逃避在非肛门生殖器肿瘤(如口咽癌)中驱动治疗耐药,凸显了在乳腺组织中探究病毒活性的必要性。在高危型HPV中,HPV16是最常见且致癌潜能最高的型别之一。因此,本研究旨在评估HPV16致癌基因E5、E6和E7在乳腺肿瘤中的表达,以及与病毒活性相关的PI3K/AKT/mTOR信号通路调节。方法 在获得伦理委员会批准后,纳入92例乳腺癌患者。临床数据来自医疗记录。从福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋组织中提取RNA并逆转录为cDNA。通过RT-qPCR定量检测HPV致癌基因(E5、E6和E7)、PI3K/AKT/mTOR通路组分及调控基因(EGFR和PTEN)的转录本。基因表达水平采用ΔCt法计算。结果 48份样本满足RNA质量标准并纳入表达分析。其中,77.08%的样本至少检测到一种病毒致癌基因表达,E5为最常见表达基因。在HPV阳性样本中,PI3K/AKT/mTOR通路被调节,表现为PI3K表达升高和mTOR表达降低。值得注意的是,与免疫逃避相关的E5高表达结合mTOR表达降低提示HPV16状态可能影响乳腺癌患者的治疗反应。结论 这些发现强调了进一步研究HPV在乳腺肿瘤中的活性,以更好地理解其生物学和临床影响的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乳腺癌是女性中最常见的恶性肿瘤,而高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),特别是HPV16,自1990年代以来已在乳腺肿瘤中被检测到。除其致癌潜能外,病毒介导的免疫逃避在非肛门生殖器肿瘤(如口咽癌)中凸显了探究HPV在乳腺组织中活性的必要性。
设计与对象
经伦理委员会批准后,共纳入92例乳腺癌患者,并从病历中获取临床数据。从福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织中提取 RNA 并反转录成 cDNA 以供下游分析。
核心方法
通过 RT-qPCR 对 HPV 癌基因(E5、E6 和 E7)、PI3K/AKT/mTOR 通路成分以及调节基因(EGFR 和 PTEN)的转录本进行定量。使用ΔCt方法计算基因表达水平。
主要结果
48 个样本符合 RNA 质量标准并被纳入表达分析。其中,77.08%表现出至少一种病毒癌基因的表达,其中E5表达最频繁。HPV 阳性样本中 PI3K/AKT/mTOR 通路受到调节,PI3K 表达增加,mTOR 表达减少。
解释与创新
E5 的高表达与免疫逃避相关,再加上 mTOR 表达的减少,表明 HPV16 状态可能会影响乳腺癌患者的治疗反应。这些发现强调了进一步研究乳腺肿瘤中 HPV 活性的重要性,以更好地了解其生物学和临床影响。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了 HPV16 癌基因表达在调节乳腺癌 PI3K/AKT/mTOR 通路中的潜在作用,这可能为未来有关 HPV 相关肿瘤的治疗抵抗和免疫逃避机制的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量的限制,并且依赖于从福尔马林固定、石蜡包埋的组织中提取的 RNA,这可能会影响 RNA 的质量和定量准确性。由于观察设计和缺乏对观察到的通路调节的功能验证,证据强度中等。

Transcriptional modulation of the PI3K/AKT/mTOR signaling pathway mediated by HPV16 E5 , E6 , and E7 oncogene expression in breast cancer.

Authors: Beatriz Eda de Oliveira Isídio, Pedro Henrique Bezerra Fontes, Gabriel Rômulo Parente da Silva, Stephanie Loureiro Leão, Bianca de França São Marcos, Isabelle Silva Simões, Elisa Fotin Genn Barros, David Beltrán Lussón, Gabriela Vitória de Araujo, Isabela Duarte de Farias
Original abstract (English)
Aim Breast cancer is the most prevalent malignant tumor among women. Human papillomavirus (HPV) has been detected in breast tumors since the 1990s, and beyond its oncogenic potential, therapy resistance driven by viral immune evasion in non-anogenital tumors, such as oropharyngeal cancers, highlights the need to investigate viral activity in breast tissues. Among high-risk HPV types, HPV16 is one of the most prevalent and exhibits the highest carcinogenic potential. Therefore, this study aimed to evaluate the expression of HPV16 oncogenes E5 , E6 , and E7 in breast tumors, as well as the modulation of the PI3K/AKT/mTOR signaling pathway associated with viral activity. Methods A total of 92 breast cancer patients were included after Ethics Committee approval. Clinical data were obtained from medical records. RNA was extracted from formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissues and reverse-transcribed into cDNA. Transcripts of HPV oncogenes ( E5 , E6 , and E7 ), components of the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway, and regulatory genes ( EGFR and PTEN ) were quantified by RT-qPCR. Gene expression levels were calculated using the ΔCt method. Results Forty-eight samples met RNA quality criteria and were included in the expression analysis. Among these, 77.08% showed expression of at least one viral oncogene, with E5 being the most frequently expressed. The PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway was modulated in HPV-positive samples, with increased PI3K expression and decreased mTOR expression. Notably, the high expression of E5-associated with immune evasion-combined with reduced mTOR expression suggests that HPV16 status may influence therapeutic response in breast cancer patients. Conclusions These findings reinforce the importance of further studies investigating HPV activity in breast tumors to better understand its biological and clinical impact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Breast cancer is the most prevalent malignant tumor among women, and high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV), particularly HPV16, has been detected in breast tumors since the 1990s. Beyond its oncogenic potential, viral immune evasion in non-anogenital tumors such as oropharyngeal cancers underscores the need to investigate HPV activity in breast tissues.
Design and population
A total of 92 breast cancer patients were included after Ethics Committee approval, and clinical data were obtained from medical records. RNA was extracted from formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissues and reverse-transcribed into cDNA for downstream analyses.
Core methods
Transcripts of HPV oncogenes (E5, E6, and E7), components of the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway, and regulatory genes (EGFR and PTEN) were quantified by RT-qPCR. Gene expression levels were calculated using the ΔCt method.
Main results
Forty-eight samples met RNA quality criteria and were included in the expression analysis. Among these, 77.08% showed expression of at least one viral oncogene, with E5 being the most frequently expressed. The PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway was modulated in HPV-positive samples, with increased PI3K expression and decreased mTOR expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The high expression of E5, associated with immune evasion, combined with reduced mTOR expression suggests that HPV16 status may influence therapeutic response in breast cancer patients. These findings reinforce the importance of further studies investigating HPV activity in breast tumors to better understand its biological and clinical impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights a potential role for HPV16 oncogene expression in modulating the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway in breast cancer, which may inform future research on therapeutic resistance and immune evasion mechanisms in HPV-associated tumors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its sample size and reliance on RNA extracted from formalin-fixed, paraffin-embedded tissues, which may affect RNA quality and quantification accuracy. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational design and lack of functional validation of the observed pathway modulation.

二氧化硅阴道凝胶和短期高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA 可检测性:一项随机对照试验。

Silicon dioxide vaginal gel and short-term high-risk human papillomavirus DNA detectability: A randomized controlled trial.
作者： Julia M. Hecken, Clemens B. Tempfer, Katrin Klaus, Jan Scheich, Günther A. Rezniczek
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估与单独观察相比,每日阴道内使用二氧化硅凝胶 治疗 是否会增加未检测到宫颈高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) DNA 的女性比例。检验这些针对观察的短期主张的独立随机证据是有限的。
设计与对象
这是一项前瞻性、单中心、平行组、随机、开放标签对照试验,涉及 242 名经 PCR 确诊宫颈 hrHPV 感染且无立即 治疗 指征的女性。参与者按照 1:1 的比例随机分配,接受为期 3 个月的自我施用每日阴道内凝胶 治疗 或单独观察。
核心方法
该研究使用 PCR 来评估 3 个月时是否存在可检测到的宫颈 hrHPV DNA。次要结局包括随机研究期间患者报告的满意度和不良事件。
主要结果
3个月时,凝胶治疗组中18/99名女性(18%)和观察组中23/109名女性(21%)未检测到hrHPV DNA。凝胶 治疗 组中患者报告的不良事件比观察组更常见(32/99 [32%] vs.
解释与创新
作者得出结论,与单独观察相比,每日阴道内使用二氧化硅凝胶 治疗 3 个月并不能改善可检测宫颈 hrHPV DNA 的短期损失。该试验没有重现先前产品相关研究所暗示的巨大短期病毒学益处。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对于没有立即 治疗 指征的女性,这种干预措施对于促进短期 hrHPV 不可检测性无效,特别是考虑到局部不良事件发生频率较高。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量相对较小,可获取 208 名女性的主要终点数据,事后调整的敏感性分析并没有实质性改变主要比较的解释。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Silicon dioxide vaginal gel and short-term high-risk human papillomavirus DNA detectability: A randomized controlled trial.

Authors: Julia M. Hecken, Clemens B. Tempfer, Katrin Klaus, Jan Scheich, Günther A. Rezniczek
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Intravaginal products, including silicon dioxide-based gels, are marketed to support short-term high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV) clearance or loss of detectability, but independent randomized evidence testing these short-term claims against observation is limited. We evaluated whether daily intravaginal silicon dioxide-based gel treatment increases the proportion of women with no detectable cervical hrHPV DNA compared with observation alone. MATERIAL AND METHODS: This prospective, single-center, parallel-group, randomized, open-label controlled trial included women with PCR-confirmed cervical hrHPV infection and no indication for immediate treatment. Participants were randomized 1:1 to 3 months of self-administered daily intravaginal gel treatment or observation alone. The primary outcome was absence of detectable cervical hrHPV DNA by PCR at 3 months. Secondary outcomes during the randomized study period were patient-reported satisfaction and adverse events. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT05509413, https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05509413. RESULTS: Between November 2022 and January 2025, 242 women were randomized, with 121 allocated to each group. Primary-endpoint data were available for 208 women. At 3 months, hrHPV DNA was not detected in 18/99 women (18%) in the gel treatment group and 23/109 women (21%) in the observation group (absolute risk difference -2.9 percentage points, 95% CI -14 to 8; p = 0.60). A reduced post hoc adjusted sensitivity analysis did not materially change the interpretation of the primary comparison. Patient-reported adverse events were more frequent in the gel treatment group than in the observation group (32/99 [32%] vs. 2/106 [2%]; absolute risk difference 30 percentage points, 95% CI 21 to 40; p < 0.001). Satisfaction scores were high in both groups but lower with gel treatment. CONCLUSIONS: In this randomized trial, 3 months of daily intravaginal silicon dioxide-based gel treatment did not improve short-term loss of detectable cervical hrHPV DNA compared with observation alone. The trial did not reproduce the large short-term virologic benefit suggested by prior product-related studies. The findings do not support routine use of this intervention solely to promote short-term hrHPV non-detectability in women without an indication for immediate treatment, particularly given the higher frequency of local adverse events.

莱索托青春期女孩对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的吸收程度及其决定因素:一项横断面研究。

Magnitude of human papillomavirus vaccination uptake and its determinants among adolescent girls in Lesotho: a cross-sectional study.
作者： Tsion Mulat Tebeje, Kassahun Animut Metkie, Yordanos Sisay Asgedom, Amanuel Yosef Gebrekidan, Seblewongel Gebretsadik Sertsewold, Ermias Bekele Enyew, Mesfin Abebe
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 为了消除 宫颈癌 这一公共卫生问题,世界卫生组织建议将 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 纳入国家免疫规划,并作为一级预防覆盖 90% 的 15 岁女孩。莱索托于2022年重新引入国家HPV 疫苗计划。目的调查莱索托少女中HPV 患病率及HPV 疫苗接种摄取的相关因素。设计 基于人群的横断面研究设计。参与者 15-17 岁的女孩。主要和次要结果测量 应用具有稳健方差的多级泊松回归模型来确定与 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取相关的因素。报告了调整后的 患病率 比率,置信区间为 95%。结果 莱索托少女中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取率为 52.15%(95% CI 48.43 至 55.84)。16 岁和 17 岁的女孩以及居住在马塞卢地区的女孩与 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取比例较低显着相关。然而,听说过 宫颈癌 和性传播感染 (STI) 的人被发现与较高比例的 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取有关。结论 莱索托少女中 HPV 疫苗接种 的摄取率远远落后于世界卫生组织 2030 年目标。通过有针对性的教育提高对 宫颈癌 和 HPV 疫苗接种 的认识至关重要。包含有关性传播感染和 HPV 信息的全面性健康教育计划可以提高 疫苗接种 的使用率,特别是在使用率较低的地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查莱索托少女中的 患病率 和 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取的相关因素,因为消除 宫颈癌 需要在 15 岁时达到 90% 的 疫苗接种 覆盖率。
设计与对象
在莱索托 15-17 岁女孩中进行了一项基于人群的横断面研究,该研究于 2022 年重新引入了国家 HPV 疫苗 计划。
核心方法
使用具有稳健方差的多级泊松回归模型来确定与 HPV 疫苗接种 摄取相关的因素,重点关注 患病率 及其决定因素。
主要结果
莱索托少女中 HPV 疫苗接种 的感染率为 52.15%(95% CI 48.43 至 55.84)。
解释与创新
该研究强调,对 宫颈癌 和性传播感染的认识与较高的 疫苗接种 吸收率有关,而年龄较大和居住在马塞卢地区则与较低的吸收率相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为有针对性的教育策略提供了信息,以提高 HPV 疫苗接种 率,特别是在感染率较低的地区,并强调了预防 宫颈癌 宣传活动的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系,而且由于自我报告数据存在潜在偏差,证据不充分。

Magnitude of human papillomavirus vaccination uptake and its determinants among adolescent girls in Lesotho: a cross-sectional study.

Authors: Tsion Mulat Tebeje, Kassahun Animut Metkie, Yordanos Sisay Asgedom, Amanuel Yosef Gebrekidan, Seblewongel Gebretsadik Sertsewold, Ermias Bekele Enyew, Mesfin Abebe
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background To eliminate cervical cancer as a public health problem, the WHO recommends that human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines should be included in national immunisation programmes and should reach 90% of all girls by age 15 as primary prevention. Lesotho reintroduced the national HPV vaccine programme in 2022. Objective To investigate the prevalence and associated factors of HPV vaccination uptake among adolescent girls in Lesotho. Design A population-based cross-sectional study design. Participants Girls aged 15–17. Primary and secondary outcome measures A multilevel Poisson regression model with robust variance was applied to identify factors associated with HPV vaccination uptake. An adjusted prevalence ratio with a 95% CI was reported. Results The uptake of HPV vaccination among adolescent girls in Lesotho was 52.15% (95% CI 48.43 to 55.84). Girls aged 16 and 17 and those residing in the Maseru district were significantly associated with a lower proportion of HPV vaccination uptake. While, ever heard of cervical cancer and ever heard of sexually transmitted infections (STIs) were found to be linked with a higher proportion of HPV vaccination uptake. Conclusion HPV vaccination uptake among adolescent girls in Lesotho was far behind the WHO 2030 target. Raising awareness about cervical cancer and HPV vaccination via targeted education is vital. A comprehensive sexual health education programme that includes information on STIs and HPV can improve vaccination uptake, especially in districts with low uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the prevalence and associated factors of HPV vaccination uptake among adolescent girls in Lesotho, as cervical cancer elimination requires reaching 90% vaccination coverage by age 15.
Design and population
A population-based cross-sectional study was conducted among girls aged 15–17 in Lesotho, which reintroduced the national HPV vaccine programme in 2022.
Core methods
A multilevel Poisson regression model with robust variance was used to identify factors associated with HPV vaccination uptake, focusing on prevalence and its determinants.
Main results
The uptake of HPV vaccination among adolescent girls in Lesotho was 52.15% (95% CI 48.43 to 55.84).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that awareness about cervical cancer and STIs is linked to higher vaccination uptake, while older age and residence in Maseru district are associated with lower uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform targeted education strategies to improve HPV vaccination rates, particularly in districts with low uptake, and emphasize the importance of awareness campaigns for cervical cancer prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the evidence is moderate due to potential biases in self-reported data.

老挝人民民主共和国提供综合服务以改善青少年健康和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种:一项准实验混合方法研究 - 研究方案。

Integrated service delivery to improve adolescent health and human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in Lao PDR: a quasi-experimental mixed-methods study - study protocol.
作者： Thet Lynn, Vanphanom Sychareun, Kongmany Chaleunvong, Viengnakhone Vongxay, Souksamone Thongmixay, Chandavieng Phimmavong, Chansathith Taikeophithoun, Josien Van Der Kooij
摘要中文翻译摘要 介绍 老挝人民民主共和国 (PDR) 面临着巨大的公共卫生挑战,包括较高的 宫颈癌 发病率 覆盖率和次优的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 覆盖率,以及更广泛的青少年健康问题,例如青少年怀孕率高和性与生殖健康 (SRH) 素养不足。本研究协议概述了一种通过评估集成服务交付模型来全面解决这些相互关联的问题的方法。方法和分析 本研究采用混合方法的准实验设计来评估包含 HPV 疫苗接种 的综合服务包和改善老挝人民民主共和国 Louangnamtha 省两个地区的青少年健康服务的影响。HPV 疫苗接种 的研究人群包括 10-13 岁的女孩,其他青少年健康服务的研究人群包括 10-13 岁的男孩和女孩。干预区获得了通过基于学校、基于设施和外展方法提供的综合服务包。比较区继续提供标准 HPV 疫苗接种 和常规青少年健康服务,其中不包括干预区提供的额外进修培训、全面性教育或专门的 SRH 外展服务。干预期为 7 个月,从 2024 年 11 月至 2025 年 5 月。在基线、中线(3 个月时)和终线(7 个月时)收集 HPV 疫苗 摄取、SRH 知识和态度以及青少年卫生服务利用情况的定量数据。通过与青少年、护理人员、医疗保健提供者、教育工作者、社区领袖和政策制定者进行深入访谈和焦点小组讨论,收集了有关实施障碍和促进因素、提供者工作量和能力以及利益相关者看法的定性数据。伦理与传播 该研究已获得老挝健康科学研究大学伦理委员会的伦理批准(ID:833/REC,日期为 2024 年 9 月 4 日)。已获得所有参与者或其法定监护人的知情同意。研究结果将通过学术出版物、政策简报、研讨会和社区参与活动进行传播,为老挝人民民主共和国的政策和实践提供信息,并为综合服务提供模式的全球知识库做出贡献。试验注册号NCT06650956。

问题与背景
本研究协议概述了一种通过评估集成服务交付模型来全面解决这些相互关联的问题的方法。
设计与对象
方法和分析 本研究采用混合方法的准实验设计来评估包含 HPV 疫苗接种 的综合服务包和改善老挝人民民主共和国 Louangnamtha 省两个地区的青少年健康服务的影响。
核心方法
HPV 疫苗接种 的研究人群包括 10-13 岁的女孩,其他青少年健康服务的研究人群包括 10-13 岁的男孩和女孩。
主要结果
研究结果将通过学术出版物、政策简报、研讨会和社区参与活动进行传播,为老挝人民民主共和国的政策和实践提供信息,并为综合服务提供模式的全球知识库做出贡献。
解释与创新
已获得所有参与者或其法定监护人的知情同意。

Integrated service delivery to improve adolescent health and human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in Lao PDR: a quasi-experimental mixed-methods study - study protocol.

Authors: Thet Lynn, Vanphanom Sychareun, Kongmany Chaleunvong, Viengnakhone Vongxay, Souksamone Thongmixay, Chandavieng Phimmavong, Chansathith Taikeophithoun, Josien Van Der Kooij
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction The Lao People’s Democratic Republic (PDR) faces the significant public health challenges of high cervical cancer incidence and suboptimal human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine coverage alongside broader adolescent health concerns such as high adolescent pregnancy rates and inadequate sexual and reproductive health (SRH) literacy. This study protocol outlines an approach to address these interconnected issues comprehensively by evaluating an integrated service delivery model. Methods and analysis This study employed a quasi-experimental design with mixed methods to assess the impact of an integrated service package comprising HPV vaccination and improved adolescent health services in two districts of Louangnamtha province in Lao PDR. The study population included girls aged 10–13 years for HPV vaccination and boys and girls aged 10–13 years for other adolescent health services. The intervention district received this integrated service package delivered through school-based, facility-based and outreach approaches. The comparison district continued with its standard HPV vaccination and routine adolescent health service delivery, which does not include the additional refresher training, comprehensive sexuality education or dedicated SRH outreach services provided in the intervention district. The intervention period was 7 months, from November 2024 to May 2025. Quantitative data on HPV vaccine uptake, SRH knowledge and attitudes and utilisation of adolescent health services were collected at baseline, midline (at 3 months) and endline (at 7 months). Qualitative data on barriers and facilitators to implementation, provider workload and capacity, and stakeholder perceptions were collected through in-depth interviews and focus group discussions with adolescents, caregivers, healthcare providers, educators, community leaders and policymakers at endline. Ethics and dissemination Ethical approval for the study has been received from the University of Health Sciences Research Ethics Committee in Laos (ID: 833/REC, dated 4 September 2024). Informed consent was obtained from all participants or their legal guardians. Findings will be disseminated through academic publications, policy briefs, workshops and community engagement activities to inform policies and practices in Lao PDR and contribute to the global knowledge base on integrated service delivery models. Trial registration number NCT06650956.

家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 预防 宫颈癌 的知识和看法的决定因素

Determinants of Parents’ Knowledge and Perceptions Regarding Human Papillomavirus Vaccination for Cervical Cancer Prevention
作者： Mohamed Said NCIRI, Ons TOUAHRI, Omar ABDELHEDI, Fatma KHANFIR, Yesmin DAHECH, Alya BENGAYED, Kais CHABBENE, Bassem ABID
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估突尼斯 12-18 岁女孩父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 的了解和看法,并找出与了解不足相关的因素。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 是预防 宫颈癌 和其他 HPV 相关疾病的高效策略。
设计与对象
对突尼斯斯法克斯 12 至 18 岁女孩的父母进行了一项定量、描述性、横断面和分析性研究。在 350 名参与者中,中位年龄为 35 岁,其中 302 名(86.3%)是母亲。
核心方法
使用匿名自填问卷收集数据,评估社会人口学特征、HPV 感染和 疫苗接种 的知识以及对 HPV 疫苗接种 的看法。分析了与知识匮乏相关的因素。
主要结果
尽管238名(68.0%)家长听说过人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),262名(74.9%)听说过HPV 疫苗接种,但知识水平仍有限,中位得分为2分(四分位距:1–3分);139名(39.7%)参与者知识水平较差。仅16名(4.6%)报告其子女已接种HPV 疫苗接种,而167名(47.7%)拒绝接种。
解释与创新
教育程度较低、社会经济地位较低、居住在农村、失业、以前缺乏 HPV 认识以及缺乏社交媒体使用与知识匮乏有关。尽管突尼斯对 HPV 的认识相对较高,但家长的知识和 疫苗 的吸收率仍然不足。
科研与公卫意义
需要医疗保健专业人员进行有针对性的教育、以学校为基础的宣传计划和基于证据的沟通策略,以提高 HPV 疫苗 的接受度。研究结果可为旨在提高突尼斯少女父母 HPV 疫苗接种 感染率的公共卫生举措提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于从突尼斯单个城市收集的数据,这可能会影响普遍性。证据基于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏见。信心是中等的。

Determinants of Parents’ Knowledge and Perceptions Regarding Human Papillomavirus Vaccination for Cervical Cancer Prevention

Authors: Mohamed Said NCIRI, Ons TOUAHRI, Omar ABDELHEDI, Fatma KHANFIR, Yesmin DAHECH, Alya BENGAYED, Kais CHABBENE, Bassem ABID
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction: Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a highly effective strategy for preventing cervical cancer and other HPV-related diseases. Parental knowledge and perceptions play a critical role in vaccine acceptance. This study aimed to assess parents’ knowledge and perceptions regarding HPV vaccination and identify factors associated with poor knowledge among parents of girls aged 12–18 years in Tunisia. Methods: A quantitative, descriptive, cross-sectional, and analytical study was conducted among parents of girls aged 12–18 years in Sfax, Tunisia. Data were collected using an anonymous self-administered questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV infection and vaccination, and perceptions toward HPV vaccination. Factors associated with poor knowledge were analyzed. Results: Among 350 participants, the median age was 35 years, and 302 (86.3%) were mothers. Although 238 (68.0%) parents had heard of HPV and 262 (74.9%) had heard of HPV vaccination, knowledge remained limited, with a median score of 2 (IQR: 1–3); 139 (39.7%) participants had poor knowledge. Only 16 (4.6%) reported that their child had received HPV vaccination, whereas 167 (47.7%) refused it. The main barriers were concerns about vaccine side effects (46.0%) and insufficient information (31.1%). Lower education, low socioeconomic status, rural residence, unemployment, lack of previous HPV awareness, and absence of social media use were associated with poor knowledge . Conclusion: Despite relatively high HPV awareness, parental knowledge and vaccine uptake remain insufficient in Tunisia. Targeted education by healthcare professionals, school-based awareness programs, and evidence-based communication strategies are needed to improve HPV vaccine acceptance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess parents’ knowledge and perceptions regarding HPV vaccination and identify factors associated with poor knowledge among parents of girls aged 12–18 years in Tunisia. Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is a highly effective strategy for preventing cervical cancer and other HPV-related diseases.
Design and population
A quantitative, descriptive, cross-sectional, and analytical study was conducted among parents of girls aged 12–18 years in Sfax, Tunisia. Among 350 participants, the median age was 35 years, and 302 (86.3%) were mothers.
Core methods
Data were collected using an anonymous self-administered questionnaire assessing sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge of HPV infection and vaccination, and perceptions toward HPV vaccination. Factors associated with poor knowledge were analyzed.
Main results
Although 238 (68.0%) parents had heard of HPV and 262 (74.9%) had heard of HPV vaccination, knowledge remained limited, with a median score of 2 (IQR: 1–3); 139 (39.7%) participants had poor knowledge. Only 16 (4.6%) reported that their child had received HPV vaccination, whereas 167 (47.7%) refused it.
Interpretation and novelty
Lower education, low socioeconomic status, rural residence, unemployment, lack of previous HPV awareness, and absence of social media use were associated with poor knowledge. Despite relatively high HPV awareness, parental knowledge and vaccine uptake remain insufficient in Tunisia.
Scientific and public-health significance
Targeted education by healthcare professionals, school-based awareness programs, and evidence-based communication strategies are needed to improve HPV vaccine acceptance. The findings can inform public health initiatives aimed at increasing HPV vaccination rates among parents of adolescent girls in Tunisia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to data collected from a single city in Tunisia, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is based on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The confidence is moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HPV-16 衣壳弯曲刚度的原子测定

Atomistic determination of bending rigidity in the HPV-16 capsid
作者： David Hernandez-Leon, Jason Peña, Leonardo Dagdug, David Reguera
摘要中文翻译病毒衣壳的组装是由蛋白质构建块之间的局部相互作用产生的,这些蛋白质构建块共同生成具有明确几何形状和强大机械性能的高度有序的外壳。然而,原子相互作用与决定衣壳形状和刚性的宏观弹性参数之间的定量联系仍然难以捉摸。在这里,我们提出了一个在 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型(HPV-16)中连接分子相互作用和连续尺度力学的原子框架。使用伞形采样的全原子分子动力学模拟,我们评估了控制相邻 L1 五聚体之间角度偏差的平均力的潜力,并提取了首选相互作用角度和相关的弯曲模量。我们发现衣壳的自发曲率和弯曲刚度之间存在明显的脱钩。虽然最佳相互作用角在 pH 值和离子强度变化时几乎保持不变,但弯曲模量比之前的任何估计大一个数量级,并受到静电环境的强烈调节:质子化在低离子强度下增强角刚度,而静电屏蔽在较高盐浓度下抑制这种效应。我们的结果确定衣壳间刚性是衣壳大小和形状的关键决定因素,并提供了将特定蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用与自组装病毒衣壳的新兴机械特性联系起来的定量框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解分子相互作用与病毒衣壳机械特性之间的关系,特别是解决缺乏将原子相互作用与 HPV-16 宏观弹性参数联系起来的定量数据的问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用原子框架来研究 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV-16),使用全原子分子动力学模拟和伞形采样来评估控制相邻 L1 五聚体之间角度偏差的平均力的潜力。
核心方法
研究人员使用伞形采样的全原子分子动力学模拟来评估平均力的潜力,并提取 HPV-16 中相邻 L1 五聚体之间的首选相互作用角度和弯曲模量。
主要结果
研究发现 HPV-16 衣壳的自发曲率与其弯曲刚度之间存在明显的脱钩,弯曲模量比之前的估计大一个数量级,并且受到静电环境的强烈影响。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,衣壳间刚性是衣壳大小和形状的关键决定因素,为理解特定蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用如何影响自组装病毒衣壳的机械特性提供了定量框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HPV-16 衣壳机械行为的深入了解,这可以为开发更有效的诊断工具、治疗策略和针对 HPV 相关疾病的 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于原子框架,不包括实验验证,但由于详细的计算分析和清晰的结果,证据是强有力的。

Atomistic determination of bending rigidity in the HPV-16 capsid

Authors: David Hernandez-Leon, Jason Peña, Leonardo Dagdug, David Reguera
Original abstract (English)
The assembly of viral capsids emerges from local interactions between protein building blocks that collectively generate highly ordered shells with defined geometry and robust mechanical properties. However, a quantitative link between atomistic interactions and the macroscopic elastic parameters that determine capsid shape and rigidity has remained elusive. Here, we present an atomistic framework that bridges molecular interactions and continuum-scale mechanics in human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV-16). Using all-atom molecular dynamics simulations with umbrella sampling, we evaluate the potential of mean force governing angular deviations between neighboring L1 pentamers and extract both the preferred interaction angle and the associated bending modulus. We find a clear decoupling between spontaneous curvature of the capsid and bending rigidity. While the optimal interaction angle remains almost invariant across changes in pH and ionic strength, the bending modulus, which is an order of magnitude larger than any previous estimates, is strongly modulated by the electrostatic environment: protonation enhances angular stiffness at low ionic strength, whereas electrostatic screening suppresses this effect at higher salt concentrations. Our results identify inter-capsomer rigidity as a key determinant of capsid size and shape and provide a quantitative framework for linking specific protein–protein interactions to the emergent mechanical properties of self-assembled viral capsids.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the relationship between molecular interactions and the mechanical properties of viral capsids, specifically addressing the lack of quantitative data linking atomistic interactions to macroscopic elastic parameters in HPV-16.
Design and population
The study employed an atomistic framework to investigate human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV-16) using all-atom molecular dynamics simulations with umbrella sampling to evaluate the potential of mean force governing angular deviations between neighboring L1 pentamers.
Core methods
The researchers used all-atom molecular dynamics simulations with umbrella sampling to assess the potential of mean force and extract the preferred interaction angle and bending modulus between neighboring L1 pentamers in HPV-16.
Main results
The study found a clear decoupling between the spontaneous curvature of the HPV-16 capsid and its bending rigidity, with the bending modulus being an order of magnitude larger than previous estimates and strongly influenced by the electrostatic environment.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that inter-capsomer rigidity is a key determinant of capsid size and shape, offering a quantitative framework for understanding how specific protein–protein interactions influence the mechanical properties of self-assembled viral capsids.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the mechanical behavior of HPV-16 capsids, which could inform the development of more effective diagnostic tools, therapeutic strategies, and vaccine design for HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an atomistic framework and does not include experimental validation, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed computational analysis and clear results.

人乳头瘤病毒 病毒 疫苗 在高风险和未充分研究的群体中的免疫原性:女性性工作者。

Immunogenicity of Human Papillomavirus Virus Vaccine in a High-risk and Understudied Group: Female Sex Workers.
作者： Nguyen Van Trang, Le Anh Tuan, Chu Thi Ngoc Mai, Le Thi Khanh Ly, Tran Hoang My Lien, Nguyen Thanh Chung, Dao Thi Thanh Huyen, Nguyen Thi To Uyen, Nham Thi Tuyen Thanh, Tran Thi Hong Nhung
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
女性性工作者 (FSW) 有多个性伴侣,感染 人乳头瘤病毒 病毒 (HPV) 的风险较高。他们可能是 HPV 疫苗接种 的目标群体,但由于可能接触该病毒,他们对 HPV 疫苗 的预期 免疫应答 尚不清楚。
设计与对象
We conducted a pilot study (NCT04590521) in Hai Phong, Vietnam to evaluate HPV vaccine immunogenicity among FSW aged 18-26 years. A total of 63 FSW with a median age of 23.4 years (IQR, 21.6-25.4 years) were recruited.
核心方法
所有参与者均接受了四价 Gardasil 疫苗 (4vHPV) 的标准 3 剂方案(0、2 和 6 个月)。在基线以及首次给药后 2、3、6 和 7 个月时收集血样,以确定对 HPV16 和 HPV18 以及相关 HPV52 和 HPV58 的中和抗体 (nAb) 反应。
主要结果
基线时任何 高危型HPV 类型和 HPV16/18 感染的 患病率 分别为 28.6% (18/63) 和 9.5% (6/63)。第一次接种后,90% 和 55% 的个体分别呈 HPV16 和 HPV18 血清阳性。到第 7 个月,所有参与者的 HPV16 和 HPV18 血清均为阳性。
解释与创新
三剂 4vHPV 在 FSW 中引发了针对 HPV16 和 HPV18 的强效 nAb。在 2 剂和 3 剂接种后,与 HPV-DNA 阳性参与者相比,基线时 HPV-DNA 阴性参与者的 nAb 反应更高(HPV16,几何平均比 [GMR] 2.97 和 1.63,P < .05;HPV18,GMR 2.83 和 1.49,P = .06)。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV 疫苗接种 可能对感染 HPV 风险较高的 FSW 有效。这可以为该高风险群体制定有针对性的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来评估 HPV 疫苗接种 在 FSW 中的有效性。该研究是一项试点研究,样本量有限,可能会影响结果的普遍性。

Immunogenicity of Human Papillomavirus Virus Vaccine in a High-risk and Understudied Group: Female Sex Workers.

Authors: Nguyen Van Trang, Le Anh Tuan, Chu Thi Ngoc Mai, Le Thi Khanh Ly, Tran Hoang My Lien, Nguyen Thanh Chung, Dao Thi Thanh Huyen, Nguyen Thi To Uyen, Nham Thi Tuyen Thanh, Tran Thi Hong Nhung
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Female sex workers (FSW) have multiple sexual partners and are at higher risk of human papillomavirus virus (HPV) infection. They could be a target group for HPV vaccination, but their expected immune response to the HPV vaccine is unclear due to potential exposure to the virus. METHODS: We conducted a pilot study (NCT04590521) in Hai Phong, Vietnam to evaluate HPV vaccine immunogenicity among FSW aged 18-26 years. All participants received the standard 3-dose schedule (0, 2, and 6 months) of the quadrivalent Gardasil vaccine (4vHPV). Cervical swabs were collected at time of vaccination to detect high-risk HPV DNA and cervical abnormalities. Blood samples were collected at baseline and at 2, 3, 6, and 7 months after the first dose to determine neutralizing antibody (nAb) responses to HPV16 and HPV18, as well as related HPV52 and HPV58. RESULTS: A total of 63 FSW with a median age of 23.4 years (IQR, 21.6-25.4 years) were recruited. Prevalence of any high-risk HPV type and HPV16/18 infection at baseline was 28.6% (18/63) and 9.5% (6/63), respectively. Following the first dose, 90% and 55% of individuals were seropositive for HPV16 and HPV18, respectively. By month 7, all participants were seropositive to both HPV16 and HPV18. Higher nAb responses were observed among HPV-DNA-negative participants at baseline compared with HPV-DNA-positive participants after 2 and 3 doses (HPV16, geometric mean ratio [GMR] 2.97 and 1.63, P < .05; HPV18, GMR 2.83 and 1.49, P = .06). CONCLUSIONS: Three doses of 4vHPV elicited robust nAb to HPV16 and HPV18 among FSW. Further studies are needed to evaluate the effectiveness of HPV vaccination in FSW. Clinical Trials Registration.NCT04590521.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Female sex workers (FSW) have multiple sexual partners and are at higher risk of human papillomavirus virus (HPV) infection. They could be a target group for HPV vaccination, but their expected immune response to the HPV vaccine is unclear due to potential exposure to the virus.
Design and population
We conducted a pilot study (NCT04590521) in Hai Phong, Vietnam to evaluate HPV vaccine immunogenicity among FSW aged 18-26 years. A total of 63 FSW with a median age of 23.4 years (IQR, 21.6-25.4 years) were recruited.
Core methods
All participants received the standard 3-dose schedule (0, 2, and 6 months) of the quadrivalent Gardasil vaccine (4vHPV). Blood samples were collected at baseline and at 2, 3, 6, and 7 months after the first dose to determine neutralizing antibody (nAb) responses to HPV16 and HPV18, as well as related HPV52 and HPV58.
Main results
Prevalence of any high-risk HPV type and HPV16/18 infection at baseline was 28.6% (18/63) and 9.5% (6/63), respectively. Following the first dose, 90% and 55% of individuals were seropositive for HPV16 and HPV18, respectively. By month 7, all participants were seropositive to both HPV16 and HPV18.
Interpretation and novelty
Three doses of 4vHPV elicited robust nAb to HPV16 and HPV18 among FSW. Higher nAb responses were observed among HPV-DNA-negative participants at baseline compared with HPV-DNA-positive participants after 2 and 3 doses (HPV16, geometric mean ratio [GMR] 2.97 and 1.63, P < .05; HPV18, GMR 2.83 and 1.49, P = .06).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV vaccination may be effective in FSW, who are at higher risk of HPV infection. This could inform targeted vaccination strategies for this high-risk group.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further studies are needed to evaluate the effectiveness of HPV vaccination in FSW. The study was a pilot study with a limited sample size, which may affect the generalisability of the results.

高危型HPV 检测的比色 LAMP 和 PCR 比较:对 宫颈癌 筛查的影响

Comparison of Colorimetric LAMP and PCR for High-risk HPV Detection: Implications for Cervical Cancer Screening
作者： Hee Yeun Yoon, Hyo Sung Jeon, Min Ju Kim, Jong Mi Kim, Yoo Young Lee, Gun Oh Chong
摘要中文翻译目的:宫颈癌 主要由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起,通常使用基于聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 的检测方法进行检测。然而,这些方法非常耗时并且需要专门的仪器。本研究评估了环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 检测作为 HPV 检测的快速替代方法与传统 PCR 检测相比的诊断性能。方法:本研究纳入了 933 名年龄≥19 岁的非孕妇,她们接受了 HPV 检测以进行 宫颈癌 筛查或随访。HPV 样本包括 14 种高危类型(16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68)。宫颈拭子样本经过核酸提取,然后使用类型特异性引物进行 LAMP 扩增。反应在 63 °C 下进行 35 分钟,并通过比色法解释结果(蓝色表示阳性,紫色表示阴性)。结果:与 PCR 相比,LAMP 检测对 HPV 16 型和 18 型的敏感性为 97.8%(95% CI:94.4-100.0),对其余高危型的敏感性为 100.0%(95% CI:98.8-100.0)。所有类型的特异性均为 100.0%(95% CI:98.8–100.0)。总体诊断准确率为 99.5%(95% CI:99.0–100.0)。当对所有 14 种 HPV 类型进行汇总分析时,临床敏感性和特异性分别为 99.4% 和 100.0%。结论:LAMP 检测提供了一种快速、简单且准确的 HPV 检测方法,可作为有用的护理点筛查工具,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 主要由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染引起,通常使用基于聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 的检测进行检测。然而,这些方法非常耗时并且需要专门的仪器。
设计与对象
本研究评估了环介导等温扩增 (LAMP) 检测作为 HPV 检测的快速替代方法与传统 PCR 检测相比的诊断性能。这项研究纳入了 933 名年龄≥19 岁的非孕妇,她们接受了 HPV 检测以进行 宫颈癌 筛查或随访。
核心方法
HPV 样本包括 14 种高危类型(16、18、31、33、35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66 和 68)。宫颈拭子样本经过核酸提取,然后使用类型特异性引物进行 LAMP 扩增。反应在 63 °C 下进行 35 分钟,并通过比色法解释结果(蓝色表示阳性,紫色表示阴性)。
主要结果

解释与创新
LAMP 检测提供了一种快速、简单且准确的 HPV 检测方法,可作为有用的护理点筛查工具,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LAMP 检测可能是 宫颈癌 筛查的一个有价值的工具,特别是在基于 PCR 的方法不易获得的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一个中心进行的,可能无法推广到其他人群。证据确凿,与 PCR 相比,LAMP 检测具有较高的灵敏度和特异性。

Comparison of Colorimetric LAMP and PCR for High-risk HPV Detection: Implications for Cervical Cancer Screening

Authors: Hee Yeun Yoon, Hyo Sung Jeon, Min Ju Kim, Jong Mi Kim, Yoo Young Lee, Gun Oh Chong
Original abstract (English)
Objective: Cervical cancer, primarily caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, is commonly detected using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based assays. However, these methods are time-consuming and require specialized instrumentation. This study evaluated the diagnostic performance of the loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assay as a rapid alternative for HPV detection compared with conventional PCR testing. Methods: This study included 933 nonpregnant women aged ≥19 years who underwent HPV testing for cervical cancer screening or follow-up. The HPV panel included 14 high-risk types (16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68). Cervical swab samples underwent nucleic acid extraction followed by LAMP amplification using type-specific primers. Reactions were performed at 63 °C for 35 minutes, and results were interpreted colorimetrically (blue for positive, purple for negative). Results: Compared with PCR, the LAMP assay showed a sensitivity of 97.8% (95% CI: 94.4–100.0) for HPV types 16 and 18 and 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8–100.0) for the remaining high-risk types. Specificity across all types was 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8–100.0). The overall diagnostic accuracy was 99.5% (95% CI: 99.0–100.0). When all 14 HPV types were analyzed collectively, clinical sensitivity and specificity were 99.4% and 100.0%, respectively. Conclusions: The LAMP assay provides a rapid, simple, and accurate method for HPV detection and may serve as a useful point-of-care screening tool, particularly in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer, primarily caused by human papillomavirus (HPV) infection, is commonly detected using polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-based assays. However, these methods are time-consuming and require specialized instrumentation.
Design and population
This study evaluated the diagnostic performance of the loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assay as a rapid alternative for HPV detection compared with conventional PCR testing. This study included 933 nonpregnant women aged ≥19 years who underwent HPV testing for cervical cancer screening or follow-up.
Core methods
The HPV panel included 14 high-risk types (16, 18, 31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, and 68). Cervical swab samples underwent nucleic acid extraction followed by LAMP amplification using type-specific primers. Reactions were performed at 63 °C for 35 minutes, and results were interpreted colorimetrically (blue for positive, purple for negative).
Main results
Compared with PCR, the LAMP assay showed a sensitivity of 97.8% (95% CI: 94.4–100.0) for HPV types 16 and 18 and 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8–100.0) for the remaining high-risk types. Specificity across all types was 100.0% (95% CI: 98.8–100.0). The overall diagnostic accuracy was 99.5% (95% CI: 99.0–100.0).
Interpretation and novelty
The LAMP assay provides a rapid, simple, and accurate method for HPV detection and may serve as a useful point-of-care screening tool, particularly in resource-limited settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the LAMP assay could be a valuable tool for cervical cancer screening, especially in settings where PCR-based methods are not readily available.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single center and may not be generalizable to other populations. The evidence is strong, with high sensitivity and specificity reported for the LAMP assay compared to PCR.

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' 探讨未来人乳头瘤病毒自我采样的可接受性,以及苏格兰智力障碍人群进行宫颈癌筛查的障碍与促进因素

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.
作者： Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
摘要中文翻译背景:苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 的 宫颈癌 筛查出勤率约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比率和世界卫生组织建议的 70% 目标。HPV 自我采样可以为这一服务不足的人群提供更方便的筛查选择。本研究旨在了解患有 ID 的女性对 宫颈癌 筛查以及 HPV 自我采样的可接受性的看法和经验。方法:利用焦点小组对参与苏格兰第三部门社区团体的智障女性及其护理人员进行了一项定性研究。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,共有 13 名参与者(11 名患有智力障碍的女性,2 名护理人员)。使用反思性主题分析对数据进行分析。结果:出现了五个主题:(1) 国家卫生服务 (NHS) 沟通是痛苦的根源;(2) 医疗保健提供者和智障妇女之间的权力失衡:感觉被忽视、被忽视和失控;(3) 护理不足造成的持久后果;(4)过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;(5) 自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。结论:这项研究揭示了当前 宫颈癌 针对 ID 女性的筛查服务存在显着的系统性缺陷,同时强调了自我采样的变革潜力。成功的实施需要全面的可获取信息、加强的医疗保健专业培训和创伤知情护理方法。自我采样可以显着推进健康公平目标,同时改善这一服务不足人群的医疗保健结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
苏格兰智障女性 (ID) 参加 宫颈癌 筛查的比例约为 30%,远低于总人口 69% 的比例和世界卫生组织 70% 的目标。该研究旨在探索筛查的障碍和促进因素,并评估未来 HPV 自我采样作为这一服务不足群体更容易获得的选择的可接受性。
设计与对象
通过焦点小组对苏格兰各地的智障女性及其护理人员进行了一项定性研究。2024 年 9 月至 10 月期间举办了四次焦点小组,涉及 13 名参与者(11 名患有智障的女性和 2 名护理人员)。
核心方法
通过焦点小组收集数据,并使用反思性主题分析进行分析,以确定与筛查经验和自我抽样可接受性相关的关键主题。
主要结果
出现了五个主题:NHS 沟通是痛苦的根源;医疗保健提供者和智障女性之间的权力失衡;护理不足造成的持久后果;过去的创伤塑造了未来的回避;自我抽样作为赋权的途径。参与者表现出对自我采样的高度接受度,将其视为克服传统筛查障碍的潜在解决方案。
解释与创新
该研究强调了当前针对智障女性的 宫颈癌 筛查服务存在系统性不足,同时将自我采样确定为一种变革性的潜在解决方案。新颖之处在于集中智障女性的声音来揭示未满足的需求并提出可行的改变。
科研与公卫意义
通过解决 宫颈癌 筛查项目中的准入障碍和创伤知情护理差距,自我采样可以推进健康公平目标并改善患有智力障碍的女性的医疗保健结果。
局限与证据强度
定性设计限制了普遍性,而且样本量较小(n = 13)可能无法捕捉智力障碍女性经历的全部多样性。由于缺乏定量验证并且依赖自我报告的观点,证据强度中等。

'...but look how many lives would have been saved if this was available then' Exploring the acceptability of future Human Papillomavirus self-sampling, and the barriers and facilitators of cervical cancer screening for those with intellectual disabilities in Scotland.

Authors: Catherine B White, Lesley M McGregor
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer screening attendance among women with intellectual disabilities (ID) in Scotland is approximately 30%, significantly below the general population rate of 69% and the WHO's recommended 70% target. HPV self-sampling could provide a more accessible screening option for this underserved population. This study aimed to understand the views and experiences of women with ID regarding cervical cancer screening and the acceptability of HPV self-sampling. METHODS: A qualitative study using focus groups was conducted with women with ID who attended third-sector community groups across Scotland, along with their carers. Four focus groups were held between September and October 2024, with a total of 13 participants (11 women with ID, 2 carers). Data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Five main themes emerged: (1) National Health Service (NHS) communication as a source of distress; (2) power imbalances between health care providers and women with ID: feeling unheard, dismissed and out of control; (3) enduring consequences of inadequate care; (4) past trauma shapes future avoidance; and (5) self-sampling as a pathway to empowerment. Participants demonstrated high acceptability of self-sampling, viewing it as a potential solution to overcome barriers to traditional screening. CONCLUSION: This research reveals significant systemic inadequacies in current cervical cancer screening services for women with ID, whilst highlighting self-sampling's transformative potential. Successful implementation requires comprehensive accessible information, enhanced health care professional training and trauma-informed care approaches. Self-sampling could significantly advance health equity goals whilst improving health care outcomes for this underserved population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Women with intellectual disabilities (ID) in Scotland attend cervical cancer screening at approximately 30%, far below the general population rate of 69% and the WHO target of 70%. The study sought to explore barriers and facilitators to screening and to assess the acceptability of future HPV self-sampling as a more accessible option for this underserved group.
Design and population
A qualitative study using focus groups was conducted with women with ID and their carers across Scotland. Four focus groups were held between September and October 2024, involving 13 participants (11 women with ID and 2 carers).
Core methods
Data were collected through focus groups and analysed using reflexive thematic analysis to identify key themes related to screening experiences and self-sampling acceptability.
Main results
Five main themes emerged: NHS communication as a source of distress; power imbalances between healthcare providers and women with ID; enduring consequences of inadequate care; past trauma shaping future avoidance; and self-sampling as a pathway to empowerment. Participants demonstrated high acceptability of self-sampling, viewing it as a potential solution to overcome barriers to traditional screening.
Interpretation and novelty
The research highlights systemic inadequacies in current cervical cancer screening services for women with ID while identifying self-sampling as a transformative potential solution. The novelty lies in centring the voices of women with ID to reveal unmet needs and propose actionable changes.
Scientific and public-health significance
Self-sampling could advance health equity goals and improve healthcare outcomes for women with ID by addressing access barriers and trauma-informed care gaps in cervical cancer screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The qualitative design limits generalisability, and the small sample size (n=13) may not capture the full diversity of experiences among women with ID. Evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of quantitative validation and reliance on self-reported perspectives.
