人 宿主 因子 分泌性白细胞蛋白酶抑制剂和膜联蛋白 A2 调节非流行地区鼻咽癌中 人乳头瘤病毒 DNA 的存在,但不调节 Epstein-Barr 病毒的存在。

Human host factors Secretory leukocyte protease inhibitor and Annexin A2 modulate the presence of human papillomavirus DNA, but not Epstein-Barr virus, in nasopharyngeal carcinoma from non-endemic regions.
作者： Elgar S Quabius, Christiane M Stuhlmann-Laeisz, Zech HBAA, Johannes Schultz, Stephan Remmert, Daniel F Strüder, Christian Idel, Jonas J-H Park, Inga M C Seuthe, Markus Blaurock
摘要中文翻译背景:人类 宿主 因子分泌性白细胞蛋白酶抑制剂 (SLPI) 和膜联蛋白 A2 (AnxA2) 影响上皮细胞对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的易感性。尽管鼻咽癌 (NPC) 通常与 Epstein-Barr 病毒 (EBV) 有关,但越来越多的证据表明,来自非流行地区的一部分 NPC 含有 HPV DNA,这表明与 HPV 相关的头颈癌存在生物学重叠。因此,我们研究了这种 宿主 驱动机制是否也塑造了 NPC 生物学。方法:回顾性分析来自德国非流行地区的 44 例组织病理学确诊的鼻咽癌病例。从 FFPE 组织中提取核酸。DNA 用于基于 PCR 的 HPV 和 EBV 检测,RNA 用于 RT-PCR 检测 HPV 转录活性,RT-qPCR 用于定量 SLPI 和 AnxA2 表达。使用 Fisher 精确检验、Student t 检验和单向方差分析评估病毒状态、宿主 因子表达和临床病理学参数之间的关联。结果:EBV检出率为65.9%,HPV-DNA检出率为29.6%,全部检出HPV16-DNA;没有一个表达 HPV16-RNA。EBV 状态对 SLPI 表达没有影响,并且与较低的 AnxA2 水平相关。HPV 的存在与显着较低的 SLPI 表达和较高的 AnxA2 表达相关,表明上皮环境适合病毒进入。组合状态分析证实,宿主 因子表达与 HPV-DNA 平行,但与 EBV 状态不平行。结论:这些发现表明,在非流行地区的鼻咽癌中,SLPI 和 AnxA2 表达定义了允许 HPV-DNA 的 宿主 微环境,而 EBV 状态似乎与这些因素无关。由于没有检测到 HPV-RNA,这些结果反映了 HPV 的存在,而不是转录活跃的感染,表明这些 宿主 因子可能控制病毒进入,而不决定随后的感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 宿主 因子分泌性白细胞蛋白酶抑制剂 (SLPI) 和膜联蛋白 A2 (AnxA2) 是否调节非流行地区鼻咽癌 (NPC) 中 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的存在,尽管在非流行地区,尽管与 Epstein-Barr 病毒 (EBV) 存在经典关联,但已检测到 HPV DNA。
设计与对象
该研究使用福尔马林固定石蜡包埋 (FFPE) 组织样本回顾性分析了来自德国非流行地区的 44 例组织病理学确诊的鼻咽癌病例。
核心方法
从 FFPE 组织中提取核酸,DNA 用于 PCR 检测 HPV 和 EBV,RNA 用于 RT-PCR 检测 HPV 转录活性,RT-qPCR 用于定量 SLPI 和 AnxA2 表达。使用 Fisher 精确检验、Student t 检验和单向方差分析评估病毒状态、宿主 因子表达和临床病理学参数之间的关联。
主要结果
在病例中,EBV检出率为65.9%,HPV DNA检出率为29.6%,且所有HPV阳性病例均携带HPV16 DNA;但均未检测到HPV16 RNA表达。HPV感染与SLPI表达显著降低及AnxA2表达显著升高相关,而EBV感染状态对SLPI无影响,并与AnxA2表达水平降低相关。
解释与创新
作者解释说,SLPI 和 AnxA2 表达定义了允许非流行地区 NPC 中 HPV DNA 存在的 宿主 微环境,而 EBV 状态似乎独立于这些因素,突出了一种针对 HPV 的新型 宿主 病毒相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,宿主 因子 SLPI 和 AnxA2 可能会影响 HPV 进入 NPC 上皮细胞,可能为未来的 监测 或针对 HPV 相关癌症中这些因子的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计、样本量小和缺乏 HPV RNA 表达而受到限制,这将结论限制为 HPV 存在而不是活动性感染,需要谨慎解释和更大规模的前瞻性研究。

Human host factors Secretory leukocyte protease inhibitor and Annexin A2 modulate the presence of human papillomavirus DNA, but not Epstein-Barr virus, in nasopharyngeal carcinoma from non-endemic regions.

Authors: Elgar S Quabius, Christiane M Stuhlmann-Laeisz, Zech HBAA, Johannes Schultz, Stephan Remmert, Daniel F Strüder, Christian Idel, Jonas J-H Park, Inga M C Seuthe, Markus Blaurock
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The human host factors Secretory leukocyte protease inhibitor (SLPI) and Annexin A2 (AnxA2) influence susceptibility of epithelial cells to human papillomavirus (HPV)-infections. Although nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC) is classically associated with Epstein-Barr virus (EBV), increasing evidence indicates that a subset of NPCs from non-endemic regions harbours HPV DNA, suggesting biological overlap with HPV-associated head and neck cancers. Therefore, we studied whether this host-driven mechanism also shapes NPC-biology. METHODS: Retrospectively 44 histopathological confirmed NPC cases from non-endemic German regions were analysed. Nucleic acids were extracted from FFPE tissue. DNA was used for PCR-based detection of HPV and EBV, RNA was used for RT-PCR detection of HPV transcriptional activity and RT-qPCR for quantification of SLPI- and AnxA2-expression. Associations between viral-status, host factor expression, and clinicopathological parameters were assessed using Fisher's exact test, Student's t-test, and one way-ANOVA. RESULTS: EBV was detected in 65.9% cases, HPV-DNA in 29.6%, all harboured HPV16-DNA; none expressed HPV16-RNA. EBV-status had no effect on SLPI-expression and was associated with lower AnxA2-levels. HPV-presence was associated with significantly lower SLPI- and higher AnxA2-expression, indicating a permissive epithelial environment for viral entry. Combined-status analyses confirmed that host factor expression paralleled the HPV-DNA- but not EBV-status. CONCLUSIONS: These findings indicate that in NPC from non-endemic regions, SLPI- and AnxA2-expression defines a host microenvironment permissive for HPV-DNA, whereas EBV-status appears independent of these factors. As no HPV-RNA was detected, these results reflect HPV-presence rather than transcriptionally active infection, suggesting that these host factors may govern viral entry without determining subsequent infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether host factors Secretory leukocyte protease inhibitor (SLPI) and Annexin A2 (AnxA2) modulate human papillomavirus (HPV) presence in nasopharyngeal carcinoma (NPC) from non-endemic regions, where HPV DNA has been detected despite classical association with Epstein-Barr virus (EBV).
Design and population
The study retrospectively analyzed 44 histopathological confirmed NPC cases from non-endemic German regions using formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissue samples.
Core methods
Nucleic acids were extracted from FFPE tissue, with DNA used for PCR-based detection of HPV and EBV, RNA for RT-PCR detection of HPV transcriptional activity, and RT-qPCR for quantification of SLPI and AnxA2 expression. Associations between viral status, host factor expression, and clinicopathological parameters were assessed using Fisher's exact test, Student's t-test, and one-way ANOVA.
Main results
EBV was detected in 65.9% of cases and HPV DNA in 29.6%, with all HPV-positive cases harboring HPV16 DNA; none expressed HPV16 RNA. HPV presence was associated with significantly lower SLPI and higher AnxA2 expression, while EBV status showed no effect on SLPI and was associated with lower AnxA2 levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that SLPI and AnxA2 expression defines a host microenvironment permissive for HPV DNA presence in NPC from non-endemic regions, whereas EBV status appears independent of these factors, highlighting a novel host-virus interaction specific to HPV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that host factors SLPI and AnxA2 may influence HPV entry into epithelial cells in NPC, potentially informing future surveillance or therapeutic strategies targeting these factors in HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, small sample size, and lack of HPV RNA expression, which restricts conclusions to HPV presence rather than active infection, warranting cautious interpretation and larger prospective studies.

在 13-17 岁青少年中同时注射 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 与其他推荐的 疫苗,国家青少年免疫调查 (NIS-Teen),美国,2023 年。

Simultaneous Administration of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccine With Other Recommended Vaccines Among Adolescents Aged 13-17 years, National Immunization Survey-Teen (NIS-Teen), United States, 2023.
作者： Cassandra Pingali, David Yankey, Michael Chen, Laurie D Elam-Evans, Lauri E Markowitz, Carla L DeSisto, Ruth Stefanos, James A Singleton
摘要中文翻译目的:调查在单次就诊中接种一种或多种其他推荐疫苗的同时接种人乳头瘤病毒疫苗(HPV疫苗)的青少年比例。方法:分析2023年国家青少年免疫调查(NIS-Teen)数据。通过报告机构提供的接种史评估HPV疫苗、破伤风-白喉-无细胞百日咳疫苗(Tdap)、四价脑膜炎球菌结合疫苗(MenACWY)及流感疫苗的接种时机。结果:2023年,在13-17岁青少年中,69.5%的人在单次就诊中同时接种HPV疫苗与一种或多种其他推荐疫苗。此外,47.8%的人在单次就诊中同时接种HPV疫苗、Tdap和MenACWY。讨论:HPV疫苗通常与其他推荐疫苗在单次就诊中同时接种。这些发现表明同时接种模式存在差异,提示青少年疫苗接种时间表的灵活性允许在不同临床环境和家庭偏好下采用不同的接种方法,同时仍能遵守推荐的接种时间表。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生需要,了解青少年是否在同一次医疗就诊期间与其他常规推荐的青少年 疫苗 一起接受 HPV 疫苗,这反映了对同时接种 疫苗接种 机会的担忧。
设计与对象
该分析使用 2023 年全国青少年免疫调查 (NIS-Teen) 的数据来研究美国 13-17 岁青少年的 疫苗接种 时机。
核心方法
提供者报告的 疫苗接种 历史用于评估相对于破伤风、白喉和无细胞百日咳 疫苗 (Tdap)、四价脑膜炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (MenACWY) 和流感 疫苗 的 HPV 疫苗 接收时间。
主要结果
2023 年,69.5% 的 13-17 岁青少年在一次就诊中接受了 HPV 疫苗 和一种或多种其他推荐的 疫苗,47.8% 的青少年同时接受了 HPV 疫苗、Tdap 和 MenACWY。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,HPV 疫苗接种 通常与其他青少年 疫苗 在单次就诊中一起注射,这凸显了不同环境和家庭偏好的时间表遵守的灵活性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明同步给药是可行且广泛实践的,为 疫苗接种 计划规划提供信息,支持在不影响时间表遵守情况下优化覆盖范围的策略。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于提供者报告的调查数据,并未捕获非同步 疫苗接种 的临床结果或原因,因此需要对观察到的模式进行谨慎解释。

Simultaneous Administration of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccine With Other Recommended Vaccines Among Adolescents Aged 13-17 years, National Immunization Survey-Teen (NIS-Teen), United States, 2023.

Authors: Cassandra Pingali, David Yankey, Michael Chen, Laurie D Elam-Evans, Lauri E Markowitz, Carla L DeSisto, Ruth Stefanos, James A Singleton
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: To investigate the percent of adolescents who receive human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine with one or more other vaccines recommended for adolescents in a single medical visit. METHODS: Data from the 2023 National Immunization Survey-Teen were analyzed. Timing of receipt of HPV vaccine, tetanus, diphtheria, and acellular pertussis vaccine (Tdap), quadrivalent meningococcal conjugate vaccine (MenACWY), and influenza vaccine was assessed using provider-reported vaccination histories. RESULTS: In 2023, among adolescents aged 13-17 years, 69.5% received HPV vaccine with one or more other vaccines recommended for adolescents in a single medical visit. In addition, 47.8% received specifically HPV vaccine, Tdap, and MenACWY together in a single medical visit. DISCUSSION: The HPV vaccine is commonly given with other vaccines recommended for adolescents in a single medical visit. These findings demonstrate variation in simultaneous vaccination patterns, suggesting that flexibility in the recommended adolescent vaccination schedule allows for different approaches to vaccination across clinical settings and family preferences while maintaining adherence to the recommended schedule.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to understand whether adolescents receive the HPV vaccine alongside other routinely recommended adolescent vaccines during the same medical visit, reflecting concerns about missed opportunities for simultaneous vaccination.
Design and population
The analysis used data from the 2023 National Immunization Survey-Teen (NIS-Teen) to examine vaccination timing among adolescents aged 13–17 years in the United States.
Core methods
Provider-reported vaccination histories were used to assess the timing of HPV vaccine receipt relative to tetanus, diphtheria, and acellular pertussis vaccine (Tdap), quadrivalent meningococcal conjugate vaccine (MenACWY), and influenza vaccine.
Main results
In 2023, 69.5% of adolescents aged 13–17 years received HPV vaccine with one or more other recommended vaccines in a single medical visit, and 47.8% received HPV vaccine, Tdap, and MenACWY together.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that HPV vaccination is commonly administered with other adolescent vaccines in a single visit, highlighting flexibility in schedule adherence across settings and family preferences.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform vaccination program planning by showing that simultaneous administration is feasible and widely practiced, supporting strategies to optimize coverage without compromising schedule adherence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to provider-reported survey data and does not capture clinical outcomes or reasons for non-simultaneous vaccination, warranting cautious interpretation of the observed patterns.

沙特阿拉伯麦加普通人群对 人乳头瘤病毒 及其与口咽癌关联的知识和认识:一项横断面研究

Knowledge and awareness regarding human papillomavirus and its association with oropharyngeal cancer among the general population in Makkah, Saudi Arabia: a cross-sectional study
作者： Khulud A. Alhazmi, Sulaiman Bani AbdelRahman, Hala Altarawneh, Karem Ibrahem, Bandar Hasan Saleh, Abdulaziz Alsaedi, Ahmad M. Alharbi, Radi Alsafi, Waiel S. Halabi, Mohammed Mufrrih
摘要中文翻译背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 代表全球最流行的性传播感染,并被认为是肛门生殖器癌、宫颈癌和口咽癌 (OPC) 的主要病原体。值得注意的是,HPV是OPC的一个重要危险因素,其发病率近年来在全球范围内不断上升。本研究旨在评估沙特阿拉伯麦加居民对 HPV 及其与 OPC 的关联的认识和知识水平。材料和方法 对沙特阿拉伯麦加的所有成年人(18 岁及以上,n = 420)进行了横断面调查。使用结构化和自填式在线问卷收集数据。该工具包括社会人口统计数据以及有关 HPV 感染及其与 OPC 关联的认识和知识的问题。所有变量均使用描述性统计,并且存在多项选择题,答案为“是”、“否”和“不知道”。结果 参与的成年人共有 420 名。大约一半的受访者 (51.7%) 以前听说过 HPV,只有 21.4% 曾参加过有关 HPV 和 OPC 的宣传活动。超过一半 (53.6%) 的人正确识别 HPV 不是细菌感染,而很少有人认为 HPV 具有传染性 (43.1%),并且可以通过性接触传播 (44.3%)。对 HPV 预防的了解各不相同;67.9%的人认识到HPV 疫苗接种可以降低HPV感染的风险,但只有42.4%的人知道疫苗可以帮助预防HPV感染。此外,人们对 OPC 症状和危险因素的认识也不一致,最常见的是口腔病变和颈部肿块,最常见的危险因素是吸烟和饮酒。大多数参与者 (88.1%) 表示对 HPV 和 OPC 的了解不足,并表示需要更多信息,社交媒体被认为是提高公众意识的首选策略 (38.1%)。结论 研究结果表明,麦加成年人对 HPV、其与 OPC 的关系以及 HPV 预防方面的公众知识和认识存在重大差距。需要有针对性的教育干预和公众意识活动,以提高对 HPV 传播、疫苗接种 和 OPC 预防的了解。鉴于大多数参与者表示需要更多信息,数字平台,特别是社交媒体,可能为提供公共卫生教育提供有效的方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
发病率 (HPV) 是肛门生殖器癌、宫颈癌和口咽癌的主要原因,全球 HPV 相关口咽癌 (OPC) 的 发病率 病例不断增加。该研究旨在评估沙特阿拉伯麦加成年人对 HPV 及其与 OPC 的联系的公众认识和知识差距。
设计与对象
对沙特阿拉伯麦加 18 岁及以上成年人进行了一项横断面调查,样本量为 420 名参与者。使用结构化、自我管理的在线调查问卷收集数据。
核心方法
该调查工具捕获了社会人口统计数据,并评估了人们对 HPV 感染及其与 OPC 关联的认识和知识。应用描述性统计,多项选择题使用“是”、“否”和“不知道”回答选项。
主要结果
在420名参与者中,51.7%听说过人乳头瘤病毒(HPV),21.4%接触过HPV或口咽癌(OPC)的宣传活动,88.1%报告对HPV和口咽癌的知识不足。仅43.1%的人认识到HPV具有传染性,44.3%知道HPV可通过性传播,42.4%了解HPV疫苗的可用性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明麦加成年人在 HPV 传播、预防和 OPC 风险方面存在巨大的知识差距。新颖之处在于使用结构化调查来量化沙特阿拉伯人口中的这些差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要开展有针对性的教育干预和公众宣传活动,以提高对麦加 HPV 传播、疫苗接种 和 OPC 预防的了解。社交媒体被认为是提供此类教育的首选平台。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告的在线调查问卷的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能引入反应偏差。由于在线调查中的非随机抽样和潜在的选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Knowledge and awareness regarding human papillomavirus and its association with oropharyngeal cancer among the general population in Makkah, Saudi Arabia: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Khulud A. Alhazmi, Sulaiman Bani AbdelRahman, Hala Altarawneh, Karem Ibrahem, Bandar Hasan Saleh, Abdulaziz Alsaedi, Ahmad M. Alharbi, Radi Alsafi, Waiel S. Halabi, Mohammed Mufrrih
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) represents the most prevalent sexually transmitted infection globally and is recognized as a principal etiological agent of anogenital, cervical and oropharyngeal cancers (OPC). Notably, HPV serves as a significant risk factor for OPC, the incidence of which has been rising worldwide in recent years. This study was conducted to assess the level of awareness and knowledge regarding HPV and its association with OPC among residents of Makkah, Saudi Arabia. Materials and methods All adults (aged 18 years and over, n = 420) were surveyed cross-sectionally in Makkah, Saudi Arabia. Data were collected using structured and self-administered questionnaires online. The instrument included sociodemographic data, and questions about awareness and knowledge of HPV infection and its association with OPC. Descriptive statistics were used for all the variables and there were multiple choice questions to which the answers were ‘Yes’, ‘No’ and ‘Do not know’. Results The adults participated were a total of 420. About half of the respondents (51.7%) were previously heard of HPV and only 21.4% had encountered an awareness campaign about HPV and OPC. Over half (53.6%) correctly identified HPV as not a bacterial infection, while fewer recognized HPV as being contagious (43.1%) and that it could be transmitted through sexual contact (44.3%). Knowledge of HPV prevention was variable; 67.9% recognized that HPV vaccination can reduce the risk of HPV infection, but only 42.4% were aware that vaccines are available to help prevent HPV infection. Also, awareness of the symptoms of OPC and risk factors were inconsistent, with the most common being oral lesions and neck lump, and the most identified risk factors were smoking and alcohol consumption. Most participants (88.1%) reported insufficient knowledge about HPV and OPC and expressed a need for additional information, with social media identified as the preferred strategy for increasing public awareness (38.1%). Conclusion The findings demonstrate important gaps in public knowledge and awareness of HPV, its association with OPC, and HPV prevention among adults in Makkah. Targeted educational interventions and public awareness campaigns are needed to improve understanding of HPV transmission, vaccination, and OPC prevention. Given that most participants expressed a need for additional information, digital platforms, particularly social media, may provide an effective approach for delivering public health education.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a leading cause of anogenital, cervical, and oropharyngeal cancers, with rising incidence of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer (OPC) globally. The study aimed to assess public awareness and knowledge gaps regarding HPV and its link to OPC among adults in Makkah, Saudi Arabia.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among adults aged 18 years and older in Makkah, Saudi Arabia, with a sample size of 420 participants. Data were collected using structured, self-administered online questionnaires.
Core methods
The survey instrument captured sociodemographic data and assessed awareness and knowledge of HPV infection and its association with OPC. Descriptive statistics were applied, and multiple-choice questions used 'Yes', 'No', and 'Do not know' response options.
Main results
Among 420 participants, 51.7% had heard of HPV, 21.4% had encountered an HPV or OPC awareness campaign, and 88.1% reported insufficient knowledge about HPV and OPC. Only 43.1% recognized HPV as contagious, 44.3% knew it could be sexually transmitted, and 42.4% were aware of HPV vaccine availability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating substantial knowledge gaps about HPV transmission, prevention, and OPC risk among adults in Makkah. The novelty lies in quantifying these gaps within a Saudi Arabian population using a structured survey.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted educational interventions and public awareness campaigns to improve understanding of HPV transmission, vaccination, and OPC prevention in Makkah. Social media is identified as a preferred platform for delivering such education.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported online questionnaires limit causal inference and may introduce response bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the non-random sampling and potential selection bias in an online survey.

免疫缺失作为 人乳头瘤病毒 相关 宫颈癌 中治疗意图 治疗 后延迟复发的拟议框架

Immune Absence as a Proposed Framework for Delayed Relapse After Curative-Intent Treatment in Human Papillomavirus-Associated Cervical Cancer
作者： Norihito Kamo, Shu Soeda, Tsuyoshi Honda, Keiya Fujimori
摘要中文翻译在 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的 人乳头瘤病毒 中,治疗目的 治疗 后的延迟复发仍然难以解释和预测,包括在循环肿瘤 DNA (ctDNA) 或循环 HPV DNA 检测不到的持续缓解期之后。以肿瘤为中心的 监测 的这种局限性需要评估免疫参数以及肿瘤衍生的生物标志物。我们提出“免疫缺失”作为一个假设生成的操作框架,指的是在抗原暴露可能有限或间歇性的微小残留疾病状态下获得的连续外周血样本中预先指定的肿瘤反应性T细胞受体(TCR)克隆型的持续减少或不持续。我们假设这种纵向模式可能先于部分患者复发的分子或临床证据,并与既定机制共存,例如肿瘤 进化、免疫逃逸、T 细胞功能障碍和治疗耐药。将连续 ctDNA 或循环 HPV DNA 测量与肿瘤反应性 TCR 克隆型动态相结合,可以提供有关残余肿瘤负荷和循环肿瘤反应性 T 细胞反应持续性的补充信息。未检测到外周血 TCR 并不表明抗肿瘤免疫力完全丧失,可能反映区室化免疫、克隆替代、抗原 进化 或检测限制。在临床使用之前需要进行前瞻性纵向研究来确定该框架的预后价值。

设计与对象
在临床使用之前需要进行前瞻性纵向研究来确定该框架的预后价值。
核心方法
未检测到外周血 TCR 并不表明抗肿瘤免疫力完全丧失,可能反映区室化免疫、克隆替代、抗原 进化 或检测限制。
主要结果
在 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关的 人乳头瘤病毒 中,治疗目的 治疗 后的延迟复发仍然难以解释和预测,包括在循环肿瘤 DNA (ctDNA) 或循环 HPV DNA 检测不到的持续缓解期之后。
解释与创新

科研与公卫意义
以肿瘤为中心的 监测 的这种局限性需要评估免疫参数以及肿瘤衍生的生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Immune Absence as a Proposed Framework for Delayed Relapse After Curative-Intent Treatment in Human Papillomavirus-Associated Cervical Cancer

Authors: Norihito Kamo, Shu Soeda, Tsuyoshi Honda, Keiya Fujimori
Original abstract (English)
Delayed relapse after curative-intent treatment remains difficult to explain and predict in human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated cervical cancer, including after periods of sustained remission with undetectable circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) or circulating HPV DNA. This limitation of tumor-centered surveillance demands the evaluation of immune parameters alongside tumor-derived biomarkers. We propose “immune absence” as a hypothesis-generating, operational framework referring to the sustained reduction or non-persistence of pre-specified tumor-reactive T-cell receptor (TCR) clonotypes in serial peripheral blood samples, obtained during minimal residual disease states in which antigen exposure may be limited or intermittent. We hypothesize that this longitudinal pattern may precede molecular or clinical evidence of relapse in a subset of patients and coexist with established mechanisms, such as tumor evolution, immune escape, T-cell dysfunction, and therapeutic resistance. Integrating serial ctDNA or circulating HPV DNA measurements with tumor-reactive TCR clonotype dynamics may provide complementary information on residual tumor burden and the persistence of circulating tumor-reactive T-cell responses. Peripheral blood TCR non-detection does not establish complete loss of anti-tumor immunity and may reflect compartmentalized immunity, clonal replacement, antigenic evolution, or assay limitations. Prospective longitudinal studies are required to establish the prognostic value of this framework before clinical use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Integrating serial ctDNA or circulating HPV DNA measurements with tumor-reactive TCR clonotype dynamics may provide complementary information on residual tumor burden and the persistence of circulating tumor-reactive T-cell responses.
Design and population
Prospective longitudinal studies are required to establish the prognostic value of this framework before clinical use.
Core methods
Peripheral blood TCR non-detection does not establish complete loss of anti-tumor immunity and may reflect compartmentalized immunity, clonal replacement, antigenic evolution, or assay limitations.
Main results
Delayed relapse after curative-intent treatment remains difficult to explain and predict in human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated cervical cancer, including after periods of sustained remission with undetectable circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA) or circulating HPV DNA.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

人外周血白细胞内化 HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合 VLP 的实验分析

Experimental Analysis of HPV16 L1/L2 Chimeric VLP Internalization by Human Peripheral Blood Leukocytes
作者： Aurora Marques Cianciarullo, Dirce Sakauchi, Érica Akemi Kavati Sasaki, Tania Matiko Hosoda, Primavera BORELLI, Willy Beçak, Erica Akemi Kavati Sasaki
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 (HPV16) 是宫颈癌和其他上皮癌的主要病因,但 宿主 与病原体相互作用的机制仍不完全清楚。在这项研究中,我们研究了人外周血白细胞对由 HEK 293-F 细胞悬浮产生的工程化 HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的反应。这些 VLP 旨在模仿天然病毒结构,同时结合增强稳定性和免疫原性的嵌合特征。通过实验测定,我们表征了白细胞参与,主要涉及白细胞表型、VLP 内化、共聚焦共定位和内吞途径分析。我们证明重组L1/L2蛋白组装成结构化VLP能够与单核细胞(包括淋巴细胞和单核细胞)相互作用,但不能与多形核细胞(例如中性粒细胞、嗜酸性粒细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞)相互作用。摄取通过 CD71 转铁蛋白受体介导的途径发生,此外还分析了其他内吞途径,如使用氯丙嗪、rCTB、菲律宾素、制霉菌素、利克明和叠氮化钠的阻断测定所证实的。共聚焦共定位和内吞途径分析进一步支持受体介导的摄取。这些发现表明,HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合 VLP 通过 CD71 相关途径和其他内吞途径与人外周血单核细胞相互作用并被人外周血单核细胞内化。该研究为 HPV16 VLP-白细胞相互作用提供了新的见解,并有助于更好地了解 VLP 摄取所涉及的细胞机制,这可能与 HPV 生物学和基于 VLP 的 疫苗 开发的未来研究相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV16 是宫颈癌和其他上皮癌的主要病因,但 宿主 与病原体相互作用的机制仍不完全清楚。本研究旨在研究人外周血白细胞对工程化 HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的反应。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过实验测定来表征白细胞与 HEK 293-F 细胞悬浮液中产生的 HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合 VLP 的结合。这些 VLP 旨在模仿天然病毒结构,同时结合增强稳定性和免疫原性的嵌合特征。该研究重点关注人外周血单核细胞,包括淋巴细胞和单核细胞,但不包括多形核细胞。
核心方法
该研究使用白细胞表型分析、VLP 内化、共聚焦共定位和内吞途径分析来表征相互作用。使用氯丙嗪、rCTB、菲律宾素、制霉菌素、利克明和叠氮化钠通过阻断测定评估摄取,以确认 CD71 转铁蛋白受体介导的和其他内吞途径。
主要结果
组装成结构化 VLP 的重组 L1/L2 蛋白可与单核细胞(包括淋巴细胞和单核细胞)相互作用,但不与多形核细胞(如中性粒细胞、嗜酸性粒细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞)相互作用。摄取通过 CD71 转铁蛋白受体介导的途径以及其他内吞途径发生。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV16 L1/L2 嵌合 VLP 通过 CD71 相关途径和其他内吞途径与人外周血单核细胞相互作用并被其内化。这为 HPV16 VLP-白细胞相互作用提供了新的见解,并有助于理解 VLP 摄取所涉及的细胞机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来有关 HPV 生物学和基于 VLP 的 疫苗 开发的研究提供信息,特别是了解这些颗粒如何与免疫细胞相互作用并被免疫细胞吸收。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于实验测定,不包括临床或流行病学数据。证据是中等的,因为它基于体外分析,并不延伸到体内或人类临床结果。

Experimental Analysis of HPV16 L1/L2 Chimeric VLP Internalization by Human Peripheral Blood Leukocytes

Authors: Aurora Marques Cianciarullo, Dirce Sakauchi, Érica Akemi Kavati Sasaki, Tania Matiko Hosoda, Primavera BORELLI, Willy Beçak, Erica Akemi Kavati Sasaki
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16) is a major etiological agent of cervical and other epithelial cancers, yet the mechanisms underlying host–pathogen interactions remain incompletely understood. In this study, we investigated the responses of human peripheral blood leukocytes to engineered HPV16 L1/L2 chimeric virus-like particles (VLPs), produced in suspension by HEK 293-F cells. These VLPs were designed to mimic native viral structures while incorporating chimeric features that enhance stability and immunogenicity. Through experimental assays, we characterized leukocyte engagement, primarily involving leukocyte phenotyping, VLP internalization, confocal colocalization, and endocytic pathway analyses. We demonstrated that recombinant L1/L2 proteins assembled into structured VLPs capable of interacting with mononuclear cells, including lymphocytes and monocytes, but not with polymorphonuclear cells, such as neutrophils, eosinophils and basophils. Uptake occurred via the CD71 transferrin receptor-mediated pathway, in addition to other endocytic routes analyzed, as confirmed by blockage assays using chlorpromazine, rCTB, filipin, nystatin, liquemine, and sodium azide. Confocal colocalization and endocytic pathway analyses further supported receptor-mediated uptake. These findings demonstrate that HPV16 L1/L2 chimeric VLPs interact with and are internalized by human peripheral blood mononuclear cells through CD71-associated and other endocytic pathways. The study provides new insights into HPV16 VLP–leukocyte interactions and contributes to a better understanding of the cellular mechanisms involved in VLP uptake, which may be relevant for future studies on HPV biology and VLP-based vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV16 is a major etiological agent of cervical and other epithelial cancers, yet the mechanisms underlying host–pathogen interactions remain incompletely understood. This study aimed to investigate the responses of human peripheral blood leukocytes to engineered HPV16 L1/L2 chimeric virus-like particles (VLPs).
Design and population
The study involved experimental assays to characterize leukocyte engagement with HPV16 L1/L2 chimeric VLPs, produced in suspension by HEK 293-F cells. These VLPs were designed to mimic native viral structures while incorporating chimeric features that enhance stability and immunogenicity. The study focused on human peripheral blood mononuclear cells, including lymphocytes and monocytes, but not polymorphonuclear cells.
Core methods
The study used leukocyte phenotyping, VLP internalization, confocal colocalization, and endocytic pathway analyses to characterize interactions. Uptake was assessed via blockage assays using chlorpromazine, rCTB, filipin, nystatin, liquemine, and sodium azide to confirm CD71 transferrin receptor-mediated and other endocytic pathways.
Main results
Recombinant L1/L2 proteins assembled into structured VLPs were shown to interact with mononuclear cells, including lymphocytes and monocytes, but not with polymorphonuclear cells such as neutrophils, eosinophils, and basophils. Uptake occurred via the CD71 transferrin receptor-mediated pathway, as well as other endocytic routes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that HPV16 L1/L2 chimeric VLPs interact with and are internalized by human peripheral blood mononuclear cells through CD71-associated and other endocytic pathways. This provides new insights into HPV16 VLP–leukocyte interactions and contributes to understanding the cellular mechanisms involved in VLP uptake.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform future studies on HPV biology and VLP-based vaccine development, particularly in understanding how these particles interact with and are taken up by immune cells.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to experimental assays and does not include clinical or epidemiological data. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on in vitro analyses and does not extend to in vivo or human clinical outcomes.

意大利青少年中关于性传播感染、人乳头瘤病毒 和 HPV 相关口咽癌的知识、态度和实践 (KAP):一项横断面研究

Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices (KAP) Regarding Sexually Transmitted Infections, Human Papillomavirus, and HPV-Related Oropharyngeal Cancer Among Italian Adolescents: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Gennaro Musella, Noemi Giorgio, Alejandro I. Lorenzo-Pouso, Lorenzo Lo Muzio, Stefania Cantore, Bojan Poposki, Carlos Gabriel Morales Arenas, Andrea Ballini
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 目前是大多数口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的罪魁祸首,这是一种与牙科行业直接相关的 HPV 相关癌症;口腔保健提供者可能有能力进行常规口腔检查、识别可疑体征、适当转诊并对患者进行有关 HPV 相关口咽癌和 HPV 疫苗接种 的教育。本研究评估了意大利普利亚中学生对性传播感染 (STI)、HPV、HIV/AIDS 以及 HPV 相关口腔和口咽癌的知识、态度和做法,特别关注牙科环境。方法:福贾大学在 2025/2026 学年结束时对来自三所中学的 1224 名学生进行了一项横断面研究,使用了包含 108 项的自填问卷。计算了 63 项综合知识得分;“不知道”的答案被评分为不正确,并且先验的充分知识定义为 ≥60%(布卢姆截止值)。所需的最小样本 (737) 是使用针对整群抽样设计效果进行调整的 Cochran 公式得出的。结果:总体知识水平较低(平均 28.5%,SD 18.2);只有 5% 达到 ≥60% 阈值(布卢姆截止值)。只有 14.8% 的学生知道 HPV 可导致口咽癌,这是所有评估的致癌结果中最低的数字,并且男孩 (11.2%) 显着低于女孩 (17.3%;p = 0.003),尽管男孩 OPSCC 风险较高。知识与女性性别、年龄和性经历独立相关。尽管知识匮乏,但 61.2% 的学生希望接受 HPV 疫苗。结论:看牙医可能是弥补口腔癌预防知识差距的一个未被充分利用的机会;将口腔保健专业人员纳入学校和临床 HPV 预防策略可能对公共卫生产生影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 越来越多地与口咽鳞状细胞癌(一种与牙科诊所相关的疾病)相关,但青少年对 HPV 及其致癌风险的了解仍然存在差距。该研究旨在评估意大利中学生对性传播感染、HPV 和 HPV 相关口咽癌的知识、态度和实践。
设计与对象
2025/2026 学年结束时,我们对意大利普利亚的 1,224 名中学生进行了一项横断面研究,使用了包含 108 项的自填问卷。
核心方法
计算 63 项综合知识得分,“不知道”答案得分为不正确,充分知识定义为 ≥60%(布卢姆截止值)。所需的最小样本量为 737 个,是使用针对整群抽样设计效果进行调整的 Cochran 公式计算得出的。
主要结果
总体知识水平较低(平均 28.5%,SD 18.2),只有 5% 的人达到 ≥60% 的阈值。只有 14.8% 的学生知道 HPV 可导致口咽癌,男孩 (11.2%) 的知晓率显着低于女孩 (17.3%;p = 0.003)。知识与女性性别、年龄和性经历独立相关。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明人们对 HPV 及其口咽癌风险存在严重的知识差距,特别是在面临较高疾病风险的男孩中。他们强调了牙科专业人员通过教育和预防策略来解决这一差距的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,基于学校和临床的 HPV 预防策略可以受益于口腔健康专业人员的整合,以提高青少年的认识和 疫苗接种 的吸收。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且对自我报告数据的依赖可能会引入偏差。由于样本量较大,证据强度中等,但受到仅限摘要范围和缺乏详细统计报告的限制。

Knowledge, Attitudes, and Practices (KAP) Regarding Sexually Transmitted Infections, Human Papillomavirus, and HPV-Related Oropharyngeal Cancer Among Italian Adolescents: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Gennaro Musella, Noemi Giorgio, Alejandro I. Lorenzo-Pouso, Lorenzo Lo Muzio, Stefania Cantore, Bojan Poposki, Carlos Gabriel Morales Arenas, Andrea Ballini
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is now responsible for the majority of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinomas (OPSCCs), an HPV-related cancer of direct relevance to the dental profession; oral-health providers may be well-positioned to perform routine oral examination, recognize suspicious signs, refer appropriately, and educate patients about HPV-related oropharyngeal cancer and HPV vaccination. This study assessed knowledge, attitudes and practices regarding sexually transmitted infections (STIs), HPV, HIV/AIDS and HPV-related oral and oropharyngeal cancer among secondary-school students in Apulia, Italy, with particular attention to the dental setting. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted by the University of Foggia among 1224 students from three secondary schools at the end of the 2025/2026 school year) using a self-administered 108-item questionnaire. A 63-item composite knowledge score was computed; “don’t know” answers were scored as incorrect and adequate knowledge defined a priori as ≥60% (Bloom’s cut-off). The minimum required sample (737) was derived using Cochran’s formula adjusted for a cluster-sampling design effect. Results: Overall knowledge was low (mean 28.5%, SD 18.2); only 5% reached the ≥60% threshold (Bloom’s cut-off). Only 14.8% of students were aware that HPV can cause oropharyngeal cancer, the lowest figure among all oncogenic outcomes assessed and significantly lower in boys (11.2%) than girls (17.3%; p = 0.003), despite boys carrying the higher OPSCC risk. Knowledge was independently associated with female sex, age and sexual experience. Despite poor knowledge, 61.2% of students would like to receive the HPV vaccine. Conclusions: The dental visit may represent an under-used opportunity to address this oral-cancer-prevention knowledge gap; the integration of oral-health professionals into school-based and clinical HPV-prevention strategies is a plausible public-health implication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV is increasingly implicated in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinomas, a disease relevant to dental practice, yet gaps persist in adolescents' knowledge about HPV and its oncogenic risks. The study aimed to evaluate knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding STIs, HPV, and HPV-related oropharyngeal cancer among Italian secondary-school students.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 1,224 secondary-school students in Apulia, Italy, at the end of the 2025/2026 school year using a self-administered 108-item questionnaire.
Core methods
A 63-item composite knowledge score was computed with “don’t know” answers scored as incorrect and adequate knowledge defined as ≥60% (Bloom’s cut-off). The minimum required sample size of 737 was calculated using Cochran’s formula adjusted for a cluster-sampling design effect.
Main results
Overall knowledge was low (mean 28.5%, SD 18.2), with only 5% reaching the ≥60% threshold. Only 14.8% of students were aware that HPV can cause oropharyngeal cancer, significantly lower in boys (11.2%) than girls (17.3%; p = 0.003). Knowledge was independently associated with female sex, age, and sexual experience.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as indicating a critical knowledge gap about HPV and its oropharyngeal cancer risk, particularly among boys who face higher disease risk. They highlight the potential role of dental professionals in addressing this gap through education and prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that school-based and clinical HPV-prevention strategies could benefit from integrating oral-health professionals to improve awareness and vaccination uptake among adolescents.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the reliance on self-reported data may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size but constrained by the abstract-only scope and lack of detailed statistical reporting.

接种 HPV 疫苗和未接种疫苗的女性的宫颈细胞学异常和 人乳头瘤病毒 状态:一项横断面研究

Cervical cytological abnormalities and human papillomavirus status in HPV-vaccinated and unvaccinated women: a cross-sectional study
作者： Hoda Biari, Mina Galeshi, Shahla Yazdani, Mohammad Ranaei, Hoda Shirafkan
摘要中文翻译旨在调查伊朗女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 状态与宫颈细胞学结果、HPV 患病率 和基因型分布之间的关联,为流行病学数据有限的人群提供真实世界的证据。2023 年 3 月至 2024 年 2 月,对伊朗巴博尔一家三级妇科诊所就诊的 216 名 30-65 岁女性进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了液基细胞学检查和 HPV DNA 基因分型。使用结构化问卷和医疗记录收集人口统计和临床数据。使用适当的统计测试对接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的女性进行比较。总体 HPV 患病率 为 39.8%。接种疫苗的女性 (33.3%) HPV 感染率低于未接种疫苗的女性 (42.9%),但差异无统计学意义 (P = 0.18)。在接种疫苗的女性中未检测到 疫苗 靶向 HPV 16 型和 18 型。高危型HPV 基因型的分布在接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的参与者之间存在显着差异(P < 0.001),非 疫苗 高危基因型在 HPV 阳性疫苗接种的女性中占主导地位。HPV 疫苗接种 状态与宫颈细胞学异常之间没有观察到显着关联。HPV 疫苗接种 与 疫苗 靶向 HPV 16 型和 18 型的缺失以及 HPV 基因型分布的差异相关。然而,非 疫苗 高危型HPV 类型在接种疫苗的女性中仍然很常见,这支持了 宫颈癌 筛查与 HPV 疫苗接种 一起的持续重要性。这些发现提供了有关已接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的伊朗妇女中 HPV 基因型分布的新流行病学数据,并可能有助于为未来的 HPV 预防策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查伊朗女性中 HPV 疫苗接种 状态与宫颈细胞学结果、HPV 患病率 和基因型分布之间的关联,为流行病学数据有限的人群提供真实世界的证据。
设计与对象
2023 年 3 月至 2024 年 2 月,对伊朗巴博尔一家三级妇科诊所就诊的 216 名 30-65 岁女性进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了液基细胞学检查和 HPV DNA 基因分型。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷和医疗记录收集人口统计和临床数据。使用适当的统计测试对接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的女性进行比较。
主要结果
总体 HPV 患病率 为 39.8%。接种疫苗的女性 (33.3%) HPV 感染率低于未接种疫苗的女性 (42.9%),但差异无统计学意义 (P = 0.18)。高危型HPV 基因型的分布在接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的参与者之间存在显着差异 (P < 0.001),非 疫苗 高风险基因型在 HPV 阳性接种疫苗的女性中占主导地位。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了有关已接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的伊朗妇女中 HPV 基因型分布的新流行病学数据,并可能有助于为未来的 HPV 预防策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持 宫颈癌 筛查与 HPV 疫苗接种 一起的持续重要性,因为非 疫苗 高危型HPV 类型在接种疫苗的女性中仍然很常见。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,而且接种组和未接种组之间的 HPV 感染率缺乏统计学意义,表明证据强度中等。

Cervical cytological abnormalities and human papillomavirus status in HPV-vaccinated and unvaccinated women: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Hoda Biari, Mina Galeshi, Shahla Yazdani, Mohammad Ranaei, Hoda Shirafkan
Original abstract (English)
To investigate the association between HPV vaccination status and cervical cytological findings, HPV prevalence, and genotype distribution among Iranian women, providing real-world evidence from a population with limited epidemiological data. A cross-sectional study was conducted from March 2023 to February 2024 among 216 women aged 30–65 years attending a tertiary gynecology clinic in Babol, Iran. Participants underwent liquid-based cytology and HPV DNA genotyping. Demographic and clinical data were collected using structured questionnaires and medical records. Comparisons between vaccinated and unvaccinated women were performed using appropriate statistical tests. Overall HPV prevalence was 39.8%. HPV infection was less frequent among vaccinated women (33.3%) than unvaccinated women (42.9%), although the difference was not statistically significant ( P = 0.18). Vaccine-targeted HPV types 16 and 18 were not detected in vaccinated women. The distribution of high-risk HPV genotypes differed significantly between vaccinated and unvaccinated participants ( P < 0.001), with non-vaccine high-risk genotypes predominating among HPV-positive vaccinated women. No significant association was observed between HPV vaccination status and abnormal cervical cytology. HPV vaccination was associated with the absence of vaccine-targeted HPV types 16 and 18 and with differences in HPV genotype distribution. However, non-vaccine high-risk HPV types remained common among vaccinated women, supporting the continued importance of cervical cancer screening alongside HPV vaccination. These findings provide novel epidemiological data on HPV genotype distribution among vaccinated and unvaccinated Iranian women and may help inform future HPV prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the association between HPV vaccination status and cervical cytological findings, HPV prevalence, and genotype distribution among Iranian women, providing real-world evidence from a population with limited epidemiological data.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from March 2023 to February 2024 among 216 women aged 30–65 years attending a tertiary gynecology clinic in Babol, Iran. Participants underwent liquid-based cytology and HPV DNA genotyping.
Core methods
Demographic and clinical data were collected using structured questionnaires and medical records. Comparisons between vaccinated and unvaccinated women were performed using appropriate statistical tests.
Main results
Overall HPV prevalence was 39.8%. HPV infection was less frequent among vaccinated women (33.3%) than unvaccinated women (42.9%), although the difference was not statistically significant (P = 0.18). The distribution of high-risk HPV genotypes differed significantly between vaccinated and unvaccinated participants (P < 0.001), with non-vaccine high-risk genotypes predominating among HPV-positive vaccinated women.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings provide novel epidemiological data on HPV genotype distribution among vaccinated and unvaccinated Iranian women and may help inform future HPV prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the continued importance of cervical cancer screening alongside HPV vaccination, as non-vaccine high-risk HPV types remained common among vaccinated women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and the lack of statistical significance in HPV infection rates between vaccinated and unvaccinated groups, suggesting moderate evidence strength.

智利中北部地区公共卫生系统中的患病率或人乳头瘤病毒。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile.
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译目标 此前,我们发现在私人医疗系统就诊的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的 患病率 较高。因此,本研究的目的是确定在公共卫生系统就诊的大量 队列研究 女性中 HR-HPV 的频率,并比较这些结果。方法 使用 qPCR 和巴氏技术对 2020 年至 2024 年间参加妇科检查的 18 至 91 岁女性的 39,512 份宫颈样本进行分析。结果 2020-2024年HR-HPV感染总体患病率为13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),2020年、2021年分别为13.07%、12.64%、14.80%、14.24%和14.15%。分别为 2022 年、2023 年和 2024 年。在25岁以下女性中,评估期间HR-HPV的患病率为34.03%,而25至44岁女性为16.35%,45至64岁女性为10.47%,64岁以上女性为11.52%。子宫颈抹片检查显示,HPV 阴性女性的 NILM 结果为 89.71%,而 HPV 阳性女性的 NILM 结果为 74.59%。HPV阴性女性中LSIL和HSIL的发生率分别为0.14%和0.07%;然而,在 HPV 阳性女性中,这些值分别增加到 4.73% 和 4.43%。HPV 阴性女性中只有 0.006% 可能患有癌症,而 HPV 阳性女性中则有 0.46% 可能患有癌症。结论 中北部地区公共卫生系统女性 HR-HPV 感染 患病率 似乎与智利之前报告的相似,但低于同一地区私营医疗系统女性报告的 患病率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定就读公共卫生系统的女性中 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 的频率,并将这些结果与之前报告的私人卫生系统数据进行比较。
设计与对象
队列研究 的一项研究分析了 2020 年至 2024 年间在智利中北部地区接受妇科检查的 39,512 份 18 至 91 岁女性的宫颈样本。
核心方法
使用 qPCR 和 Papanicolaou 技术对样本进行测试,以检测 HR-HPV 感染和细胞学异常。
主要结果
总体 HR-HPV 患病率 为 13.70%(95% CI:13.37-14.05%),与年龄较大的群体相比,25 岁​​以下女性的比率较高(34.03%)。与 HPV 阴性女性相比,HPV 阳性女性的 LSIL(4.73% vs. 0.14%)和 HSIL(4.43% vs. 0.07%)发生率更高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,公共卫生系统中的 HR-HPV 患病率 与之前的国家报告相似,但低于私人卫生系统,突显了各个医疗部门 HPV 感染率的潜在差异。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为有针对性的 HPV 筛查策略(特别是针对年轻女性)提供了信息,并支持公共卫生干预措施,以减少服务不足人群中的 HPV 相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势在于其大样本量和纵向设计,但其局限性包括来自单区域数据的潜在选择偏差以及对自我报告的医疗就诊情况的依赖。

Prevalence of human papillomavirus in the public health system of the central-northern region of Chile.

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE Previously, we found a high prevalence of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the private health system. Therefore, the objective of this study was to determine the frequency of HR-HPV in a large cohort of women attending the public health system and to compare these findings. METHODS A total of 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 were analyzed using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique. RESULTS The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection between 2020 and 2024 was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with annual values of 13.07%, 12.64%, 14.80%, 14.24%, and 14.15% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. In women under 25 years of age, the prevalence of HR-HPV was 34.03% during the evaluated period, compared to 16.35% in women aged 25 to 44 years, 10.47% in those aged 45 to 64 years, and 11.52% in women over 64 years of age. Pap smears showed 89.71% NILM results in HPV-negative women, compared to 74.59% in HPV-positive women. The rates of LSIL and HSIL were 0.14% and 0.07% in HPV-negative women, respectively; however, these values increased to 4.73% and 4.43% in HPV-positive women, respectively. Probable carcinoma was found in only 0.006% of HPV-negative women, but in 0.46% of HPV-positive women. CONCLUSIONS The prevalence of HR-HPV infection in women attending the public health system in the central-northern region appears to be similar to that previously reported in Chile, but lower than that reported in the same region for women attending the private health system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the frequency of high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) in women attending the public health system, comparing these findings to previously reported data from the private health system.
Design and population
A cohort study analyzed 39,512 cervical samples from women aged 18 to 91 years who attended gynecological check-ups between 2020 and 2024 in the central-northern region of Chile.
Core methods
Samples were tested using qPCR and the Papanicolaou technique to detect HR-HPV infection and cytological abnormalities.
Main results
The overall HR-HPV prevalence was 13.70% (95% CI: 13.37-14.05%), with higher rates in women under 25 years (34.03%) compared to older age groups. HPV-positive women showed increased rates of LSIL (4.73% vs. 0.14%) and HSIL (4.43% vs. 0.07%) compared to HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that HR-HPV prevalence in the public health system was similar to previous national reports but lower than in the private health system, highlighting potential disparities in HPV infection rates across healthcare sectors.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings inform targeted HPV screening strategies, particularly for younger women, and support public health interventions to reduce HPV-related diseases in underserved populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's strength lies in its large sample size and longitudinal design, but limitations include potential selection bias from single-region data and reliance on self-reported healthcare attendance.

LIG4 综合征表现为多型 HPV 感染引起的广泛皮肤病毒疣

LIG4 Syndrome Presenting with Extensive Cutaneous Viral Warts Caused by Multi-Type HPV Infection
作者： Kaichen Tang, Shiqi Fan, Rui Zhang, Miao Sun, Dong‐Lai Ma, Xue Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景:DNA 连接酶 4 (LIG4) 催化 V(D)J 重组过程中的最终连接步骤。LIG4 的双等位基因致病性变异会导致严重的联合免疫缺陷 (SCID),这是一种危及生命的免疫缺陷,其特征是缺乏成熟的 T 细胞和 B 细胞。病例介绍:一名 18 岁男性,面部、颈部和四肢出现广泛的疣状和角化性皮肤病变。先证者表现为早发性身材矮小、慢性营养不良、反复肺部感染伴支气管扩张、慢性腹泻和双侧腱鞘炎。免疫表型分析显示持续性全低丙种球蛋白血症、B 细胞缺失、T 细胞和自然杀伤 (NK) 细胞减少以及几乎检测不到的初始 T 细胞。皮损中同时检测到α-HPV和β-HPV。鉴定出复合杂合 LIG4 变体(hg19、NM206937.2、c.833G > T;p.Arg278Leu 遗传自母亲;c.833G > A;p.Arg278 His 遗传自父亲)。结论:本研究报道了一名患有 LIG4 综合征的先证者,其表现出罕见的多发性病毒疣表型以及 T、B 和 NK 细胞联合淋巴细胞减少症。该先证者在同一密码子(c.833G)处存在复合杂合变异,这不仅证实了临床诊断,而且丰富了LIG4综合征的已知表型谱。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 LIG4 综合征的表型谱,这是一种由双等位基因 LIG4 变异引起的罕见免疫缺陷,重点关注其与广泛的皮肤病毒疣和多型 HPV 感染的关联。其基本原理源于需要扩大对 LIG4 综合征基因型-表型相关性的临床理解,超越经典的严重联合免疫缺陷表现。
设计与对象
这是一名单一患者 病例报告,描述一名 18 岁男性,患有复合杂合 LIG4 变异,表现为广泛的皮肤病毒疣和联合免疫缺陷。先证者表现出身材矮小、慢性营养不良、反复肺部感染和慢性腹泻等全身症状。
核心方法
该研究通过基因测序鉴定了复合杂合 LIG4 变体(c.833G > T;p.Arg278Leu 和 c.833G > A;p.Arg278His)。免疫表型分析评估淋巴细胞亚群,HPV 分型检测皮肤病变中的 α-HPV 和 β-HPV。
主要结果
免疫表型分析显示全丙种球蛋白血症、B 细胞缺失、T 细胞和 NK 细胞减少以及几乎检测不到的幼稚 T 细胞。皮损中同时检测到α-HPV和β-HPV。确认了同一密码子 (c.833G) 处的复合杂合 LIG4 变体。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 LIG4 综合征表型谱的扩展,强调了广泛的病毒疣和联合淋巴细胞减少的罕见表现。新颖之处在于记录了这种免疫缺陷综合征中的多型 HPV 感染。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,临床医生应在患有不明原因的联合免疫缺陷和广泛的皮肤病毒疣的患者中考虑 LIG4 综合征,从而能够更早地进行 诊断 和量身定制的治疗。该报告还强调了对此类患者进行 HPV 筛查的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于单个 病例报告,限制了普遍性和因果推理。由于缺乏控制、纵向数据或机制验证,证据强度较低。

LIG4 Syndrome Presenting with Extensive Cutaneous Viral Warts Caused by Multi-Type HPV Infection

Authors: Kaichen Tang, Shiqi Fan, Rui Zhang, Miao Sun, Dong‐Lai Ma, Xue Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Background: DNA Ligase 4 (LIG4) catalyzes the final ligation step during V(D)J recombination. Biallelic pathogenic variants in LIG4 cause severe combined immunodeficiency (SCID), a life-threatening immunodeficiency characterized by the lack of mature T and B cells. Case Presentation: An 18-year-old male exhibited widespread verrucous and keratotic cutaneous lesions across the face, neck, and extremities. The proband manifested early-onset short stature, chronic malnutrition, recurrent pulmonary infections with bronchiectasis, chronic diarrhea, and bilateral tenosynovitis. Immunophenotyping revealed persistent panhypogammaglobulinemia, absent B cells, reduced T and natural killer (NK) cells, and nearly undetectable naïve T cells. Both α-HPV and β-HPV were detected in the cutaneous lesions. Compound heterozygous LIG4 variants (hg19, NM206937.2, c.833G > T; p.Arg278Leu inherited from the mother; c.833G > A; p.Arg278 His inherited from the father) were identified. Conclusions: This study reports a proband with LIG4 syndrome presenting with rare phenotypes of multiple viral warts and combined lymphopenia of T, B, and NK cells. This proband harbors compound heterozygous variants at the same codon (c.833G), which not only confirm the clinical diagnosis but also enrich the known phenotypic spectrum of LIG4 syndrome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the phenotypic spectrum of LIG4 syndrome, a rare immunodeficiency caused by biallelic LIG4 variants, focusing on its association with extensive cutaneous viral warts and multi-type HPV infection. The rationale stems from the need to expand clinical understanding of genotype-phenotype correlations in LIG4 syndrome beyond classical severe combined immunodeficiency presentations.
Design and population
This is a single-patient case report describing an 18-year-old male with compound heterozygous LIG4 variants presenting with extensive cutaneous viral warts and combined immunodeficiency. The proband exhibited systemic manifestations including short stature, chronic malnutrition, recurrent pulmonary infections, and chronic diarrhea.
Core methods
The study identified compound heterozygous LIG4 variants (c.833G > T; p.Arg278Leu and c.833G > A; p.Arg278His) via genetic sequencing. Immunophenotyping assessed lymphocyte subsets, and HPV typing detected both α-HPV and β-HPV in cutaneous lesions.
Main results
Immunophenotyping revealed panhypogammaglobulinemia, absent B cells, reduced T and NK cells, and nearly undetectable naïve T cells. Both α-HPV and β-HPV were detected in the cutaneous lesions. Compound heterozygous LIG4 variants at the same codon (c.833G) were confirmed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as an expansion of the phenotypic spectrum of LIG4 syndrome, highlighting rare presentations with extensive viral warts and combined lymphopenia. The novelty lies in documenting multi-type HPV infection in this immunodeficiency syndrome.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that clinicians should consider LIG4 syndrome in patients with unexplained combined immunodeficiency and extensive cutaneous viral warts, enabling earlier diagnosis and tailored management. The report also underscores the importance of HPV screening in such patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a single case report, restricting generalizability and causal inference. The strength of evidence is low due to the absence of controls, longitudinal data, or mechanistic validation.

Journal: Chemical Communications  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1039/d6cc03106d  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

用于检测 HPV-16 DNA 的蒽基锌框架的共反应物特异性电化学发光。

Coreactant-specific electrochemiluminescence of an anthracene-based zinc framework for detection of HPV-16 DNA.
作者： Zhenglong Tao, Yufei Wang, Xueyi Zheng, Zhiyuan Xu, Jianping Lei
摘要中文翻译共反应物的有效激活在电化学发光(ECL)增强中具有挑战性。在此,设计了一种以蒽衍生物作为发光体配体和Zn 2+ 节点的金属有机骨架纳米发射体。它通过共反应物和 Zn 2+ 节点之间的协调相互作用证明了 1,4-二氮杂双环[2.2.2]辛烷 (DABCO) 特异性 ECL 途径,与传统共反应物相比,显着增强了发射,建立了 DNA 检测的灵敏策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景

Coreactant-specific electrochemiluminescence of an anthracene-based zinc framework for detection of HPV-16 DNA.

Authors: Zhenglong Tao, Yufei Wang, Xueyi Zheng, Zhiyuan Xu, Jianping Lei
Original abstract (English)
The efficient activation of a coreactant is challenging in electrochemiluminescence (ECL) enhancement. Herein, a metal-organic framework nanoemitter was designed with an anthracene derivative as a luminophore ligand and a Zn 2+ node. It demonstrated a 1,4-diazabicyclo[2.2.2]octane (DABCO)-specific ECL pathway via coordination interaction between the coreactant and the Zn 2+ node, which significantly enhanced emission over conventional coreactants, establishing a sensitive strategy for DNA detection.

外阴尖锐湿疣作为高危宫颈 HPV 感染和宫颈细胞学异常标志物的临床意义

Clinical Significance of Vulvar Condyloma as a Marker of Concurrent High-Risk Cervical HPV Infection and Abnormal Cervical Cytology
作者： Eda GÜNER ÖZEN, Süleyman Özen, Özgün Akbaş, Ahkam Göksel Kanmaz, Yaşam Kemal Akpak
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 人乳头瘤病毒 及其前驱病变的主要病因。尽管外阴尖锐湿疣通常与低危 HPV 类型相关,但 高危型HPV 基因型可能共存。本研究评估了外阴尖锐湿疣、宫颈细胞学异常、高危型HPV 阳性和宫颈组织病理学结果之间的关联。方法:这项回顾性观察研究纳入了 2025 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月期间在伊兹密尔市医院妇产科接受评估的 407 名女性。患者在有临床指征时接受了宫颈细胞学检查、HPV 检测、外阴检查和阴道镜活检。根据组织病理学证实的外阴湿疣是否存在对患者进行分类。进行逻辑回归分析以确定与外阴尖锐湿疣相关的因素。结果:180 名患者(44.2%)发现外阴尖锐湿疣。66/180 名外阴尖锐湿疣患者 (36.7%) 和 41/227 名外阴尖锐湿疣患者 (18.1%) 记录异常宫颈细胞学检查 (p < 0.001)。分别有 104/180 (57.8%) 和 86/227 (37.9%) 患者观察到 高危型HPV 阳性 (p < 0.001)。在多变量分析中,HPV16 阳性的调整 OR 为 2.30(95% CI 1.36–3.89,p = 0.002),HPV18 阳性的调整 OR 为 3.29(95% CI 1.40–7.73,p = 0.006),宫颈细胞学异常为 2.01(95% CI 1.24–3.25,p = 0.006)。= 0.005)。在总体 队列研究 中,尖锐湿疣阳性组记录的 CIN2+ 为 15/180 (8.3%),尖锐湿疣阴性组为 7/227 (3.1%);在活检限制分析中,CIN2+ 分别为 15/82 (18.3%) 和 7/41 (17.1%)。结论:外阴湿疣与宫颈细胞学异常和高危型HPV感染,特别是HPV16/18阳性独立相关。这些强有力的关联表明,外阴尖锐湿疣可能是并发宫颈 HPV 相关疾病的临床可见标志。对于患有外阴湿疣的女性,需要进行仔细的宫颈评估,而 CIN2+ 结果应谨慎解释,直到通过标准化组织病理学验证在更大的前瞻性 队列研究 中得到证实。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 HPV 是 宫颈癌 和前驱病变的主要原因,该研究探讨了外阴尖锐湿疣作为并发高危宫颈 HPV 感染和异常宫颈细胞学的潜在指标的临床意义。
设计与对象
这项回顾性观察研究招募了 2025 年 1 月至 2026 年 1 月期间在伊兹密尔市医院妇产科接受评估的 407 名女性,并根据是否存在经组织病理学证实的外阴尖锐湿疣对她们进行分类。
核心方法
当有临床指征时,患者接受宫颈细胞学检查、HPV 检测、外阴检查和阴道镜活检,并使用逻辑回归分析来确定与外阴尖锐湿疣相关的因素。
主要结果
180 名患者 (44.2%) 发现外阴尖锐湿疣,宫颈细胞学检查异常的患者为 66/180 (36.7%),患有尖锐湿疣的患者为 41/227 (18.1%),无尖锐湿疣的患者为 41/227 (18.1%) (p < 0.001),高危型HPV 阳性的患者为 104/180 (57.8%) 与 86/227 (37.9%) (p < 0.001)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,外阴尖锐湿疣与宫颈细胞学异常和 高危型HPV 感染(尤其是 HPV16/18)独立相关,表明它可能作为并发宫颈 HPV 相关疾病的临床可见标志物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持对患有外阴尖锐湿疣的女性进行仔细的宫颈评估,以检测潜在的 高危型HPV 感染和相关的宫颈异常。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对单中心 队列研究 的依赖限制了普遍性,在通过标准化组织病理学验证在更大的前瞻性 队列研究 中得到证实之前,应谨慎解释 CIN2+ 关联。

Clinical Significance of Vulvar Condyloma as a Marker of Concurrent High-Risk Cervical HPV Infection and Abnormal Cervical Cytology

Authors: Eda GÜNER ÖZEN, Süleyman Özen, Özgün Akbaş, Ahkam Göksel Kanmaz, Yaşam Kemal Akpak
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the principal etiological factor in cervical cancer and its precursor lesions. Although vulvar condyloma is typically associated with low-risk HPV types, high-risk HPV genotypes may coexist. This study evaluated the association between vulvar condyloma, cervical cytological abnormalities, high-risk HPV positivity, and cervical histopathological outcomes. Methods: This retrospective observational study included 407 women evaluated at the Department of Obstetrics and Gynecology, Izmir City Hospital, between January 2025 and January 2026. Patients underwent cervical cytology, HPV testing, vulvar examination, and colposcopic biopsy when clinically indicated. Patients were categorized according to the presence or absence of histopathologically confirmed vulvar condyloma. Logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with vulvar condyloma. Results: Vulvar condyloma was identified in 180 patients (44.2%). Abnormal cervical cytology was recorded in 66/180 patients (36.7%) with vulvar condyloma and 41/227 patients (18.1%) without condyloma (p < 0.001). High-risk HPV positivity was observed in 104/180 (57.8%) and 86/227 (37.9%) patients, respectively (p < 0.001). In multivariate analysis, adjusted ORs were 2.30 for HPV16 positivity (95% CI 1.36–3.89, p = 0.002), 3.29 for HPV18 positivity (95% CI 1.40–7.73, p = 0.006), and 2.01 for abnormal cervical cytology (95% CI 1.24–3.25, p = 0.005). Recorded CIN2+ was 15/180 (8.3%) in the condyloma-positive group and 7/227 (3.1%) in the condyloma-negative group in the overall cohort; in the biopsy-restricted analysis, CIN2+ was 15/82 (18.3%) and 7/41 (17.1%), respectively. Conclusions: Vulvar condyloma was independently associated with abnormal cervical cytology and high-risk HPV infection, particularly HPV16/18 positivity. These robust associations suggest that vulvar condyloma may represent a clinically visible marker of concurrent cervical HPV-related disease. Careful cervical evaluation is warranted in women presenting with vulvar condyloma, whereas CIN2+ findings should be interpreted cautiously until confirmed in larger prospective cohorts with standardized histopathological verification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the clinical significance of vulvar condyloma as a potential indicator of concurrent high-risk cervical HPV infection and abnormal cervical cytology, given that HPV is the principal cause of cervical cancer and precursor lesions.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study enrolled 407 women evaluated at the Department of Obstetrics and Gynecology, Izmir City Hospital, between January 2025 and January 2026, and categorized them by the presence or absence of histopathologically confirmed vulvar condyloma.
Core methods
Patients underwent cervical cytology, HPV testing, vulvar examination, and colposcopic biopsy when clinically indicated, with logistic regression analyses used to identify factors associated with vulvar condyloma.
Main results
Vulvar condyloma was identified in 180 patients (44.2%), abnormal cervical cytology in 66/180 (36.7%) with condyloma versus 41/227 (18.1%) without (p < 0.001), and high-risk HPV positivity in 104/180 (57.8%) versus 86/227 (37.9%) (p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that vulvar condyloma was independently associated with abnormal cervical cytology and high-risk HPV infection, particularly HPV16/18, suggesting it may serve as a clinically visible marker of concurrent cervical HPV-related disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support careful cervical evaluation in women presenting with vulvar condyloma to detect potential high-risk HPV infection and related cervical abnormalities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on a single-center cohort limit generalisability, and CIN2+ associations should be interpreted cautiously until confirmed in larger prospective cohorts with standardized histopathological verification.

<b>巴基斯坦拉合尔三级护理医院护士和医生关于人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的知识、态度和实践:</b><b>跨部门研究</b>

<b>KNOWLEDGE, ATTITUDE AND PRACTICE REGARDING HUMAN PAPILLOMA VIRUS (HPV) VACCINE AMONG NURSES AND DOCTORS IN TERTIARY CARE HOSPITALS, LAHORE PAKISTAN: </b><b>A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY</b>
作者： Azwa Hameed, Tehmina Ishaq, Shahla Waris, Iqra Naz, Asther Boota, Asifa Shehzadi
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌 是一种严重的疾病,导致世界各地(尤其是发展中国家)许多妇女死亡。它主要是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的。医生和护士非常重要,因为他们可以向人们传授 HPV 疫苗 知识并帮助阻止这种癌症。目的:本研究的主要目的是评估巴基斯坦拉合尔三级护理医院的医生和护士对 HPV 疫苗 的知识、态度和实践。这项研究还评估了参与者的态度和实践之间的关联。材料和方法:使用改编问卷的描述性横断面研究,有 3 个部分的问题:知识问题、态度问题和练习问题。拉合尔法蒂玛纪念医院共有 200 名医护人员(61% 是医务人员,39% 是护士)参加。数据收集于2025年12月至2026年5月,并使用社会科学统计软件包(SPSS)26平均值和标准进行分析。结果:结果显示大多数医护人员 (73% n=146) 具有良好的知识水平。例如,78% 的人知道 HPV 会导致 宫颈癌,69% 的人知道 疫苗 在巴基斯坦可用,77% 的人知道 疫苗 的正确年龄是 9-14 岁。此外,61% 的人正确选择子宫颈抹片检查作为筛查测试。然而,也存在一些问题:大约一半的参与者不知道追赶疫苗接种的正确指导方针。此外,72% 的参与者获得了良好的态度和实践水平。此外,结果还显示,对于 HPV 疫苗接种 的态度和实践之间存在显着关联。结论: 总之,拉合尔的医护人员对 HPV 疫苗 有很好的了解,但对具体指南仍存在一定的知识差距。医疗领导者和政策制定者应制定明确的培训计划和研讨会来弥补这些差距,以便医生和护士可以自信地向患者推荐 疫苗。

问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一种严重的疾病,导致世界各地(尤其是发展中国家)许多妇女死亡。它主要是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起的。

对巴基斯坦拉合尔三级医院的医护人员进行了一项描述性横断面研究。拉合尔法蒂玛纪念医院共有 200 名医护人员(61% 是医务人员,39% 是护士)参加。
核心方法
使用改编问卷的描述性横断面研究,有 3 个部分的问题:知识问题、态度问题和实践问题。数据收集于2025年12月至2026年5月,并使用社会科学统计软件包(SPSS)26平均值和标准进行分析。
主要结果
结果显示,大多数医护人员 (73% n=146) 具有良好的知识水平。例如,78% 的人知道 HPV 会导致 宫颈癌,69% 的人知道 疫苗 在巴基斯坦可用,77% 的人知道 疫苗 的正确年龄是 9-14 岁。此外,61% 的人正确选择子宫颈抹片检查作为筛查测试。
解释与创新
然而,也存在一些问题:大约一半的参与者不知道追赶疫苗接种的正确指导方针。此外,结果还显示,对于 HPV 疫苗接种 的态度和实践之间存在显着关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为医护人员制定有针对性的培训计划提供信息,以提高他们有关 HPV 疫苗接种 的知识和实践,这对于 宫颈癌 的预防至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于巴基斯坦拉合尔的一家三级护理医院,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它基于自我报告的数据和横截面设计。

<b>KNOWLEDGE, ATTITUDE AND PRACTICE REGARDING HUMAN PAPILLOMA VIRUS (HPV) VACCINE AMONG NURSES AND DOCTORS IN TERTIARY CARE HOSPITALS, LAHORE PAKISTAN: </b><b>A CROSS-SECTIONAL STUDY</b>

Authors: Azwa Hameed, Tehmina Ishaq, Shahla Waris, Iqra Naz, Asther Boota, Asifa Shehzadi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cervical cancer is a serious disease that kills many women around the world, especially in developing countries. It is mostly caused by the Human Papillomavirus (HPV). Doctors and nurses are very important because they can teach people about the HPV vaccine and help stop this cancer. Objective: The main objective of this study was to assess knowledge, attitude and practices of doctors and nurses regarding HPV vaccine, employed in tertiary care hospital, Lahore, Pakistan. This study also assesses the association between attitude and practice of participants. Materials and Methods: A descriptive cross-sectional study using an adapted questionnaire, having 3 portions of questions, knowledge questions, attitude questions, and practice questions. A total of 200 healthcare personnel (61% medical officers and 39% of nurses) took part from Fatima Memorial Hospital, Lahore. The data was collected from December 2025 to May 2026, and analyzed using Statistical Package for Social Sciences (SPSS) 26 Mean and Standard. Results: The results showed that most healthcare workers (73% n=146) have a good knowledge level. For example, 78% knew that HPV causes cervical cancer, 69% knew the vaccine is available in Pakistan, and 77% knew the correct age for the vaccine is 9–14 years. Also, 61% correctly chose the Pap smear as the screening test. However, there were some problems: about half of the participants did not know the correct guidelines for catch-up vaccination. Moreover, 72% of participants acquire a good level of attitude and practices. Additionally, results also show a significant association between attitude and practice regarding HPV vaccination. Conclusion: In conclusion, healthcare workers in Lahore have a good understanding of the HPV vaccine, but they still have some knowledge gap about specific guidelines. Medical leaders and policymakers should create clear training programs and workshops to fix these gaps so that doctors and nurses can confidently recommend the vaccine to patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a serious disease that kills many women around the world, especially in developing countries. It is mostly caused by the Human Papillomavirus (HPV).
Design and population
A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted among healthcare personnel in tertiary care hospitals in Lahore, Pakistan. A total of 200 healthcare personnel (61% medical officers and 39% nurses) took part from Fatima Memorial Hospital, Lahore.
Core methods
A descriptive cross-sectional study using an adapted questionnaire, having 3 portions of questions, knowledge questions, attitude questions, and practice questions. The data was collected from December 2025 to May 2026, and analyzed using Statistical Package for Social Sciences (SPSS) 26 Mean and Standard.
Main results
The results showed that most healthcare workers (73% n=146) have a good knowledge level. For example, 78% knew that HPV causes cervical cancer, 69% knew the vaccine is available in Pakistan, and 77% knew the correct age for the vaccine is 9–14 years. Also, 61% correctly chose the Pap smear as the screening test.
Interpretation and novelty
However, there were some problems: about half of the participants did not know the correct guidelines for catch-up vaccination. Additionally, results also show a significant association between attitude and practice regarding HPV vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted training programs for healthcare workers to improve their knowledge and practices regarding HPV vaccination, which is crucial for cervical cancer prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary care hospital in Lahore, Pakistan, which may affect the generalisability of the findings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported data and a cross-sectional design.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 不同剂量方案的免疫原性、功效和安全性:随机对照试验的贝叶斯网络荟萃分析

Immunogenicity, efficacy, and safety of different dose schedules of human papillomavirus vaccines: a Bayesian network meta–analysis of randomized controlled trials
作者： Jing Ye, Zhen-qiong Zhu, Fang Wang, Ya Chen, De-yan Xie, Xiao-feng He
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了优化 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 免疫计划的公共卫生需求,通过从标准三剂量方案转向减少剂量计划来增加全球覆盖率并降低成本。它询问与三剂方案相比,一剂或两剂方案是否能保持足够的免疫原性、有效性和安全性。
设计与对象
对随机对照试验进行贝叶斯网络荟萃分析,综合了截至 2026 年 2 月 15 日的 PubMed、Embase、CENTRAL、CNKI 和万方数据的数据。纳入的试验评估了 9-26 岁女性中 HPV 疫苗 的不同剂量方案。
核心方法
两位评价者独立检索了多个数据库,并使用 R 中的 multinma 包构建了贝叶斯随机效应模型。治疗 采用累积排名下的表面(SUCRA)值进行排名,主要结果是 HPV-16/18 血清转化率和 6 个月持续感染。
主要结果
For immunogenicity, the 9-valent three-dose regimen showed the highest seroconversion rate versus placebo (OR 62.45, 95% CrI 15.20–158.30; SUCRA 0.96), though estimates were driven by near-zero events in placebo arms. Single-dose schedules maintained clinically significant protection against persistent infection (e.g., 2-valent one-dose vs. placebo: OR 0.32, 95% CrI 0.07–0.96; SUCRA 0.61). Safety rankings indicated one-dose schedules had the lowest risk of injection-site pain.
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,与标准三剂量方案相比,减少剂量方案产生的抗体滴度较低,但针对持续性 HPV 感染提供了强大的保护功效,且安全性优异。他们强调,9-14 岁青少年的两剂方案可能与三剂方案相当。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在青少年中采用两剂方案,并表明单剂策略可能是资源有限环境中加速 宫颈癌 消除的可行替代方案。结果表明,减少剂量方案的政策变化可能会在不影响保护的情况下增强 疫苗接种 的覆盖范围。
局限与证据强度
证据受到终点的替代性质和用于确定非劣效性的网络荟萃分析的限制的限制,需要谨慎的解释和长期临床终点数据的需要。由于依赖间接证据和接近于零的安慰剂事件的潜在偏差,对比较排名的信心中等。

Immunogenicity, efficacy, and safety of different dose schedules of human papillomavirus vaccines: a Bayesian network meta–analysis of randomized controlled trials

Authors: Jing Ye, Zhen-qiong Zhu, Fang Wang, Ya Chen, De-yan Xie, Xiao-feng He
Original abstract (English)
Background To enhance global vaccine coverage and reduce costs, Human papillomavirus (HPV) immunization schedules are shifting from the standard three–dose (3D) regimen toward reduced–dose (1D or 2D) schedules. We aimed to evaluate the comparative immunogenicity, efficacy, and safety of diverse dose schedules and vaccine types using a Bayesian network meta–analysis. Methods Two reviewers independently searched PubMed, Embase, Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials (CENTRAL), CNKI, and Wanfang Data from inception to February 15, 2026, for randomized controlled trials (RCTs) evaluating different dose schedules of HPV vaccines in females aged 9–26 years. Primary outcomes were HPV–16/18 Seroconversion rates (SCR) and 6–month Persistent infection (PI). A Bayesian random–effects model was constructed using the multinma package in R, and treatments were ranked using the Surface Under the Cumulative Ranking (SUCRA) values. Results For immunogenicity, the 9v–3D regimen demonstrated the highest SCR compared with placebo (OR 62.45, 95% CrI 15.20–158.30; SUCRA 0.96). However, these large effect estimates are largely driven by near–zero events in placebo arms. Regarding protective efficacy against PI, while 3–dose regimens ranked highest (9v–3D SUCRA 0.95; 2v–3D SUCRA 0.89), single–dose schedules (1D) maintained clinically significant protection (e.g., 2v–1D vs. placebo: OR 0.32, 95% CrI 0.07–0.96; SUCRA 0.61). Safety rankings identified 1D schedules as having the lowest risk of injection–site pain (SUCRA: 2v–1D 88.0%, 9v–1D 82.0%). Subgroup analysis suggested comparable efficacy of 2–dose regimens in adolescents aged 9–14 years compared to the standard 3D regimen (P–interaction = 0.04). Conclusions Although reduced–dose schedules induce lower antibody titers than the standard 3–dose regimen, they provide robust protective efficacy against persistent HPV infection with a superior safety profile. Our findings support the widespread adoption of 2–dose schedules in adolescents and suggest that a single–dose strategy is a viable alternative in resource–constrained settings to accelerate the elimination of cervical cancer. However, given the surrogate nature of the study endpoints and the limitations of network meta-analysis for establishing non-inferiority, cautious interpretation of these results is warranted, and further long-term clinical endpoint data are needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to optimize human papillomavirus (HPV) immunization schedules to increase global coverage and reduce costs by shifting from the standard three-dose regimen to reduced-dose schedules. It asked whether one-dose or two-dose schedules maintain adequate immunogenicity, efficacy, and safety compared with the three-dose schedule.
Design and population
A Bayesian network meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials was conducted, synthesizing data from PubMed, Embase, CENTRAL, CNKI, and Wanfang Data up to February 15, 2026. Included trials evaluated different dose schedules of HPV vaccines in females aged 9–26 years.
Core methods
Two reviewers independently searched multiple databases and constructed a Bayesian random-effects model using the multinma package in R. Treatments were ranked with Surface Under the Cumulative Ranking (SUCRA) values, and primary outcomes were HPV-16/18 seroconversion rates and 6-month persistent infection.
Main results
For immunogenicity, the 9-valent three-dose regimen showed the highest seroconversion rate versus placebo (OR 62.45, 95% CrI 15.20–158.30; SUCRA 0.96), though estimates were driven by near-zero events in placebo arms. Single-dose schedules maintained clinically significant protection against persistent infection (e.g., 2-valent one-dose vs. placebo: OR 0.32, 95% CrI 0.07–0.96; SUCRA 0.61). Safety rankings indicated one-dose schedules had the lowest risk of injection-site pain.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that reduced-dose schedules induce lower antibody titers than the standard three-dose regimen but provide robust protective efficacy against persistent HPV infection with a superior safety profile. They highlighted that two-dose schedules in adolescents aged 9–14 years may be comparable to the three-dose regimen.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support adopting two-dose schedules in adolescents and suggest that a single-dose strategy could be a viable alternative in resource-constrained settings to accelerate cervical cancer elimination. The results imply that policy changes toward reduced-dose regimens may enhance vaccination coverage without compromising protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the surrogate nature of endpoints and the constraints of network meta-analysis for establishing non-inferiority, warranting cautious interpretation and the need for long-term clinical endpoint data. Confidence in the comparative rankings is moderate due to reliance on indirect evidence and potential bias from near-zero placebo events.

高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 与结直肠癌风险:伊朗南部的初步研究

High-risk human papillomavirus and risk of colorectal cancer: a preliminary study in South of Iran
作者： Reza Taherkhani, Fatemeh Farshadpour, Elham Abshirini, Mohammad Reza Ravanbod, Azam Amini, Maria Zahiri
摘要中文翻译伊朗结直肠癌的 发病率 发病率呈上升趋势。尽管结直肠癌的确切病因仍不清楚,但高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 被认为是结直肠癌 发病机制 的潜在贡献者。结直肠癌患者HPV感染患病率的信息可以通过改进区域特异性筛查策略和HPV 疫苗接种来影响有针对性的预防和管理策略。因此,本研究旨在检测伊朗南部结直肠癌组织标本中的高危型HPV。通过巢式 PCR 检测,对 147 名结直肠癌患者和 144 名患有结直肠炎症和息肉的非癌症患者的结直肠活检样本进行了 HPV 检测。128 份结直肠癌样本中,有 2 份样本检测到 高危型HPV(1.56%,95% CI:0.48%-5.49%),而非癌性结直肠样本 HPV 呈阴性。HPV阳性患者在≥80岁年龄组(8.33%)和70-79岁年龄组(5.26%)中发现,而<70岁年龄组没有表现出HPV阳性。两性的 HPV 感染频率大致相同(男性为 1.54%,女性为 1.59%)。Ahram 地区 9.09% 的居民和 Khormoj 地区 4.35% 的居民检测出 HPV DNA,而 Bushehr、Borazjan、Dayer、Genaveh 和 Kangan 地区的居民 HPV 感染呈阴性。结直肠癌患者HPV感染患病率与年龄、性别、居住地无统计学相关性。尽管直肠腺癌患者(3.33%)的HPV阳性率高于结肠腺癌患者(1.02%),但HPV感染频率与结直肠癌的解剖位置并无显着相关性。尽管这项研究在 1.56% 的结直肠癌活检标本中检测到 HPV,但仍无法确定 HPV 感染与伊朗南部结直肠癌发生之间可能存在的关联。HPV 感染与结直肠癌之间的因果关系似乎受到环境和个人危险因素、遗传背景以及 HPV 感染的区域 流行病学 的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于伊朗结直肠癌 发病率 发病率不断上升以及 HPV 可能促进其发展的建议,该研究旨在调查高危 发病率 (HPV) 在结直肠癌 发病率 中的潜在作用。
设计与对象
这项在伊朗南部进行的初步研究分析了 147 名结直肠癌患者和 144 名患有结直肠炎症和息肉的非癌患者的结直肠活检样本,以检测 高危型HPV。
核心方法
该研究使用巢式 PCR 测定来测试结直肠活检样本中是否存在 高危型HPV,比较癌症和非癌症患者的结果。
主要结果
128 份结直肠癌样本中有 2 份检测到 高危型HPV(1.56%,95% CI:0.48%-5.49%),而所有非癌性结直肠样本均为 HPV 阴性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 高危型HPV 可能存在于一小部分结直肠癌病例中的初步证据,但他们强调,该研究无法确定 HPV 感染与结直肠癌发生之间可能存在的关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要进一步研究来探索 HPV 在结直肠癌中的潜在作用,这可以为特定区域的筛查策略和 HPV 疫苗接种 计划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小以及无法建立 HPV 感染与结直肠癌之间的因果关系,从而导致可信度评估适中。

High-risk human papillomavirus and risk of colorectal cancer: a preliminary study in South of Iran

Authors: Reza Taherkhani, Fatemeh Farshadpour, Elham Abshirini, Mohammad Reza Ravanbod, Azam Amini, Maria Zahiri
Original abstract (English)
The incidence rate of colorectal cancer is on the rise in Iran. Although the precise etiology of colorectal cancer remains unknown, high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs) are suggested as potential contributors to the pathogenesis of colorectal cancer. Information on the prevalence of HPV infection among patients with colorectal cancer can affect targeted prevention and management strategies by improving region-specific screening strategies and HPV vaccination. Therefore, this study was designed to detect high-risk HPVs in colorectal cancer tissue specimens in the South of Iran. Colorectal biopsy samples from 147 patients with colorectal cancer and 144 non-cancerous patients with colorectal inflammation and polyps were tested for the detection of HPVs by nested PCR assay. High-risk HPVs were detected in 2 samples of 128 colorectal cancer samples (1.56%, 95% CI: 0.48%-5.49%), while the non-cancerous colorectal samples were HPV negative. HPV-positive patients were identified in the age group ≥ 80 years (8.33%) and the age group 70–79 years (5.26%), while the age groups < 70 years did not show HPV positivity. The frequency of HPV infection was approximately equal in both genders (1.54% in males and 1.59% in females). HPV DNA was detected in 9.09% of patients resident in Ahram and 4.35% of patients resident in Khormoj, whereas the residents of Bushehr, Borazjan, Dayer, Genaveh, and Kangan were negative for HPV infection. The prevalence of HPV infection among patients with colorectal cancer was not statistically associated with age, gender, and place of residency. Although the patients with rectal adenocarcinoma (3.33%) showed a higher rate of HPV positivity compared to the patients with colon adenocarcinoma (1.02%), the frequency of HPV infection was not significantly associated with the anatomical location of colorectal cancer. This study, despite detecting HPVs in 1.56% of colorectal cancer biopsy specimens, cannot determine the possible association between HPV infection and colorectal carcinogenesis in the South of Iran. The causal relationship between HPV infection and colorectal cancer appears to be influenced by environmental and individual risk factors, the genetic backgrounds, as well as the regional epidemiology of HPV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential role of high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs) in the pathogenesis of colorectal cancer, given the rising incidence rate of colorectal cancer in Iran and the suggestion that HPVs may contribute to its development.
Design and population
This preliminary study in South of Iran analyzed colorectal biopsy samples from 147 patients with colorectal cancer and 144 non-cancerous patients with colorectal inflammation and polyps to detect high-risk HPVs.
Core methods
The study used a nested PCR assay to test colorectal biopsy samples for the presence of high-risk HPVs, comparing results between cancer and non-cancerous patients.
Main results
High-risk HPVs were detected in 2 out of 128 colorectal cancer samples (1.56%, 95% CI: 0.48%-5.49%), while all non-cancerous colorectal samples were HPV negative.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as preliminary evidence that high-risk HPVs may be present in a small subset of colorectal cancer cases, but they emphasized that the study cannot determine a possible association between HPV infection and colorectal carcinogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that further research is needed to explore the potential role of HPV in colorectal cancer, which could inform region-specific screening strategies and HPV vaccination programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its small sample size and the inability to establish a causal relationship between HPV infection and colorectal cancer, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.
