马罗瓦伊区马达加斯加成年育龄妇女的阴道微生物群:与 人乳头瘤病毒 和埃及血吸虫感染关联的首次表征和探索。

The Vaginal Microbiota of Adult Malagasy Women of Reproductive Age in the Marovoay District: First Characterization and Exploration of Associations With Human Papillomavirus and Schistosoma haematobium Infections.
作者： Jana Christina Hey, Johanna Saalfrank, Tahinamandranto Rasamoelina, Anjarasoa Ravo Razafindrakoto, Matthieu Razafindralava, Valeri Gildas Rajaoniarivo, Jean-Marc Kutz, Sonya Ratefiarisoa, Thorsten Thye, Irina Kislaya
摘要中文翻译背景:阴道微生物群对女性健康起着重要作用。其成分的变化与多种性传播感染有关,包括 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 或埃及血吸虫等寄生虫感染。在马达加斯加,妇科疾病例如埃及沙门氏菌感染(女性生殖器血吸虫病)、HPV 感染和 宫颈癌 的慢性表现非常普遍;然而,有关这些条件与阴道微生物群 (VM) 之间相互作用的数据仍然很少。此外,大多数数据来自北半球,导致不同地理和社会背景下对“健康”虚拟机的理解存在偏见。我们研究的目的是首次描述马达加斯加育龄成年女性的 VM 特征,并描述在 3 种影响泌尿生殖道的情况下阴道环境的变化。方法和结果:我们对 443 名参与者的 VM 进行了表征,确定了 5 种社区状态类型 (CST I-V),其中 CST IV(57.1%,多样化)和 CST III(34.1%,乳酸杆菌占主导)最为普遍。CST 与之前的抗生素使用相关,而 α 和 β 多样性的变异性与饮食行为和之前的抗生素使用相关。差异丰度分析显示 HPV 阳性和女性生殖器血吸虫病阳性参与者的特定分类群之间存在差异。结论:通过在马达加斯加对 VM 进行的首次研究,我们有助于更广泛地了解阴道健康,并通过丰富微生物群数据库来缩小撒哈拉以南非洲 VM 研究的差距。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了马达加斯加阴道微生物群 (VM) 数据的缺乏及其与流行的泌尿生殖道感染(包括 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV))的相互作用的问题。作者强调了现有 VM 研究中的地理和社会偏见,这些偏见主要来自北半球。
设计与对象
这是对马达加斯加马洛沃伊区 443 名育龄成年女性的横断面特征研究。该研究旨在描述三种泌尿生殖道疾病(包括 HPV 感染)下 VM 的变异性。
核心方法
VM 的特点是识别群落状态类型 (CST) I-V,其中 CST IV(多样化)和 CST III(惰性乳杆菌为主)最为普遍。评估了 α 和 β 多样性,并进行了差异丰度分析,以比较 HPV 感染参与者、女性生殖器血吸虫病和对照者之间的分类群。
主要结果
参与者中观察到57.1%的受试者为CST IV型,34.1%的受试者为CST III型。CST类型与既往抗生素使用史相关,而α多样性和β多样性的变异性则与饮食行为及既往抗生素使用史相关联。差异丰度分析显示,与其他受试者相比,HPV阳性受试者中特定分类单元存在差异。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为有助于更广泛地了解马达加斯加的阴道健康,并通过丰富微生物群数据库来解决撒哈拉以南非洲地区的研究空白。他们强调了作为第一个描述该人群 VM 特征的研究的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可能通过强调 VM 组成、HPV 感染和其他泌尿生殖系统疾病之间的关联,为未来的 监测 和马达加斯加的研究重点提供信息。这可以指导该地区妇女生殖健康的有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法进行因果推断。由于饮食和抗生素使用因素的潜在混淆以及缺乏纵向随访,证据强度中等。

The Vaginal Microbiota of Adult Malagasy Women of Reproductive Age in the Marovoay District: First Characterization and Exploration of Associations With Human Papillomavirus and Schistosoma haematobium Infections.

Authors: Jana Christina Hey, Johanna Saalfrank, Tahinamandranto Rasamoelina, Anjarasoa Ravo Razafindrakoto, Matthieu Razafindralava, Valeri Gildas Rajaoniarivo, Jean-Marc Kutz, Sonya Ratefiarisoa, Thorsten Thye, Irina Kislaya
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The vaginal microbiome plays an important role for women's health. Changes in its composition have been associated with several sexually transmitted infections, including human papillomavirus (HPV) or parasitic infections such as Schistosoma haematobium. In Madagascar, gynecological conditions such as chronic manifestations of S. haematobium infections (female genital schistosomiasis), HPV infections, and cervical cancer are highly prevalent; however, data on the interplay between these conditions and the vaginal microbiota (VM) are still scarce. In addition, the majority of data originates from the Global North, generating a biased understanding of "healthy" VM across different geographic and social contexts. The objective of our study was to characterize for the first time the VM of adult women of reproductive age in Madagascar and to describe the variability of the vaginal environment in the presence of 3 conditions affecting the urogenital tract. METHODS AND RESULTS: We characterized the VM of 443 participants, identifying the 5 community state types (CSTs I-V), with CST IV (57.1%, diverse) and CST III (34.1%, Lactobacillus iners dominated) as the most prevalent. CSTs were associated with previous antibiotics usage, while variability in the alpha and beta diversity was associated with dietary behavior and previous antibiotic usage. Differential abundance analysis showed variations among specific taxa in HPV-positive and female genital schistosomiasis-positive participants. CONCLUSIONS: With this first study of the VM in Madagascar we contribute to a broader understanding of vaginal health, as well as narrowing the gap of VM research in sub-Saharan Africa by enriching microbiota databases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the scarcity of data on the vaginal microbiota (VM) in Madagascar and its interplay with prevalent urogenital infections, including human papillomavirus (HPV). The authors highlighted a geographic and social bias in existing VM research, which is largely derived from the Global North.
Design and population
This was a cross-sectional characterization study of 443 adult women of reproductive age in the Marovoay District of Madagascar. The study aimed to describe VM variability in the presence of three urogenital tract conditions, including HPV infection.
Core methods
The VM was characterized by identifying community state types (CSTs) I–V, with CST IV (diverse) and CST III (Lactobacillus iners dominated) being the most prevalent. Alpha and beta diversity were assessed, and differential abundance analysis was performed to compare taxa among participants with HPV infection, female genital schistosomiasis, and controls.
Main results
CST IV was observed in 57.1% of participants and CST III in 34.1%. CSTs were associated with previous antibiotic usage, while alpha and beta diversity variability was linked to dietary behavior and prior antibiotic use. Differential abundance analysis revealed variations in specific taxa among HPV-positive participants compared to others.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as contributing to a broader understanding of vaginal health in Madagascar and addressing the research gap in sub-Saharan Africa by enriching microbiota databases. They emphasized the novelty of being the first study to characterize the VM in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future surveillance and research priorities in Madagascar by highlighting associations between VM composition, HPV infection, and other urogenital conditions. This could guide targeted interventions for women's reproductive health in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential confounding from dietary and antibiotic use factors and the lack of longitudinal follow-up.

尼泊尔丘陵地区育龄妇女人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)比例和基因型分布

Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Proportion and Genotype Distribution among Reproductive age Women of a Hilly District of Nepal
作者： Rajesh Kumar Gupta, Kopila Shrestha, Bhawna Bhandari, Binod Manandhar, Krishna Das Manandhar
摘要中文翻译背景:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,特别是在获得预防服务的机会有限的低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC)。本研究旨在评估尼泊尔帕尔帕区育龄妇女的 高危型HPV 患病率 和基因型分布,并将其与人口统计和临床数据进行比较。方法:采用小型、社区非概率便利抽样方法收集数据,其中从帕尔帕区拉姆普尔和兰巴市 2,331 名 20 至 45 岁的妇女采集了宫颈拭子,这些妇女参加了 2017 年举办的健康营。2025年2月7日至2025年10月7日,在获得尼泊尔健康研究委员会(NHRC)的伦理批准后。在 2,331 个宫颈拭子样本中,由于使用核酸提取试剂盒进行 HPV DNA 检测以及使用 人乳头瘤病毒 核酸扩增检测试剂盒通过 RT-PCR 鉴定的基因型存在分析前错误,仅对 2,024 个拭子样本进行了处理。结果:参与者的 HPV 阳性率为 3.5%。其他HPV基因型(非16/18)在HPV阳性参与者35(49.3%)中最常见,其次是HPV 16基因型(29.5%)和HPV 18(16.9%)。35-40岁年龄段的女性HPV阳性病例比例最高(36例)。HPV 感染与存在宫颈病变之间的逻辑回归得出的优势比为 2.999,表明有宫颈病变的女性感染 HPV 的可能性是没有宫颈病变的女性的近三倍。结论:本研究HPV阳性率较低,以非16/18基因型为主。35-40岁女性HPV阳性率最高,且HPV感染在有宫颈病变的女性中更为常见。基因型;高风险——HPV;实时PCR。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是低收入和中等收入国家的主要公共卫生负担,这些国家获得预防服务的机会有限,而且致癌 HPV 基因型在尼泊尔特定地区的分布记录很少。
设计与对象
一项基于社区的非概率便利抽样研究收集了 2025 年 2 月 7 日至 2025 年 10 月 7 日期间参加帕尔帕区拉姆普尔市和拉姆巴市健康营的 2,331 名 20-45 岁女性的宫颈拭子。
核心方法
使用核酸提取试剂盒对宫颈拭子进行 HPV DNA 检测,并使用 人乳头瘤病毒 核酸扩增检测试剂盒通过 RT-PCR 鉴定基因型。
主要结果
参与者中 HPV 阳性率为 3.5%,其中 49.3% 的 HPV 阳性病例检测到非 16/18 基因型,HPV 16 占 29.5%,HPV 18 占 16.9%。35-40岁女性HPV阳性病例比例最高(36例)。
解释与创新
作者将 HPV 总体阳性率较低和非 16/18 基因型占主导地位解释为与基因型特异性 疫苗接种 和筛查策略局部相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为基因型分布数据稀缺的尼泊尔山区的针对性 HPV 疫苗接种 计划和基因型筛查算法提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
307 个样本的方便抽样和分析前误差限制了普遍性,并且横截面设计排除了因果推断;证据强度中等。

Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Proportion and Genotype Distribution among Reproductive age Women of a Hilly District of Nepal

Authors: Rajesh Kumar Gupta, Kopila Shrestha, Bhawna Bhandari, Binod Manandhar, Krishna Das Manandhar
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is a leading cause of cervical cancer, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) with limited access to preventive services. This study was aimed to assess high-risk HPV prevalence and genotype distribution among reproductive-age women in Palpa district of Nepal and compare them with demographic and clinical data.Methods: A small, community based, non-probability convenience sampling approach was used for data collection in which cervical swabs were collected from 2,331 women aged 20 to 45 years from Rampur and Rambha municipalities of Palpa district, who attended the health camps organized from 7th February 2025 to 7th October 2025, after obtaining ethical approval from the Nepal Health Research Council (NHRC), Nepal. Out of 2,331 cervical swab samples, 2,024 swab samples were only processed due to pre-analytical errors for HPV DNA detection using the Nucleic Acid Extraction Kit and genotypes identified through RT-PCR using the Human Papillomavirus Nucleic Acid Amplification Test Kit. Results: HPV positivity among participants were found to be 3.5%. Other HPV genotype (non 16/18) were most frequently observed among HPV positive participant 35(49.3%), followed by HPV 16 genotype (29.5%) and HPV 18 (16.9%). The women between 35–40 years age group had the highest proportion of HPV positive cases (36 cases). The logistic regression between HPV infection and the presence of cervical lesions yielded an odds ratio of 2.999, indicating that women with cervical lesions were nearly three times more likely to have HPV infection than those without lesions. Conclusions: The study showed low HPV positive proportion rate, with non-16/18 genotypes predominating. Women aged 35–40 years had the highest HPV positivity, and HPV infection was found to be more common among women with cervical lesions.Keywords: Cervical swabs; genotypes; High risk- HPV; real time PCR.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a major public-health burden in low- and middle-income countries with limited access to preventive services, and the distribution of oncogenic HPV genotypes in specific districts of Nepal was poorly documented.
Design and population
A community-based, non-probability convenience sampling study collected cervical swabs from 2,331 women aged 20–45 years attending health camps in Rampur and Rambha municipalities of Palpa district between 7 February 2025 and 7 October 2025.
Core methods
Cervical swabs were processed for HPV DNA detection using a Nucleic Acid Extraction Kit and genotypes identified by RT-PCR with a Human Papillomavirus Nucleic Acid Amplification Test Kit.
Main results
HPV positivity was 3.5% among participants, with non-16/18 genotypes detected in 49.3% of HPV-positive cases, HPV 16 in 29.5% and HPV 18 in 16.9%. Women aged 35–40 years had the highest proportion of HPV-positive cases (36 cases).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the low overall HPV positivity and the predominance of non-16/18 genotypes as locally relevant for genotype-specific vaccination and screening strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform targeted HPV vaccination programs and genotype-informed screening algorithms in hilly districts of Nepal where genotype distribution data are scarce.
Limitations and evidence strength
Convenience sampling and pre-analytical errors in 307 samples limit generalisability, and the cross-sectional design precludes causal inference; evidence strength is moderate.

高危型HPV 细胞学阴性的阳性临床意义:可以安全地推迟进一步评估吗?

Clinical Significance of High-Risk HPV Positivity with Negative Cytology: Can Further Evaluation Be Safely Delayed?
作者： Aleksandar R. Zivadinovic, Aleksandra N. Petrić, Lazar R. Živadinović, Ivan R. Ilić, Dušan D. Simić, Sonja Z. Pop-Trajković Dinić, Milan S. Trenkić, Jelena D. Milošević Stevanović, Nataša K. Rančić, Aleksandar R. Živadinović
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:初次 HPV 筛查对于检测宫颈癌前病变和恶性宫颈病变具有较高的敏感性。然而,由于其特异性相对较低,这种筛查方法需要分类测试。由于其高度特异性,细胞学最常用于 HPV 阳性结果的分类。尽管如此,鉴于细胞学的敏感性相对较低,问题仍然是细胞学在分类这些病例时的可靠性如何。本研究评估了细胞学结果阴性或低级别女性中 高危型HPV (hrHPV) 阳性的临床意义,并评估是否可以使用 HPV 基因分型、阴道镜检查和 p16/Ki-67 生物标志物安全地推迟进一步评估。材料和方法:对 125 名 hrHPV 宫颈检测呈阳性的女性进行了一项前瞻性医院观察研究。所有参与者均接受了常规细胞学检查、扩展 HPV 基因分型、阴道镜检查以及通​​过活检或环切除进行的组织病理学评估。对组织病理学组织样本进行 p16 和 Ki-67 表达的免疫组织化学分析。特别关注上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤(NILM)阴性和低级别细胞学检查结果的女性。分析了细胞学、HPV 基因型分布、阴道镜检查结果、组织病理学、吸烟状况和生物标志物表达之间的关联。结果:在进行 NILM 细胞学检查的 HPV 阳性患者中,28.0% 的病例经组织病理学证实为 CIN2+ 病变。在 HPV 阳性女性中检测到的所有 CIN2+ 病变中,超过一半 (51.4%) 是在细胞学低级别或阴性的患者中发现的。HPV16/18 阳性在患有 CIN2+ 病变和低级别细胞学检查结果的女性中非常普遍 (78.4%)。在超过一半的患者中,阴道镜检查结果不满意,鳞柱状交界处不可见(3 型转化区,TZ3)。免疫组织化学标记物 p16 和 Ki-67 在检测具有低级别细胞学检查(NILM、ASC-US 和 LSIL)的 hrHPV 阳性女性中的 CIN2+ 病变时表现出高灵敏度 (95.5%) 和阴性预测值 (93.3%)。在该患者组中,吸烟状况与 CIN2+ 病变显着相关 (p < 0.001)。结论:仅根据阴性细胞学结果推迟对 hrHPV 阳性女性进行进一步评估可能是不安全的,特别是对于老年女性、吸烟者和 HPV16/18 阳性女性。p16/Ki-67 阳性与 CIN2+ 病变之间的密切相关性表明,直接对细胞学样本进行双染色检测可以显着改善分诊策略并减少侵入性操作的需要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
初级 HPV 筛查可检测宫颈癌前病变和恶性宫颈病变,敏感性高但特异性低,需要进行细胞学等分类测试。该研究探讨了细胞学对细胞学结果阴性或低级别 hrHPV 阳性女性进行分类的可靠性,以及是否可以安全地推迟进一步评估。
设计与对象
对 125 名 hrHPV 宫颈检测呈阳性的女性进行了一项前瞻性医院观察研究。参与者接受了常规细胞学检查、扩展 HPV 基因分型、阴道镜检查和组织病理学评估,并对 p16 和 Ki-67 表达进行免疫组织化学分析。
核心方法
进行了常规细胞学检查、扩展 HPV 基因分型、阴道镜检查以及通​​过活检或环切除进行的组织病理学评估。对组织病理学组织样本进行 p16 和 Ki-67 表达的免疫组织化学分析,并分析细胞学、HPV 基因型分布、阴道镜检查结果、组织病理学、吸烟状况和生物标志物表达之间的关联。
主要结果
在进行 NILM 细胞学检查的 HPV 阳性患者中,28.0% 的病例确诊为 CIN2+ 病变。在 HPV 阳性女性中,超过一半 (51.4%) 的 CIN2+ 病变是在细胞学级别低或阴性的患者中发现的。HPV16/18 阳性在 CIN2+ 病变和低级别细胞学检查的女性中普遍存在 (78.4%)。
解释与创新
该研究解释说,仅根据阴性细胞学结果推迟对 hrHPV 阳性女性进行进一步评估可能是不安全的,特别是对于老年女性、吸烟者和 HPV16/18 阳性女性。p16/Ki-67 阳性与 CIN2+ 病变之间的密切相关性表明双染色测试可以改善分诊策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果告诫不要延迟对细胞学阴性的 hrHPV 阳性女性进行评估,尤其是在高危亚组中,并支持使用 p16/Ki-67 双染色检测来完善分诊并减少侵入性操作。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心、基于医院的设计和小样本量而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于观察性质和潜在的选择偏差,证据强度中等。

Clinical Significance of High-Risk HPV Positivity with Negative Cytology: Can Further Evaluation Be Safely Delayed?

Authors: Aleksandar R. Zivadinovic, Aleksandra N. Petrić, Lazar R. Živadinović, Ivan R. Ilić, Dušan D. Simić, Sonja Z. Pop-Trajković Dinić, Milan S. Trenkić, Jelena D. Milošević Stevanović, Nataša K. Rančić, Aleksandar R. Živadinović
Original abstract (English)
Background and Objectives: Primary HPV screening has high sensitivity in detecting premalignant and malignant cervical lesions. However, due to its relatively low specificity, this screening approach requires triage tests. Owing to its high specificity, cytology is most commonly employed in the triage of HPV-positive results. Nevertheless, the question remains as to how reliable cytology is in triaging these cases, given its relatively low sensitivity. This study evaluates the clinical significance of high-risk HPV (hrHPV) positivity in women with negative or low-grade cytological findings and assesses whether further evaluation can be safely deferred using HPV genotyping, colposcopy, and p16/Ki-67 biomarkers. Materials and Methods: A prospective hospital-based observational study was conducted on 125 women with a positive hrHPV cervical test. All participants underwent conventional cytology, extended HPV genotyping, colposcopy, and histopathological evaluation via biopsy or loop excision. Immunohistochemical analysis of p16 and Ki-67 expressions was performed on histopathological tissue samples. Particular attention was focused on women with negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM) and low-grade cytological findings. Associations between cytology, HPV genotype distribution, colposcopic findings, histopathology, smoking status, and biomarker expression were analyzed. Results: Among HPV-positive patients with NILM cytology, CIN2+ lesions were histopathologically confirmed in 28.0% of cases. More than half (51.4%) of all CIN2+ lesions detected in HPV-positive women were identified in patients with low-grade or negative cytology. HPV16/18 positivity was highly prevalent among women with CIN2+ lesions and low-grade cytological findings (78.4%). Unsatisfactory colposcopy with a non-visible squamocolumnar junction (transformation zone type 3, TZ3) was identified in more than half of these patients. Immunohistochemical markers p16 and Ki-67 demonstrated high sensitivity (95.5%) and negative predictive value (93.3%) for detecting CIN2+ lesions in hrHPV-positive women with low-grade cytology (NILM, ASC-US, and LSIL). Smoking status was significantly associated with CIN2+ lesions in this patient group (p < 0.001). Conclusions: Deferring further evaluation of hrHPV-positive women solely based on negative cytology may be unsafe, particularly in older women, smokers, and those positive for HPV16/18. The strong association between p16/Ki-67 positivity and CIN2+ lesions suggests that dual-stain testing directly from cytological samples could significantly improve triage strategies and reduce the need for invasive procedures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Primary HPV screening detects premalignant and malignant cervical lesions with high sensitivity but low specificity, necessitating triage tests such as cytology. The study addresses the reliability of cytology in triaging hrHPV-positive women with negative or low-grade cytological findings and whether further evaluation can be safely deferred.
Design and population
A prospective hospital-based observational study was conducted on 125 women with a positive hrHPV cervical test. Participants underwent conventional cytology, extended HPV genotyping, colposcopy, and histopathological evaluation, with immunohistochemical analysis of p16 and Ki-67 expressions.
Core methods
Conventional cytology, extended HPV genotyping, colposcopy, and histopathological evaluation via biopsy or loop excision were performed. Immunohistochemical analysis of p16 and Ki-67 expressions was conducted on histopathological tissue samples, and associations between cytology, HPV genotype distribution, colposcopic findings, histopathology, smoking status, and biomarker expression were analyzed.
Main results
Among HPV-positive patients with NILM cytology, CIN2+ lesions were confirmed in 28.0% of cases. More than half (51.4%) of all CIN2+ lesions in HPV-positive women were identified in patients with low-grade or negative cytology. HPV16/18 positivity was prevalent among women with CIN2+ lesions and low-grade cytology (78.4%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study interprets that deferring further evaluation of hrHPV-positive women based solely on negative cytology may be unsafe, particularly in older women, smokers, and those positive for HPV16/18. The strong association between p16/Ki-67 positivity and CIN2+ lesions suggests that dual-stain testing could improve triage strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against delaying evaluation in hrHPV-positive women with negative cytology, especially in high-risk subgroups, and support the use of p16/Ki-67 dual-stain testing to refine triage and reduce invasive procedures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-centre, hospital-based design and small sample size, which may affect generalisability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature and potential selection bias.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2015年至2024年伊朗中部伊斯法罕女性人乳头瘤病毒类型患病率的回顾性研究

A retrospective study on the prevalence of human papillomavirus types in women in Isfahan, central Iran between 2015 and 2024
作者： Bahram Bagherpour, Rasool Jafari, Alireza Hassanpour, Sevda Valilou, Mohammadreza Valilou, Marzieh Safari
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起最流行的性传播感染,也是 宫颈癌 的主要原因。尽管伊朗发布了几份区域报告,但有关伊朗中部伊斯法罕妇女中 HPV 患病率 和基因型分布的全面和最新数据仍然有限。本研究旨在确定转介至伊朗中部伊斯法罕医学实验室进行生殖器 HPV 筛查的女性记录中 HPV 感染的 患病率 和 HPV 类型。在这项回顾性研究中,收集、分析和报告了 2015 年至 2024 年间在伊斯法罕转诊实验室筛查的 4,973 名女性的 HPV PCR 和基因分型结果及其可用的人口统计数据。在接受筛查的 4,973 名女性中,819 名 (16.5%) 检测出 HPV 呈阳性。HPV6 是最常见的基因型(9.2%)。在HPV阳性女性中,高危型的发生率略高于低危型(55.2% vs. 44.8%),其中HPV16(14.4%)是最常见的高危基因型,其次是HPV53(13.2%)和HPV18(6.2%)。20 岁以下女性的 HPV 阳性率最高(27.3%;P = 0.002)。根据本研究的结果,伊斯法罕的 HPV 感染率很高,但与伊朗其他几个地区报告的情况相当。在伊斯法罕地区,超过一半的HPV阳性女性感染了高危型。在受测人群中,20岁以下的女性阳性率最高。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是最常见的性传播感染,也是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,尽管有区域报告,但伊朗中部伊斯法罕的 HPV 患病率 和基因型分布的全面和最新数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
一项回顾性研究分析了 2015 年至 2024 年间在伊朗中部伊斯法罕转诊实验室筛查的 4,973 名女性的 HPV PCR 和基因分型结果。
核心方法
该研究收集并分析了 HPV PCR 和基因分型结果以及转介进行生殖器 HPV 筛查的女性的可用人口统计数据。
主要结果
在接受筛查的 4,973 名女性中,819 名 (16.5%) 检测出 HPV 阳性,其中 HPV6 是最常见的基因型 (9.2%)。在HPV阳性女性中,高危型的发生率略高于低危型(55.2% vs. 44.8%),其中HPV16(14.4%)是最常见的高危基因型,其次是HPV53(13.2%)和HPV18(6.2%)。20 岁以下女性的 HPV 阳性率最高(27.3%;P = 0.002)。
解释与创新
作者将伊斯法罕的高 HPV 感染率解释为与伊朗其他地区相当,并指出超过一半的 HPV 阳性女性感染了高危类型,凸显了人群中致癌 HPV 的负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,伊斯法罕 HPV 感染和高风险基因型的负担相当大,这可能为当地的 疫苗接种 策略和 宫颈癌 筛查计划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对单一转诊实验室的依赖限制了普遍性,并且缺乏临床结果或纵向随访降低了因果推断的证据强度。

A retrospective study on the prevalence of human papillomavirus types in women in Isfahan, central Iran between 2015 and 2024

Authors: Bahram Bagherpour, Rasool Jafari, Alireza Hassanpour, Sevda Valilou, Mohammadreza Valilou, Marzieh Safari
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes the most prevalent sexually transmitted infection and is the principal cause of cervical cancer. Despite several regional reports from Iran, comprehensive and updated data on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution among women in Isfahan, central Iran, remain limited. The present study aimed to determine the prevalence of HPV infection and HPV types in the recordings of women referred for genital HPV screening to a Medical Laboratory in Isfahan, central Iran. In this retrospective study, HPV PCR and genotyping results of 4,973 women and their available demographic data screened between 2015 and 2024 at a referral laboratory in Isfahan were collected, analyzed and reported. Of 4,973 women screened, 819 (16.5%) tested HPV-positive. HPV6 was the most common genotype (9.2%). Among HPV-positive women, high-risk types were slightly more frequent than low-risk types (55.2% vs. 44.8%), with HPV16 (14.4%) the most prevalent high-risk genotype, followed by HPV53 (13.2%) and HPV18 (6.2%). HPV positivity was highest in women under 20 years of age (27.3%; P = 0.002). Based on the results of the present study, the rate of HPV infection in Isfahan was high, yet comparable to that reported in several other regions in Iran. More than half of HPV-positive women were infected with high-risk types in Isfahan area. Among the tested population, the highest positivity was observed in women under 20 years old.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infection and a principal cause of cervical cancer, yet comprehensive and updated data on HPV prevalence and genotype distribution in Isfahan, central Iran, remain limited despite regional reports.
Design and population
A retrospective study analyzed HPV PCR and genotyping results from 4,973 women screened between 2015 and 2024 at a referral laboratory in Isfahan, central Iran.
Core methods
The study collected and analyzed HPV PCR and genotyping results alongside available demographic data from women referred for genital HPV screening.
Main results
Of 4,973 women screened, 819 (16.5%) tested HPV-positive, with HPV6 being the most common genotype (9.2%). Among HPV-positive women, high-risk types were slightly more frequent than low-risk types (55.2% vs. 44.8%), with HPV16 (14.4%) the most prevalent high-risk genotype, followed by HPV53 (13.2%) and HPV18 (6.2%). HPV positivity was highest in women under 20 years of age (27.3%; P = 0.002).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the high HPV infection rate in Isfahan as comparable to other regions in Iran and noted that more than half of HPV-positive women were infected with high-risk types, highlighting the burden of oncogenic HPV in the population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest a substantial burden of HPV infection and high-risk genotypes in Isfahan, which may inform local vaccination strategies and cervical cancer screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on a single referral laboratory limit generalizability, and the absence of clinical outcomes or longitudinal follow-up reduces the strength of evidence for causal inference.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 病毒载量与不同亚型宫颈病变之间的相关性以及临床预测模型的开发

Correlation between high-risk Human Papillomavirus (HPV) viral load and cervical lesions across different subtypes, along with the development of a clinical prediction model
作者： Hongyi Chen, Chenxi Yang, Hongwei Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 探讨HPV16/18及其他16种高危型HPV型(指定为非16/18组)病毒载量与宫颈病变严重程度的相关性,建立HSIL+风险预测模型,为临床实用分诊和精准管理策略提供依据。方法对2025年7月至2026年1月收集的331例HR-HPV阳性患者的资料进行回顾性分析,采用半定量混合捕获化学发光平台测定HPV16/18组和非16/18组的病毒载量,并根据病理结果将患者分为正常组、LSIL组、HSIL组和宫颈癌组。采用多变量逻辑回归分析来检查病毒载量、HPV 亚型、年龄和病变严重程度之间的关联,并构建 HSIL+ 预测列线图。结果 HPV16/18感染的患病率随着病变严重程度的增加而升高(HSIL:OR=6.50;宫颈癌:OR=23.28),呈现出“持续增强”的模式。相反,非16/18组的病毒载量在LSIL和HSIL阶段增加,但在癌症阶段下降,表现出“先受累后减弱”的模式。虽然单一非 16/18 群体感染表现出早期预警价值,但 HPV16/18 型和中至高病毒载量被确定为 HSIL+ 的独立危险因素。列线图经过 Bootstrap 验证,AUC 为 0.763,表明校准令人满意。结论本研究全面分析了HPV16/18及其他高危亚型相关病毒载量的致病特征,阐明了不同亚型的病变模式。虽然 HPV16/18 是主要的持续驱动因素,但非 16/18 组的高病毒载量在癌前阶段提供了关键的“机会之窗”迹象。设计了实用的 HSIL+ 风险分层列线图,作为早期识别高风险病变的可用临床辅助工具,从而优化现实世界的个性化管理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),特别是 HPV16/18 的持续感染是宫颈癌发生的主要驱动因素,但不同 HPV 亚型的病毒载量与病变严重程度之间的关系仍未完全确定。该研究试图量化这种关联,并得出一种临床工具来指导 HPV 阳性患者的分类。
设计与对象
对2025年7月至2026年1月期间收集的331名高危型HPV阳性患者进行回顾性分析。患者根据HPV亚型(HPV16/18与其他16种高危类型)和宫颈病变严重程度分为正常组、LSIL组、HSIL组和宫颈癌组。
核心方法
使用半定量混合捕获-化学发光平台测量 HPV16/18 和非 16/18 组的病毒载量。使用多变量逻辑回归评估病毒载量、HPV 亚型、年龄和病变严重程度之间的关联,并构建 HSIL+ 风险预测列线图并通过 bootstrap 进行验证。
主要结果
HPV16/18 感染 患病率 随着病变严重程度而增加(HSIL:OR=6.50;宫颈癌:OR=23.28),而非 16/18 病毒载量在 LSIL 和 HSIL 期间上升,但在癌症中下降。HPV16/18型和中高病毒载量被确定为HSIL+的独立危险因素,列线图的AUC为0.763。
解释与创新
作者将 HPV16/18 解释为进展的主要持续驱动因素,而高非 16/18 病毒载量则标志着关键的癌前窗口。他们报告了一种新颖的 HSIL+ 风险分层列线图,该列线图整合了亚型特异性病毒载量和年龄,以进行实用的临床分诊。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持基于亚型的病毒载量分类,优先考虑 高危型HPV 阳性患者进行早期阴道镜检查或干预,从而有可能减少过度治疗并改善 宫颈癌 预防计划的资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计、单中心抽样以及对半定量病毒载量测量的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性;自举验证产生了中等辨别力(AUC 0.763),表明证据强度中等。

Correlation between high-risk Human Papillomavirus (HPV) viral load and cervical lesions across different subtypes, along with the development of a clinical prediction model

Authors: Hongyi Chen, Chenxi Yang, Hongwei Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective The study aims to investigate the correlation between the viral loads of HPV16/18 and 16 other high-risk HPV types (designated as non-16/18 group) and the severity of cervical lesions, and to develop a HSIL+ risk prediction model to furnish evidence for pragmatic clinical triage and precision management strategies. Methods A retrospective analysis was performed on the data from 331 HR-HPV-positive patients collected between July 2025 and January 2026. Using a semi-quantitative hybrid capture-chemiluminescence platform,The viral loads of HPV16/18 and non-16/18 group were measured, and patients were categorized into normal, LSIL, HSIL, and cervical cancer groups based on pathological findings. Multivariate logistic regression analysis was employed to examine the associations among viral load, HPV subtypes, age, and lesion severity, and a HSIL+ prediction nomogram was constructed. Results The prevalence of HPV16/18 infection escalated with increasing lesion severity (HSIL: OR=6.50; cervical cancer: OR=23.28), demonstrating a pattern of “continuous augmentation”. Conversely, the viral load of non-16/18 group increased during the LSIL and HSIL stages but declined in the cancer stage, exhibiting a pattern of “early involvement followed by later attenuation”. While single non-16/18 group infections demonstrated early warning value, HPV16/18 types and medium to high viral loads were identified as independent risk factors for HSIL+. The nomogram underwent Bootstrap validation and yielded an AUC of 0.763, indicating satisfactory calibration. Conclusion This study comprehensively analyzed the pathogenic features of viral loads associated with HPV16/18 and other high-risk subtypes, elucidating the lesion patterns of distinct subtypes. While HPV16/18 acts as the principal persistent driver, the high viral load of the non-16/18 group provides a critical "window-of-opportunity" indication during the precancerous phase. A pragmatic HSIL+ risk-stratification nomogram was devised to serve as an accessible clinical aid for the early identification of high-risk lesions, thereby optimizing real-world personalized management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPV), particularly HPV16/18, is a principal driver of cervical carcinogenesis, yet the relationship between viral load and lesion severity across HPV subtypes remains incompletely defined. The study sought to quantify this association and to derive a clinical tool to guide triage of HPV-positive patients.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on 331 high-risk HPV-positive patients whose data were collected between July 2025 and January 2026. Patients were stratified by HPV subtype (HPV16/18 versus 16 other high-risk types) and by cervical lesion severity into normal, LSIL, HSIL, and cervical cancer groups.
Core methods
Viral loads for HPV16/18 and the non-16/18 group were measured using a semi-quantitative hybrid capture–chemiluminescence platform. Multivariate logistic regression was used to assess associations among viral load, HPV subtype, age, and lesion severity, and a HSIL+ risk prediction nomogram was constructed and validated by bootstrap.
Main results
HPV16/18 infection prevalence increased with lesion severity (HSIL: OR=6.50; cervical cancer: OR=23.28), while non-16/18 viral load rose during LSIL and HSIL but declined in cancer. HPV16/18 types and medium-to-high viral loads were identified as independent risk factors for HSIL+, and the nomogram achieved an AUC of 0.763.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret HPV16/18 as the principal persistent driver of progression, whereas high non-16/18 viral load signals a critical precancerous window. They report a novel HSIL+ risk-stratification nomogram that integrates subtype-specific viral load and age for pragmatic clinical triage.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support subtype-aware, viral-load–based triage to prioritize high-risk HPV-positive patients for early colposcopy or intervention, potentially reducing overtreatment and improving resource allocation in cervical cancer prevention programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design, single-center sampling, and reliance on semi-quantitative viral load measurement limit causal inference and generalizability; bootstrap validation yielded moderate discrimination (AUC 0.763), indicating moderate evidence strength.

通过公共卫生传播策略解决印度对 HPV 疫苗 的犹豫和错误信息。

Addressing HPV vaccine hesitancy and misinformation in India through public health communication strategies.
作者： Robin Jindal, R R, Naman Ghanghas, Raktima Gupta, Ritu Arya, Rubal Kanozia, Trishu Sharma, Ragendu R
摘要中文翻译摘要 印度在 宫颈癌 预防和 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 覆盖方面取得了重大进展,在全国范围内推出了旨在为 14 岁女孩接种疫苗的免费 HPV 疫苗接种 活动。然而,如果不及时采取措施解决 疫苗 犹豫不决和相关错误信息的问题,该活动的成功率可能会大大降低。本文讨论了 HPV 疫苗 错误信息的影响,确定了全球 疫苗 犹豫因素,并重点介绍了影响 HPV 疫苗 犹豫及其在印度覆盖范围的行为决定因素。报告指出,印度青少年对 疫苗 犹豫不决和 疫苗 接受率低的关键挑战是认知度低、误解、社会文化信仰、耻辱、安全问题、禁令规范薄弱、父母抵制、城乡差异和卫生系统障碍。它利用综合的健康信念模型 (HBM) 和 COM-B 理论框架,提出了一种文化敏感、基于证据的多因素沟通策略,重点是提高认识、建立信任、反击错误信息、医疗保健提供者的能力建设以及增加采取 HPV 预防行为的机会。评论强调,需要积极吸引家长、青少年、医疗保健提供者、教育工作者、影响者、媒体和社区成员参与,以增强 疫苗 信心、社会规范,并支持印度更广泛的 疫苗 预防目标,与世界卫生组织到 2030 年实现 90-70-90 的目标保持一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本评论讨论了印度对 HPV 疫苗 犹豫不决和错误信息的挑战,强调需要制定有针对性的公共卫生传播策略,以提高 疫苗 覆盖率和 宫颈癌 预防。
设计与对象
该研究是对印度全国免费 HPV 疫苗接种 活动的评论性分析,重点关注影响青少年对 疫苗 犹豫不决的因素以及更广泛的公共卫生影响。
核心方法
该评论采用综合健康信念模型 (HBM) 和 COM-B 理论框架,提出了一种文化敏感、基于证据的沟通策略,旨在解决 疫苗 的犹豫和错误信息。
主要结果
该评论指出了影响印度 HPV 疫苗 感染的主要挑战,例如认识不足、误解、社会文化信仰、耻辱、安全担忧、禁令规范薄弱、家长抵制、城乡差异以及卫生系统障碍。
解释与创新
作者将这些挑战解释为到 2030 年实现 WHO 90-70-90 宫颈癌 预防目标的关键障碍,他们提出了一种多因素沟通策略作为解决这些问题的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为制定适合文化的公共卫生传播策略提供了信息,以增强 HPV 疫苗 信心、社会规范,并支持印度的 宫颈癌 预防目标。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象分析,这限制了证据的深度。然而,所提出的策略是以既定的理论框架为基础的,对其适用性提供了一定程度的信心。

Addressing HPV vaccine hesitancy and misinformation in India through public health communication strategies.

Authors: Robin Jindal, R R, Naman Ghanghas, Raktima Gupta, Ritu Arya, Rubal Kanozia, Trishu Sharma, Ragendu R
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT India witnessed a significant step forward in cervical cancer prevention and human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine coverage with the rollout of a nationwide free HPV vaccination drive aimed at vaccinating 14‐y‐old girls. However, the campaign’s success rate can be significantly reduced if timely measures to tackle vaccine hesitancy and related misinformation are not taken into account. This commentary article discusses the effects of HPV vaccine misinformation, identifies global vaccine hesitancy factors, and highlights behavioral determinants affecting HPV vaccine hesitancy and its coverage in India. It identifies low awareness, misconceptions, sociocultural beliefs, stigma, safety concerns, weak injunctive norms, parental resistance, urban–rural disparities, and health system barriers as key challenges associated with vaccine hesitancy and low vaccine uptake among adolescents in India. Using an integrated Health Belief Model (HBM) and COM-B theoretical framework, it proposes a culturally sensitive, evidence-based multifactorial communication strategy focused on raising awareness, building trust, countering misinformation, capacity building of healthcare providers, and enhancing opportunities for the adoption of HPV preventive behavior. The commentary highlights the need to actively engage parents, adolescents, healthcare providers, educators, influencers, media, and community members to strengthen vaccine confidence, social norms, and support India’s broader cervical cancer prevention goals in alignment with WHO’s 90–70–90 targets by 2030.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This commentary addresses the challenges of HPV vaccine hesitancy and misinformation in India, emphasizing the need for targeted public health communication strategies to improve vaccine coverage and cervical cancer prevention.
Design and population
The study is a commentary analyzing the nationwide free HPV vaccination drive in India, focusing on the factors influencing vaccine hesitancy among adolescents and the broader public health implications.
Core methods
The commentary employs an integrated Health Belief Model (HBM) and COM-B theoretical framework to propose a culturally sensitive, evidence-based communication strategy aimed at addressing vaccine hesitancy and misinformation.
Main results
The commentary identifies key challenges such as low awareness, misconceptions, sociocultural beliefs, stigma, safety concerns, weak injunctive norms, parental resistance, urban–rural disparities, and health system barriers affecting HPV vaccine uptake in India.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these challenges as critical barriers to achieving the WHO’s 90–70–90 targets for cervical cancer prevention by 2030, and they propose a multifactorial communication strategy as a novel approach to address these issues.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of culturally tailored public health communication strategies to enhance HPV vaccine confidence, social norms, and support India’s cervical cancer prevention goals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only analysis, which limits the depth of evidence. However, the proposed strategy is grounded in established theoretical frameworks, providing a moderate level of confidence in its applicability.

模拟宣传活动对 HPV 传播 动态的影响:神经网络增强的数学模型。

Modelling the impact of awareness campaigns on HPV transmission dynamics: a neural network-enhanced mathematical model.
作者： Kamil Shah, Changqing Du, Ali Akgül, Fahad Sameer Alshammari, Sanaa Ahmed Bajri, Hamiden Abd El-Wahed Khalifa
摘要中文翻译HPV 是最常见的性传播感染;大多数性活跃的人在一生中的某个阶段都会接触 HPV,并且大多数感染会通过免疫系统自然消退。然而,高危型HPV 类型的持续感染是全球 宫颈癌 的主要原因,这凸显了 疫苗接种 和早期检测对于减轻疾病负担的重要性。在这项研究中,通过使用神经网络的数学模型研究了宣传活动对 人乳头瘤病毒 感染动态的影响。考察了该模型的基本特征、有界性、流行与无病平衡点以及繁殖数。使用再生数,还研究了模型的稳定性。此外,还进行敏感性分析以确定影响 HPV 传播和控制的最敏感参数。首先使用基于Runge-Kutta方法的ode45求解器获得了HPV 传播模型的确定性数值解;然后将所得的解决方案轨迹用作前馈神经网络的训练数据,该网络充当代理近似模型来验证和重现系统动态。数值模拟检查不同水平的 疫苗接种 覆盖率、公众意识和宫颈感染率的均方误差、验证性能、梯度和 mu。数值模拟表明,疫苗接种 和对 人乳头瘤病毒 感染风险的认识在控制病毒在人群中的传播方面发挥着至关重要的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
HPV 是最常见的性传播感染;大多数性活跃的人在一生中的某个阶段都会接触 HPV,并且大多数感染会通过免疫系统自然消退。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,通过使用神经网络的数学模型研究了宣传活动对 人乳头瘤病毒 感染动态的影响。
核心方法
首先使用基于Runge-Kutta方法的ode45求解器获得了HPV 传播模型的确定性数值解;然后将所得的解决方案轨迹用作前馈神经网络的训练数据,该网络充当代理近似模型来验证和重现系统动态。
主要结果
考察了该模型的基本特征、有界性、流行与无病平衡点以及繁殖数。
解释与创新
使用再生数,还研究了模型的稳定性。
科研与公卫意义
数值模拟表明,疫苗接种 和对 人乳头瘤病毒 感染风险的认识在控制病毒在人群中的传播方面发挥着至关重要的作用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Modelling the impact of awareness campaigns on HPV transmission dynamics: a neural network-enhanced mathematical model.

Authors: Kamil Shah, Changqing Du, Ali Akgül, Fahad Sameer Alshammari, Sanaa Ahmed Bajri, Hamiden Abd El-Wahed Khalifa
Original abstract (English)
HPV is the most common sexually transmitted infection; most sexually active individuals are exposed to HPV at some point in their lives, and the majority of these infections resolve naturally through the immune system. However, persistent infection with high-risk HPV types is the leading cause of cervical cancer worldwide, underscoring the importance of vaccination and early detection in reducing disease burden. In this study, the effect of an awareness campaign on the dynamics of human papillomavirus infection has been investigated through a mathematical model using neural networks. The basic characteristic, boundedness, the endemic and disease-free equilibrium point, and the reproduction number of the model have been investigated. Using the reproduction number, the stability of the model is also investigated. Moreover, a sensitivity analysis is performed to identify the most sensitive parameter affecting the spread and control of HPV. The deterministic numerical solution of the HPV transmission model was first obtained using the ode45 solver based on the Runge-Kutta method; the resulting solution trajectories were then used as training data for a feedforward neural network, which serves as a surrogate approximation model to validate and reproduce the system dynamics. The numerical simulation examines mean square error, validation performance, gradient, and mu across varying levels of vaccination coverage, public awareness, and cervical infection rates. Numerical simulation suggests that vaccination and awareness about the risk of human papillomavirus infection play a crucial role in controlling the spread of the virus in the population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
HPV is the most common sexually transmitted infection; most sexually active individuals are exposed to HPV at some point in their lives, and the majority of these infections resolve naturally through the immune system.
Design and population
In this study, the effect of an awareness campaign on the dynamics of human papillomavirus infection has been investigated through a mathematical model using neural networks.
Core methods
The deterministic numerical solution of the HPV transmission model was first obtained using the ode45 solver based on the Runge-Kutta method; the resulting solution trajectories were then used as training data for a feedforward neural network, which serves as a surrogate approximation model to validate and reproduce the system dynamics.
Main results
The basic characteristic, boundedness, the endemic and disease-free equilibrium point, and the reproduction number of the model have been investigated.
Interpretation and novelty
Using the reproduction number, the stability of the model is also investigated.
Scientific and public-health significance
Numerical simulation suggests that vaccination and awareness about the risk of human papillomavirus infection play a crucial role in controlling the spread of the virus in the population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

应用扩展的信息-动机-行为技能模型来预测中国同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者对 HPV 疫苗 的可接受性。

Application of an extended information-motivation-behavioral skills model to predict HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men.
作者： Qiufeng Liu, Li Ping Wong, Sin How Lim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者 (GBMSM) 中造成巨大的疾病负担,但男性的 HPV 疫苗接种 途径在中国大陆仍在不断发展。
设计与对象
2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 1 月期间,对居住在中国大陆的 1,045 名 GBMSM 进行了一项横断面在线调查。
核心方法
衡量标准包括 HPV 相关信息、健康信念、预期后悔、主观规范、自我效能和 HPV 疫苗 可接受性。测量和结构方程模型估计直接和间接路径。
主要结果
HPV vaccine acceptability was moderate (mean 3.40, SD 1.14). Subjective norms were the strongest predictor of acceptability (β = 0.68, p < .001), with additional positive effects from health beliefs (β = 0.17, p < .01), information (β = 0.14, p < .001), and self-efficacy (β = 0.13, p = .02). Anticipated regret showed a positive indirect effect through self-efficacy (unstandardized B = 0.056, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.12) but a negative direct association with acceptability (β = -0.11, p = .03).
解释与创新
中国 GBMSM 中 HPV 疫苗 的可接受性主要受社会规范影响,自我效能感是心理社会因素与可接受性相关的关键途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对主观规范和自我效能的干预措施可能会提高中国 GBMSM 中 HPV 疫苗 的接受度。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且在线抽样可能会引入选择偏差,降低了对中国大陆所有 GBMSM 的普适性。

Application of an extended information-motivation-behavioral skills model to predict HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men.

Authors: Qiufeng Liu, Li Ping Wong, Sin How Lim
Original abstract (English)
Background: Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes substantial disease burden among gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM), yet HPV vaccination pathways for men remain evolving in mainland China. Purpose: To apply a culturally adapted, extended Information-Motivation-Behavioral Skills (IMB) model to identify psychosocial pathways associated with HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese GBMSM. Methods: A cross-sectional online survey was conducted between December 2024 and January 2025 among 1,045 GBMSM living in mainland China. Measures included HPV-related information, health beliefs, anticipated regret, subjective norms, self-efficacy, and HPV vaccine acceptability. Measurement and structural equation models estimated direct and indirect pathways. Results: HPV vaccine acceptability was moderate (mean 3.40, SD 1.14). Subjective norms were the strongest predictor of acceptability (β = 0.68, p < .001), with additional positive effects from health beliefs (β = 0.17, p < .01), information (β = 0.14, p < .001), and self-efficacy (β = 0.13, p = .02). Anticipated regret showed a positive indirect effect through self-efficacy (unstandardized B = 0.056, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.12) but a negative direct association with acceptability (β = -0.11, p = .03). Conclusions: HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese GBMSM was shaped predominantly by social norms, with self-efficacy acting as a key pathway through which psychosocial factors were associated with acceptability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) causes substantial disease burden among gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men (GBMSM), yet HPV vaccination pathways for men remain evolving in mainland China.
Design and population
A cross-sectional online survey was conducted between December 2024 and January 2025 among 1,045 GBMSM living in mainland China.
Core methods
Measures included HPV-related information, health beliefs, anticipated regret, subjective norms, self-efficacy, and HPV vaccine acceptability. Measurement and structural equation models estimated direct and indirect pathways.
Main results
HPV vaccine acceptability was moderate (mean 3.40, SD 1.14). Subjective norms were the strongest predictor of acceptability (β = 0.68, p < .001), with additional positive effects from health beliefs (β = 0.17, p < .01), information (β = 0.14, p < .001), and self-efficacy (β = 0.13, p = .02). Anticipated regret showed a positive indirect effect through self-efficacy (unstandardized B = 0.056, 95% CI 0.01 to 0.12) but a negative direct association with acceptability (β = -0.11, p = .03).
Interpretation and novelty
HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese GBMSM was shaped predominantly by social norms, with self-efficacy acting as a key pathway through which psychosocial factors were associated with acceptability.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that interventions targeting subjective norms and self-efficacy may increase HPV vaccine acceptability among Chinese GBMSM.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the online sampling may introduce selection bias, reducing generalisability to all GBMSM in mainland China.

单细胞转录组学揭示了 HPV 驱动的免疫逃避,并在 宫颈癌 中建立了基于 TAB2 的预后特征。

Single-cell transcriptomics uncovers HPV-driven immune evasion and establishes a TAB2-based prognostic signature in cervical cancer.
作者： Jiarui Mi, Wenzhe Guo, Li Li, Si Wang, Bei Wang, Yanping Fu, Ning Hu, Haipeng He, Bo Li, Wenyan Wang
摘要中文翻译背景:持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的关键驱动因素,但将 HPV 与免疫逃逸联系起来的分子机制仍不完全清楚。方法:我们整合单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq)、批量转录组和临床数据来分析 宫颈癌 异质性。scRNA-seq、inferCNV 和伪时间分析表征了 HPV 相关的细胞特征和恶性转化。hdWGCNA 鉴定了核心基因模块。使用单变量 Cox、Lasso 和多变量 Cox 回归构建预后风险模型。还进行了 TAB2 的体外功能验证。结果:鉴定出 11 个主要细胞群,其中上皮细胞是 HPV 的主要目标。HPV 相关基因富集随着疾病进展而增加。恶性肿瘤细胞的免疫逃逸评分显着高于非肿瘤细胞,与 HPV 感染水平密切相关 (P < 0.001)。hdWGCNA 确定了与免疫抑制、应激反应和增殖相关的三个模块。八基因风险模型在 TCGA 和 GEO 队列研究 上显示出稳定的预后性能,作为独立的预后因素 (P < 0.05),并与 WNT/TGFβ 通路激活和免疫检查点基因相关。TAB2 敲低显着抑制了 宫颈癌 细胞的体外增殖、迁移和侵袭。结论:这项多组学分析确定了 宫颈癌 中与 HPV 感染和免疫逃逸相关的转录程序。八基因风险模型显示出预后预测和患者分层的潜力,为个体化治疗提供了理论基础。产生的机制假设需要进一步的实验验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的关键驱动因素,但将 HPV 与免疫逃逸联系起来的分子机制仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
我们整合了单细胞 RNA 测序 (scRNA-seq)、批量转录组和临床数据来分析 宫颈癌 异质性。
核心方法
scRNA-seq、inferCNV 和伪时间分析表征了 HPV 相关的细胞特征和恶性转化。hdWGCNA 鉴定了核心基因模块。使用单变量 Cox、Lasso 和多变量 Cox 回归构建预后风险模型。还进行了 TAB2 的体外功能验证。
主要结果
鉴定出 11 个主要细胞群,其中上皮细胞是 HPV 的主要靶标。HPV 相关基因富集随着疾病进展而增加。恶性肿瘤细胞的免疫逃逸评分显着高于非肿瘤细胞,与 HPV 感染水平密切相关 (P < 0.001)。八基因风险模型在 TCGA 和 GEO 队列研究 上显示出稳定的预后性能,作为独立的预后因素 (P < 0.05),并与 WNT/TGFβ 通路激活和免疫检查点基因相关。TAB2 敲低显着抑制了 宫颈癌 细胞的体外增殖、迁移和侵袭。
解释与创新
这种多组学分析确定了 宫颈癌 中与 HPV 感染和免疫逃逸相关的转录程序。产生的机制假设需要进一步的实验验证。
科研与公卫意义
八基因风险模型显示出预后预测和患者分层的潜力,为个体化治疗提供了理论基础。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告研究局限性或样本量详细信息。由于仅依赖摘要数据且缺乏体内验证,证据强度中等。

Single-cell transcriptomics uncovers HPV-driven immune evasion and establishes a TAB2-based prognostic signature in cervical cancer.

Authors: Jiarui Mi, Wenzhe Guo, Li Li, Si Wang, Bei Wang, Yanping Fu, Ning Hu, Haipeng He, Bo Li, Wenyan Wang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a key driver in cervical cancer, yet the molecular mechanisms linking HPV to immune escape remain incompletely understood. METHODS: We integrated single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq), bulk transcriptome, and clinical data to analyze cervical cancer heterogeneity. scRNA-seq, inferCNV, and pseudotime analysis characterized HPV-associated cellular features and malignant transformation. hdWGCNA identified core gene modules. Univariate Cox, Lasso, and multivariate Cox regressions were used to construct a prognostic risk model. In vitro functional validation of TAB2 was also performed. RESULTS: Eleven major cell populations were identified, with epithelial cells as primary HPV targets. HPV-related gene enrichment increased with disease progression. Malignant cells exhibited significantly higher immune escape scores than non-tumor cells, strongly correlating with HPV infection levels (P < 0.001). hdWGCNA identified three modules linked to immunosuppression, stress response, and proliferation. An eight-gene risk model showed stable prognostic performance across TCGA and GEO cohorts, served as an independent prognostic factor (P < 0.05), and correlated with WNT/TGFβ pathway activation and immune checkpoint genes. TAB2 knockdown significantly suppressed cervical cancer cell proliferation, migration, and invasion in vitro. CONCLUSION: This multi-omics analysis identifies transcriptional programs associated with HPV infection and immune escape in cervical cancer. The eight-gene risk model shows potential for prognosis prediction and patient stratification, offering a theoretical basis for individualized treatment. The mechanistic hypotheses generated warrant further experimental validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is a key driver in cervical cancer, yet the molecular mechanisms linking HPV to immune escape remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
We integrated single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq), bulk transcriptome, and clinical data to analyze cervical cancer heterogeneity.
Core methods
scRNA-seq, inferCNV, and pseudotime analysis characterized HPV-associated cellular features and malignant transformation. hdWGCNA identified core gene modules. Univariate Cox, Lasso, and multivariate Cox regressions were used to construct a prognostic risk model. In vitro functional validation of TAB2 was also performed.
Main results
Eleven major cell populations were identified, with epithelial cells as primary HPV targets. HPV-related gene enrichment increased with disease progression. Malignant cells exhibited significantly higher immune escape scores than non-tumor cells, strongly correlating with HPV infection levels (P < 0.001). An eight-gene risk model showed stable prognostic performance across TCGA and GEO cohorts, served as an independent prognostic factor (P < 0.05), and correlated with WNT/TGFβ pathway activation and immune checkpoint genes. TAB2 knockdown significantly suppressed cervical cancer cell proliferation, migration, and invasion in vitro.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations or sample size details. Evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on abstract-only data and absence of in vivo validation.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

全球唇癌和口腔癌:2021 年全球疾病负担研究分析。

Global Lip and Oral Cavity Cancer: Analysis of the 2021 Global Burden of Disease Study.
作者： Shuang Li, Tao Yan, Qun Wang, Quanying Qu, Hao Xu, Na Xu
摘要中文翻译简介和目的:本研究旨在利用2021年全球疾病负担(GBD)研究来分析全球、区域和国家层面(1990-2021年)唇癌和口腔癌(LOCC)疾病负担的趋势;量化人口增长、老龄化和流行病学变化对 发病率、患病率、死亡率 和伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 的影响;评估各国与社会人口指数(SDI)相关的健康不平等;并基于贝叶斯年龄周期-队列研究模型(BAPC)模型预测到2050年的负担趋势。方法:GBD 2021 研究的数据纳入年龄-周期-队列研究 (APC) 模型,以评估年龄、周期和 队列研究 的影响。采用分解分析法确定影响因素。结果:全球 LOCC 负担呈现“北高南重”的双峰分布,且性别差异显着。分解分析表明,老龄化和人口增长导致发病率和 患病率 增加,而流行病学变化则降低 死亡率 和 DALY。使用不平等斜率指数 (SII) 测量的绝对不平等表明,与 SDI 相关的绝对不平等持续存在。同时,集中指数 (CI) 显示 患病率 仍然集中在高 SDI 设置中(CI:0.26-0.13),而 死亡率 和 DALY 则越来越集中在低 SDI 设置中。到 2050 年的预测显示,年龄标准化 发病率 率 (ASIR)、年龄标准化 患病率 率 (ASPR)、年龄标准化 死亡率 率 (ASMR) 和年龄标准化 DALY 率 (ASDR) 呈上升趋势。结论:从 1990 年到 2021 年,全球 LOCC 显示 发病率 和 患病率 增加,同时 死亡率 和 DALY 下降,其中男性负担更高。人口老龄化和 患病率 增加,而流行病学变化则减轻 死亡率。BAPC 预测表明,到 2050 年,所有指标都会有所增加。需要 SDI 分层策略:人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 和高 SDI 区域的筛查;SDI 中部地区的烟草控制;对低 SDI 地区的槟榔液监管和初级保健投资;以及针对唇癌热点的紫外线 (UV) 防护。临床试验编号:不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究讨论了唇和口腔癌 (LOCC) 的全球负担,旨在量化 1990 年至 2021 年 发病率、发病率、死亡率 和残疾调整生命年 (DALY) 的趋势,通过社会人口指数 (SDI) 评估健康不平等,并使用贝叶斯预测未来负担年龄-时期-队列研究 模型 (BAPC)。
设计与对象
该研究使用《2021 年全球疾病负担 (GBD) 研究》的数据,采用年龄阶段 队列研究 (APC) 和分解分析来评估全球、区域和国家层面 LOCC 的趋势和不平等。
核心方法
应用分解分析来量化人口增长、老龄化和流行病学变化对 LOCC 负担的影响,同时使用贝叶斯年龄周期 队列研究 (BAPC) 模型来预测到 2050 年的趋势。
主要结果
全球LOCC负担呈现“北高南重”双峰分布,性别差异显着,分解分析显示老龄化和人口增长增加了发病率和患病率,而流行病学变化降低了死亡率和DALY。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明与 SDI 相关的不平等现象持续存在,患病率 集中在高 SDI 环境中,而 死亡率/DALY 则越来越集中在低 SDI 环境中,并预计到 2050 年所有指标的负担都会增加。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了 SDI 分层策略的必要性,包括高 SDI 地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 和筛查、中 SDI 地区的烟草控制、低 SDI 地区的槟榔监管和初级保健投资,以及唇癌热点地区的紫外线防护。
局限与证据强度
证据受到对 GBD 2021 数据的依赖的限制,该数据可能无法捕捉当地变化或新出现的风险因素,并且预测取决于模型假设,需要对未来趋势进行谨慎解释。

Global Lip and Oral Cavity Cancer: Analysis of the 2021 Global Burden of Disease Study.

Authors: Shuang Li, Tao Yan, Qun Wang, Quanying Qu, Hao Xu, Na Xu
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION AND AIMS: This study aimed to use the Global Burden of Disease (GBD) 2021 Study to analyse the trends in the disease burden of lip and oral cavity cancer (LOCC) at the global, regional, and national levels (1990-2021); to quantify the impacts of population growth, aging, and epidemiological changes on incidence, prevalence, mortality, and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs); assess health inequalities associated with the Socio-demographic Index (SDI) across countries; and predict the burden trends up to 2050 based on the Bayesian age-period-cohort model (BAPC) model. METHODS: Data from the GBD 2021 Study were included in the age-period-cohort (APC) model to evaluate the effects of age, period, and cohort. Decomposition analysis was used to determine the influencing factors. RESULTS: The global burden of LOCC showed a 'high north, heavy south' bimodal distribution with significant sex differences. Decomposition analyses indicated aging and population growth drive increased morbidity and prevalence, whereas epidemiological changes reduce mortality and DALYs. Absolute inequalities measured using the slope index of inequality (SII) revealed indicating persistent SDI-related absolute inequalities. Concurrently, the concentration index (CI) showed that prevalence remained concentrated in high-SDI settings (CI: 0.26-0.13), whereas mortality and DALYs became increasingly concentrated in low-SDI settings. Projections to 2050 show upward trends in age-standardized incidence rate (ASIR), age-standardized prevalence rate (ASPR), age-standardized mortality rate (ASMR), and age-standardized DALYs rate (ASDR). CONCLUSION: Global LOCC showed increasing incidence and prevalence alongside declining mortality and DALYs from 1990 to 2021, with higher burden in males. Population aging and prevalence increases, whereas epidemiological changes mitigated mortality. BAPC projections indicate increases across all indicators by 2050. SDI-stratified strategies are required: human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination and screening for high-SDI regions; tobacco control for middle-SDI regions; betel quid regulation and primary care investment for low-SDI regions; and ultraviolet (UV) protection for lip cancer hotspots. CLINICAL TRIAL NUMBER: Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the global burden of lip and oral cavity cancer (LOCC) and aimed to quantify trends in incidence, prevalence, mortality, and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs) from 1990 to 2021, assess health inequalities by Socio-demographic Index (SDI), and project future burden using the Bayesian age-period-cohort model (BAPC).
Design and population
The study used data from the Global Burden of Disease (GBD) 2021 Study to evaluate trends and inequalities in LOCC at global, regional, and national levels, employing age-period-cohort (APC) and decomposition analyses.
Core methods
Decomposition analysis was applied to quantify the impacts of population growth, aging, and epidemiological changes on LOCC burden, while the Bayesian age-period-cohort (BAPC) model was used to project trends up to 2050.
Main results
Global LOCC burden exhibited a 'high north, heavy south' bimodal distribution with significant sex differences, and decomposition analyses showed aging and population growth increased morbidity and prevalence, whereas epidemiological changes reduced mortality and DALYs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating persistent SDI-related inequalities, with prevalence concentrated in high-SDI settings and mortality/DALYs increasingly concentrated in low-SDI settings, and projected rising burden across all indicators by 2050.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the need for SDI-stratified strategies, including HPV vaccination and screening in high-SDI regions, tobacco control in middle-SDI regions, betel quid regulation and primary care investment in low-SDI regions, and ultraviolet protection for lip cancer hotspots.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by reliance on GBD 2021 data, which may not capture local variations or emerging risk factors, and projections depend on model assumptions, warranting cautious interpretation of future trends.

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.01698-25  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0169825
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

人乳头瘤病毒 的聚合酶环境。

The polymerase milieu of human papillomavirus.
作者： Keira R. Ylvisaker, Gopishankar Thirumoorthy, Emma Wisniewski, Kavi P. M. Mehta
摘要中文翻译摘要 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 会导致宫颈癌、肛门生殖器癌和头颈癌。一个关键的致癌机制涉及病毒利用 宿主 DNA 损伤耐受途径来支持高效复制,这一过程也会导致基因组不稳定。尽管有证据表明特定的细胞聚合酶参与 HPV 基因组 复制,但在病毒生命周期中部署的完整聚合酶仍不清楚。这篇小型综述探讨了复制聚合酶和跨损伤合成 (TLS) 聚合酶在 HPV 生命周期不同阶段的潜在作用,以及可用于评估它们在驱动癌症、生命周期和改变诱变潜力方面的潜在重要性的新工具。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了细胞聚合酶(包括复制和跨病灶合成 (TLS) 聚合酶)在高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 生命周期中的作用,特别是 HPV16、HPV18、HPV31、HPV33、HPV45、HPV52 和 HPV58,这些病毒与宫颈癌、肛门生殖器癌和头颈癌存在因果关系。核心问题集中于确定 HPV 生命周期不同阶段部署的全部聚合酶及其对病毒复制、基因组不稳定性和肿瘤发生的贡献。
证据基础与方法
该综述是一篇叙述性小型综述,综合了细胞聚合酶参与 HPV 基因组 复制和肿瘤发生的现有证据。它不报告系统检索、资格标准、纳入研究计数或荟萃分析方法。该综述强调了在了解 HPV 在其生命周期中使用的完整聚合酶环境方面的差距。
共识与结论
人们一致认为,高危型HPV 利用 宿主 DNA 损伤耐受途径(包括特定的细胞聚合酶)来支持高效复制,从而导致基因组不稳定和肿瘤发生。然而,参与 HPV 基因组 复制的完整聚合酶仍不清楚,并且复制聚合酶与 TLS 聚合酶在不同生命周期阶段的具体作用尚未完全阐明。
争议与缺口
该审查发现了有关 HPV 在其生命周期中使用的整套聚合酶的知识中的一个关键差距,特别是复制聚合酶和 TLS 聚合酶的相对贡献。缺乏明确的证据表明聚合酶在每个阶段都是必需的,以及它们在驱动突变潜力和癌症进展中的作用仍然悬而未决。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要新的实验工具和方法来评估特定聚合酶在 HPV 复制、生命周期进展和致癌潜力中的重要性。优先事项包括确定 抗病毒药物 策略的聚合酶靶标、了解聚合酶介导的诱变过程以及开发工具来剖析聚合酶对 HPV 相关癌症的影响。

The polymerase milieu of human papillomavirus.

Authors: Keira R. Ylvisaker, Gopishankar Thirumoorthy, Emma Wisniewski, Kavi P. M. Mehta
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT High-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs) cause cervical, anogenital, and head and neck cancers. A key oncogenic mechanism involves viral exploitation of host DNA damage tolerance pathways to support productive replication, a process that also drives genomic instability. Despite evidence that specific cellular polymerases participate in HPV genome replication, the full complement of polymerases deployed across the viral life cycle remains poorly defined. This minireview examines the potential roles of both replicative and translesion synthesis (TLS) polymerases at distinct stages of the HPV life cycle, and new tools that can be used to assess their potential importance in driving cancer, the life cycle, and altering mutagenic potential.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the roles of cellular polymerases, including replicative and translesion synthesis (TLS) polymerases, in the life cycle of high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs), particularly HPV16, HPV18, HPV31, HPV33, HPV45, HPV52, and HPV58, which are causally linked to cervical, anogenital, and head and neck cancers. The central question focuses on identifying the full complement of polymerases deployed during distinct stages of the HPV life cycle and their contributions to viral replication, genomic instability, and oncogenesis.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative minireview that synthesizes existing evidence on the involvement of cellular polymerases in HPV genome replication and oncogenesis. It does not report a systematic search, eligibility criteria, included-study count, or meta-analytic methods. The review highlights gaps in understanding the complete polymerase milieu utilized by HPV across its life cycle.
Consensus and conclusions
There is consensus that high-risk HPVs exploit host DNA damage tolerance pathways, including specific cellular polymerases, to support productive replication, which contributes to genomic instability and oncogenesis. However, the full complement of polymerases involved in HPV genome replication remains poorly defined, and the specific roles of replicative versus TLS polymerases at distinct life cycle stages are not fully elucidated.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a critical gap in knowledge regarding the complete set of polymerases deployed by HPV during its life cycle, particularly the relative contributions of replicative and TLS polymerases. The lack of definitive evidence on which polymerases are essential at each stage and their roles in driving mutagenic potential and cancer progression remains unresolved.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for new experimental tools and approaches to assess the importance of specific polymerases in HPV replication, life cycle progression, and oncogenic potential. Priorities include identifying polymerase targets for antiviral strategies, understanding polymerase-mediated mutagenic processes, and developing tools to dissect polymerase contributions to HPV-associated cancers.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

日本母亲对青春期儿子的意向 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种:心理社会因素和意向群体之间的相对影响。

Mothers' intentions for adolescent sons' human papillomavirus vaccination in Japan: Psychosocial factors and relative influences across intention groups.
作者： Mei Ueda, Miwa Imai, Noriko Yamamoto, Mika Kawai, Yukie Kameda
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了日本母亲为青春期儿子接种 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗的意愿的心理社会决定因素,其中男性的 HPV 疫苗接种 并不常规,重点关注区分接受、犹豫和拒绝意图群体的因素。
证据基础与方法
一项基于网络的横断面调查针对日本 11-16 岁男孩的母亲,将参与者分为接受、犹豫不决和拒绝意向组,并使用二元逻辑回归来比较接受组与未决定组、接受组与拒绝组。
共识与结论
规范信念与 疫苗接种 意图的关联性最强,接受组与未决定组的比值比为 12.63 (95% CI 6.03–26.47),接受组与拒绝组的比值比为 26.72 (95% CI 9.69–73.74)。接受组和拒绝组之间的关联通常比接受组和未决定组之间的关联更强。
争议与缺口
犹豫不决的组报告了更大的感知后勤麻烦,而拒绝的组不太可能完成儿童的常规童年 疫苗接种 或长子的流感 疫苗接种,这表明 疫苗 的吸收存在潜在的行为和态度差距。
科研与实践启示
需要量身定制的干预措施,包括增强主观规范和感知预防价值,为犹豫不决的母亲提供后勤支持,以及实施多渠道建立信任沟通策略,以拒绝母亲优先考虑日本男孩的 HPV 疫苗接种。

Mothers' intentions for adolescent sons' human papillomavirus vaccination in Japan: Psychosocial factors and relative influences across intention groups.

Authors: Mei Ueda, Miwa Imai, Noriko Yamamoto, Mika Kawai, Yukie Kameda
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT As human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination for men remains non-routine in Japan, this study aimed to explore psychosocial factors associated with mothers’ intentions to vaccinate their adolescent sons, and compared the strength of these correlates across intention groups. A web-based cross-sectional survey targeted mothers with boys aged 11–16 years in Japan. Participants were classified by intention into accepting (strongly/relatively agree), undecided (unknown), and refusing (strongly/relatively disagree) groups. Binary logistic regression compared the accepting versus undecided groups and accepting versus refusing groups. Among 301 mothers, 22.6% were accepting, 57.5% undecided, and 19.9% refusing. Normative beliefs exhibited the strongest association, with the highest odds ratio (OR), when comparing accepting versus undecided (OR = 12.63, 95% CI 6.03–26.47) and accepting versus refusing groups (OR = 26.72, 95% CI 9.69–73.74). This was followed by perceived importance of prevention, best-friend influence, anticipatory regret, and perceived susceptibility. Associations were generally stronger in comparisons between accepting and refusing groups than between accepting and undecided groups. The undecided group reported greater perceived logistical hassle. The refusing group was less likely to have completed children’s routine childhood vaccinations before entering elementary school or eldest son’s influenza vaccination and was less influenced by municipal notifications or doctors’ recommendations. Tailored interventions are needed, including those enhancing subjective norms, perceived prevention value, and risk communication for undecided and refusing mothers; providing logistical support for undecided mothers; and implementing multi‐channel, trust‐building communication strategies for refusing mothers. Japan should prioritize boys’ HPV vaccination through multifaceted strategies addressing these psychosocial barriers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study examines psychosocial determinants of mothers’ intentions to vaccinate their adolescent sons against human papillomavirus (HPV) in Japan, where HPV vaccination for males is not routine, focusing on factors distinguishing accepting, undecided, and refusing intention groups.
Evidence base and method
A web-based cross-sectional survey targeted mothers with boys aged 11–16 years in Japan, classifying participants into accepting, undecided, and refusing intention groups and using binary logistic regression to compare accepting versus undecided and accepting versus refusing groups.
Consensus and conclusions
Normative beliefs showed the strongest association with vaccination intention, with odds ratios of 12.63 (95% CI 6.03–26.47) for accepting versus undecided and 26.72 (95% CI 9.69–73.74) for accepting versus refusing groups. Associations were generally stronger in comparisons between accepting and refusing groups than between accepting and undecided groups.
Controversies and gaps
The undecided group reported greater perceived logistical hassle, and the refusing group was less likely to have completed children’s routine childhood vaccinations or eldest son’s influenza vaccination, indicating potential behavioral and attitudinal gaps in vaccine uptake.
Research and practice implications
Tailored interventions are needed, including enhancing subjective norms and perceived prevention value, providing logistical support for undecided mothers, and implementing multi-channel trust-building communication strategies for refusing mothers to prioritize boys’ HPV vaccination in Japan.

对促进佐治亚州农村社区 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 多层次干预小额赠款计划的评估。

Evaluation of a Minigrant Program for Multilevel Interventions to Promote Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in Rural Communities in Georgia.
作者： Cam Escoffery, Courtney Petagna, Rithika Chari, Erica Hsu, Rohini Vijay Kadoo, Regine Haardörfer, Yue Guan, Alexandra B. Morshed, Jessica Wells, Sarah Blake
摘要中文翻译简介:农村县的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 发病率低于城市地区。小额赠款评估的目的是评估 HPV 疫苗接种 的吸收情况、与实施成功相关的因素以及实施的障碍。方法:我们在 RE-AIM(覆盖范围、有效性、采用、实施和维护)和 CFIR(实施科学综合框架)框架的指导下进行了并行、混合方法评估。四个卫生部门获得了资金、包含实施策略的工具包以及每月的技术援助电话或为期一年的学习合作。数据来源是计划文件、工作人员访谈和调查、免疫数据库以及护理人员或年轻人调查。主要评估指标包括 1) 患者的覆盖面和干预水平的采用(患者、提供者或实践);2) HPV 疫苗接种 有效性;3) 工作人员、护理人员和年轻人的实施观点;4)实施障碍和促进因素;5) 计划可持续性能力。结果:大多数站点采用 3 个级别中的 2 个级别(患者、提供者或诊所)。系列完成的常见多层次策略包括患者教育、激励、提供者培训和患者提醒。2023 年至 2024 年第一季度,HPV 疫苗接种 总发病率增加了 26.7%,各县部门的启动率增加了 51.8%。总体而言,按照从 1 到 5 的评分标准,1 表示“强烈不同意”,5 表示“强烈同意”,站点报告了计划实施的积极经验,并认为该计划是可行的(平均 4.4 分)、可接受的(平均 4.4 分)和适当的(平均 4.2 分)。工作人员还报告了实施的高度容易性(平均 4.0 分)和对计划交付的承诺(平均 4.8 分)。家长或看护人 (n = 20) 在调查中表示,他们看到了教育材料 (100%)、收到了教育材料 (90%) 并收到了 疫苗 提醒 (75%)。CFIR 相关的促进者优先关注健康问题、领导力参与、技术基础设施、有关 疫苗 和计划的沟通、可用资源以及内部环境的员工培训和外部环境的外部支持。障碍包括有限的资源、沟通和结构特征(例如技术、与电子健康记录相关的问题)以及当地的态度和条件。项目可持续性能力平均得分为 4.2,其中评估、有效性和工作流程集成得分最高。结论:多层次干预策略可以成功提高农村地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 率,并提供实施支持来解决农村地区的癌症 患病率 问题。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查审查了农村社区的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种,重点关注多层次干预措施以提高佐治亚州的接受率。核心问题涉及小额资助的多层次策略在提高农村人口 HPV 疫苗接种 率方面的有效性。
证据基础与方法
The review describes a concurrent, mixed-methods evaluation using RE-AIM and CFIR frameworks. Four health departments received funding, a toolkit, and technical assistance for 1 year. Data sources included program documents, staff interviews and surveys, immunization databases, and caregiver or young adult surveys.
共识与结论
多层次干预策略,包括患者教育、激励措施、提供者培训和患者提醒,提高了农村地区的 HPV 疫苗接种 率。从 2023 年到 2024 年初,HPV 疫苗接种 总发病率上升了 26.7%,起始率上升了 51.8%。站点报告了该计划的高可行性(平均 4.4)、可接受性(平均 4.4)和适当性(平均 4.2)。
争议与缺口
实施障碍包括资源有限、沟通挑战、结构性问题(例如技术和电子健康记录)和当地态度。可持续发展能力中等(平均 4.2),在评估、有效性和工作流程集成方面得分最高,表明存在潜在的长期挑战。
科研与实践启示
未来的努力应优先解决资源限制和结构性障碍,以增强计划的可持续性。研究应侧重于优化多层次策略并评估其对农村人口 疫苗接种 率的长期影响。

Evaluation of a Minigrant Program for Multilevel Interventions to Promote Human Papillomavirus Vaccination in Rural Communities in Georgia.

Authors: Cam Escoffery, Courtney Petagna, Rithika Chari, Erica Hsu, Rohini Vijay Kadoo, Regine Haardörfer, Yue Guan, Alexandra B. Morshed, Jessica Wells, Sarah Blake
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Rates of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine are lower in rural counties than in urban areas. The objective of the minigrants evaluation was to assess HPV vaccination uptake, factors related to implementation success, and barriers to implementation. METHODS: We conducted a concurrent, mixed-methods evaluation guided by the RE-AIM (Reach, Effectiveness, Adoption, Implementation, and Maintenance) and CFIR (Consolidated Framework for Implementation Science) frameworks. Four health departments received funding, a toolkit with implementation strategies, and monthly technical assistance calls or a learning collaborative for 1 year. The data sources were program documents, staff interviews and surveys, immunization databases, and caregiver or young adult surveys. Key evaluation metrics were 1) reach of patients and adoption of intervention levels (patient, provider, or practice); 2) HPV vaccination effectiveness; 3) implementation perspectives from staff, caregivers, and young adults; 4) implementation barriers and facilitators; and 5) program sustainability capacity. RESULTS: Most sites adopted 2 of 3 levels (patient, provider, or practice). Common multilevel strategies for series completion were patient education, incentives, provider training, and patient reminders. The total HPV vaccination rate increased 26.7% from 2023 to first quarter 2024, and initiation rate increase was 51.8% across the county departments. Overall, on a scale from 1 to 5, with 1 being "strongly disagree" to 5 being "strongly agree," sites reported positive experiences with program implementation and perceived the program was feasible (mean, 4.4 points), acceptable (mean, 4.4 points), and appropriate (mean, 4.2 points). Staff members also reported high ease of implementation (mean, 4.0 points) and commitment to program delivery (mean, 4.8 points). The parents or caregivers (n = 20) reported in surveys that they saw educational materials (100%), received educational materials (90%), and received a vaccine reminder (75%). CFIR-related facilitators were priority of the health issues, leadership involvement, technology infrastructure, communications about the vaccine and program, available resources, and staff training at the inner setting and external support at the outer setting. Barriers included limited resources, communication and structural characteristics (eg, technology, issues related to electronic health records), and local attitudes and conditions. The mean program sustainability capacity score was 4.2, with highest scores for evaluation, effectiveness, and workflow integration. CONCLUSION: Multilevel intervention strategies can successfully increase HPV vaccination rates in rural settings with implementation supports to address cancer prevalence in rural areas.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in rural communities, focusing on multilevel interventions to increase uptake in Georgia. The central question addresses the effectiveness of minigrant-funded, multilevel strategies in improving HPV vaccination rates among rural populations.
Evidence base and method
The review describes a concurrent, mixed-methods evaluation using RE-AIM and CFIR frameworks. Four health departments received funding, a toolkit, and technical assistance for 1 year. Data sources included program documents, staff interviews and surveys, immunization databases, and caregiver or young adult surveys.
Consensus and conclusions
Multilevel intervention strategies, including patient education, incentives, provider training, and patient reminders, increased HPV vaccination rates in rural settings. Total HPV vaccination rates rose by 26.7% from 2023 to early 2024, with initiation rates increasing by 51.8%. Sites reported high feasibility (mean 4.4), acceptability (mean 4.4), and appropriateness (mean 4.2) of the program.
Controversies and gaps
Barriers to implementation included limited resources, communication challenges, structural issues (e.g., technology and electronic health records), and local attitudes. Sustainability capacity was moderate (mean 4.2), with highest scores for evaluation, effectiveness, and workflow integration, indicating potential long-term challenges.
Research and practice implications
Future efforts should prioritize addressing resource limitations and structural barriers to enhance program sustainability. Research should focus on optimizing multilevel strategies and evaluating their long-term impact on vaccination rates in rural populations.

人乳头瘤病毒 感染与动脉粥样硬化性心血管疾病:流行病学证据、分子机制和 疫苗接种 介导的保护的综合综述

Human papillomavirus infection and atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease: a comprehensive review of epidemiological evidence, molecular mechanisms, and vaccination-mediated protection
作者： Yiming Meng, Jing Sun, Cuicui Kong, Yushu Ma, Tao Yu
摘要中文翻译新的流行病学证据表明,高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型的持续感染与动脉粥样硬化性心血管疾病 (CVD) 风险升高独立相关,这对传统的心血管风险评估范式提出了挑战。这篇综述综合了目前关于这种关联的观察数据,探讨了可能将 HPV 与动脉粥样硬化联系起来的潜在炎症、免疫和代谢机制,并评估了 HPV 疫苗接种 赋予心血管保护的新证据。大型 队列研究 研究报告,感染 高危型HPV 的女性患心肌梗塞、中风和心血管疾病 死亡率 的风险显着升高,与传统危险因素无关。从机制上讲,HPV癌蛋白E6和E7激活核因子κB(NF-κB)介导的炎症级联反应,通过p53降解诱导内皮功能障碍,并建立以白细胞介素6(IL-6)和肿瘤坏死因子-α(TNF-α)升高为特征的促动脉粥样硬化全身炎症环境。初步回顾性分析进一步表明,HPV 发病率 与新发心脑血管事件 发病率 的减少有关,尽管健康用户偏差和残留混杂因素限制了因果解释。尽管有这些有趣的信号,但大部分证据来自横截面和回顾性设计,并且仍然缺乏因果联系的直接证明。我们讨论了关键的方法论挑战,包括性行为、社会经济地位和合并感染造成的混淆,并概述了前瞻性 队列研究 研究、机制调查和严格的风险分层研究的路线图。澄清 HPV-CVD 关系的性质可以为通过 疫苗接种 项目一级预防动脉粥样硬化疾病开辟新途径。

Human papillomavirus infection and atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease: a comprehensive review of epidemiological evidence, molecular mechanisms, and vaccination-mediated protection

Authors: Yiming Meng, Jing Sun, Cuicui Kong, Yushu Ma, Tao Yu
Original abstract (English)
Emerging epidemiological evidence indicates that persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes is independently associated with elevated atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease (CVD) risk, challenging traditional paradigms of cardiovascular risk assessment. This review synthesises current observational data on this association, explores the potential inflammatory, immune, and metabolic mechanisms that may connect HPV to atherogenesis, and evaluates the emerging evidence for cardiovascular protection conferred by HPV vaccination. Large cohort studies report significantly elevated risks of myocardial infarction, stroke, and cardiovascular mortality among women with high-risk HPV infection, independent of conventional risk factors. Mechanistically, HPV oncoproteins E6 and E7 activate nuclear factor-κB (NF-κB)-mediated inflammatory cascades, induce endothelial dysfunction through p53 degradation, and establish a pro-atherogenic systemic inflammatory milieu characterized by elevated interleukin-6 (IL-6) and tumor necrosis factor-α (TNF-α). Preliminary retrospective analyses further indicate that HPV vaccination is linked to a reduced incidence of new-onset cardiovascular and cerebrovascular events, although healthy-user bias and residual confounding limit causal interpretation. Despite these intriguing signals, the bulk of evidence is derived from cross-sectional and retrospective designs, and the direct demonstration of a causal link remains absent. We discuss critical methodological challenges—including confounding by sexual behaviour, socioeconomic status, and co-infections—and outline a roadmap for prospective cohort studies, mechanistic investigations, and rigorous risk-stratification research. Clarifying the nature of the HPV–CVD relationship could open novel avenues for the primary prevention of atherosclerotic disease through vaccination programmes.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Emerging epidemiological evidence indicates that persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) genotypes is independently associated with elevated atherosclerotic cardiovascular disease (CVD) risk, challenging traditional paradigms of cardiovascular risk assessment.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps
Despite these intriguing signals, the bulk of evidence is derived from cross-sectional and retrospective designs, and the direct demonstration of a causal link remains absent.
Research and practice implications
We discuss critical methodological challenges—including confounding by sexual behaviour, socioeconomic status, and co-infections—and outline a roadmap for prospective cohort studies, mechanistic investigations, and rigorous risk-stratification research.
